### [1] [Confidence-Based Adaptive Retrieval Fails Where It Matters Most: A Paradox in Biomedical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33210)
*Chengying She,Siyu Deng,Haiyan Ge,Lizhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究评估了基于自适应检索增强生成的策略，针对不同难度的生物医学问答任务，发现模型的信心校准在常规医学考试题目上表现良好，但在专家级问题上却随机失准，无法通过后验校准策略恢复有效的检索路由信号。这质疑了基于信心自适应检索增强生成的假设，特别是在高风险生物医学环境中，对于目标领域信心校准未知时，始终检索比信心引导策略更安全准确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究发现临床环境中使用的大规模语言模型生成的可能不正确答案的安全风险，而自适应检索增强生成可以通过在模型不确定时才进行检索来减少这一风险。

Method: 研究者在三个不同难度的生物医学问答基准测试集上评估了一种基于信心的自适应检索增强生成策略，涵盖了广泛的模型参数规模，并使用五种不确定性信号和五种后校准策略分析。

Result: 研究结果表明，信心在常规医学考试题目中准确度高，但在专家级问题中却成为随机失准，而五种不确定性信号和五种后处理校准策略无法恢复有效的检索路由信号。

Conclusion: 研究挑战了基于信心的自适应检索增强生成假设，在高风险的生物医学情境中，当目标领域信心校准未知时，始终进行检索策略比信心引导策略更安全且准确。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for biomedical question answering, but plausible incorrect answers remain a safety risk in clinical settings. Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) can ground responses in medical literature, while confidence-based adaptive RAG retrieves only when the model appears uncertain. We evaluate this routing strategy on three biomedical QA benchmarks of increasing difficulty and compare models across a broad range of parameter scales. The results reveal a paradox: confidence is well calibrated (AUROC 0.78--0.89) on standard medical exam questions where retrieval adds little value (+2.4\% to -1.4\%), yet becomes \emph{near-random} (AUROC 0.506) on specialist-level questions where retrieval helps most (+11.2\%). Five uncertainty signals---first-token probability, margin, entropy, consistency-based sampling, and verbalized confidence---show the same failure pattern. Five post-hoc calibration correction strategies, including density-ratio reweighting and rank-preserving transformation, do not recover a useful routing signal on hard questions (best AUROC: 0.507). Cross-model comparison shows that larger parameter scale and higher base accuracy do not guarantee reliable confidence-based routing under specialist-domain difficulty shift. These findings challenge the premise of confidence-based adaptive RAG: in high-stakes biomedical settings, defaulting to always-retrieve is safer and more accurate than confidence-based routing when calibration on the target domain is unknown.

</details>


### [2] [A comprehensive survey of deep face verification systems adversarial attacks and defense strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33231)
*Sohair Kilany,Ahmed Mahfouz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究综述了近年来深层面部验证领域的最新进展，涵盖算法设计、数据库利用、协议及应用场景，并深入探讨了生成对抗样例的最新算法及防御机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着面部验证系统的广泛使用和其深度学习技术的应用，对抗攻击成为了一个重要问题。研究这一问题旨在提高系统安全性，特别是在关键安全领域。

Method: 综述文章基于文献回顾的方法，总结了现有研究并将其分类，详细分析了对抗样本的生成算法和防御措施。

Result: 研究结果显示，对抗攻击在面部验证系统中非常有效，且防御对抗攻击的策略也在不断进步，但仍然存在挑战。

Conclusion: 文章指出了现有防御措施的不足之处，并对未来研究方向进行了展望。

Abstract: Face Verification (FV) systems have exhibited remarkable performance in verification tasks and have consequently garnered extensive adoption across various applications, from identity duplication to authentication in mobile payments. However, the surge in popularity of face verification has raised concerns about potential vulnerabilities in the face of adversarial attacks. These concerns originate from the fact that advanced FV systems, which rely on deep neural networks, have recently demonstrated susceptibility to crafted input samples known as adversarial examples. Although imperceptible to human observers, adversarial examples can deceive deep neural networks during the testing and deployment phases. These vulnerabilities raised significant concerns about the deployment of deep neural networks in safety-critical contexts, prompting extensive investigations into adversarial attacks and corresponding defense strategies. This comprehensive survey provides a comprehensive overview of recent advances in deep face verification, encompassing a broad spectrum of topics such as algorithmic designs, database utilization, protocols, and application scenarios. Furthermore, we conduct an in-depth examination of state-of-the-art algorithms to generate adversarial examples and the defense mechanisms devised to mitigate such adversarial threats.

</details>


### [3] [How Many Samples Is RL Worth? Best-of-N versus RL Post-Training for Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33232)
*Salil Bhatnagar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了在使用自动生成奖励的强化学习（RL）辅助训练文本生成图像模型时，基础模型样本值与RL样本值的转换率（称为交换率）。通过在多个提示上进行实验，发现对于位置指定不明确的提示，基础模型表现与RL相当；而对于位置指定明确的提示，RL表现显著优于基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在评估在使用自动检查奖励的强化学习辅助训练文本生成图像模型时，基础模型样本值与RL样本值的相对价值，这有助于更高效的模型训练。

Method: 本文通过将Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium及其公开的Flow-GRPO检查点生成的图像与人工评选和自动评选的图像进行比较，研究基础模型样本与RL样本之间的转换率。

Result: 当提示涉及位置指定时，RL表现明显优于基础模型；而对于不涉及位置指定的提示，基础模型与RL样本表现相近。具体而言，在不涉及位置指定的提示下，多个基础模型样本的表现几乎等同于一个RL样本。但对于需要精确位置信息的提示，使用RL的方法更能准确地生成所需图像。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，当提示对位置有极高要求时，强化学习方法可以显著提高生成图像的质量，但对于一般性的图像生成任务，基础模型的表现已经足够接近强化学习的效果。此外，研究还发现自动评估与人类评估在图像质量衡量上的差异，进一步说明了人工评估的重要性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) on a reward that can be checked automatically has become a common last step in the training of text-to-image models. A simpler way to get more correct images is best-of-N: draw N images from the base model and keep the one that a picker prefers. We ask how many base model samples one RL sample is worth, and call this number the exchange rate. We measure it for Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium and its public Flow-GRPO checkpoint on 398 new prompts in the style of GenEval, the benchmark behind the RL reward, with human labels on every prompt and two automatic judges other than the reward. The answer depends on the prompt. Four fifths of the prompts do not ask for a position (for example, one object left of another). On these, the base model catches up: best-of-16 with an off-the-shelf vision-language model (VLM) as the picker is about as good as one RL image. A person who looks through up to 3 base images finds a correct one as often as one RL image is correct. On the prompts that ask for a position, RL is far ahead: a person finds a correct image among 8 base images for only 52% of the prompts, against 92% for one RL image. Over all prompts, our primary test, which we fixed in advance, finds best-of-16 with the VLM picker 9.3 points behind one RL image when both automatic judges must pass an image. People see a smaller gap, 5.5 and 6.5 points in two checks. FLUX.1-dev shows a similar gap in the same test. RL also solves 20 prompts that the base model misses in 128 tries, and 1,024 tries recover half of them. The automatic judges are wrong in opposite directions: a detector-based judge like the reward, as used in our primary test, reports a larger gap than people see, and VLM judges report little or none. Comparisons of RL with best-of-N should therefore check the selected images by hand.

</details>


### [4] [MonoQPM: Splitting Features into Concepts for Inherent Interpretability and Predictive Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33252)
*Thomas Norrenbrock,Robert Zimmermann,Timo Kaiser,Sovan Biswas,Neslihan Kose,Bodo Rosenhahn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究引入了一种名为Monosemantic QPM（MonoQPM）的模型，通过重新分解释义特征，有效缓解了深度神经网络中的多义性问题。MonoQPM利用Conformal Prediction（CP）技术，提高了预测集的紧凑性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决深度神经网络中的可解释性问题，特别是如何同时避免特征的叠加与保持多义性。

Method: 提出了一种新型模型Monosemantic QPM (MonoQPM)，通过优化特征表示来分离多义性特征为单一概念，并结合Conformal Prediction (CP)技术来改进预测效果。

Result: 研究验证了MonoQPM在多种架构上解决了QPM中的多义性问题，提高了预测集的实用性和紧凑性。例如，在ImageNet数据集上，使用MonoQPM和自适应预测集时，保证了88%的覆盖范围，只需QPM冻结集大小的66%。

Conclusion: Monosemantic QPM有效地分离了多义性特征，为提高深度神经网络的可解释性提供了一种有效的策略，并通过实际应用展示了其优越性。

Abstract: Alongside superposition, polysemanticity is one of the primary obstacles to achieving interpretable deep neural networks. While these phenomena are typically intrinsically linked, the Quadratic Programming Enhanced Model (QPM) naturally decouples them. By representing classes with a sparse binary assignment of very few features, QPM prevents superposition by design on its final features but still exhibits polysemanticity. However, measuring polysemanticity is an open problem. This work proposes a utility-focused approach to measuring polysemanticity by quantifying the decrease in interference-induced activation errors, which yields the practical utility of tighter prediction sets using Conformal Prediction (CP). We explicitly disentangle the polysemantic features of QPM into monosemantic concepts to create the Monosemantic QPM (MonoQPM). Because its features are disentangled, MonoQPM acts as a significantly more efficient Conformal Predictor. Additionally, we introduce CUBCars, a dataset providing ground truth information about shared concepts. Using this and other datasets, we demonstrate that polysemanticity emerges in QPM across all tested architectures, but is effectively alleviated by MonoQPM. For instance, MonoQPM guarantees 88% coverage using Adaptive Prediction Sets on ImageNet with just 66% of the frozen QPM's set size.

</details>


### [5] [CriticalScenes: Scalable Photorealistic Critical-Scenario Generation for Closed-Loop Driving Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33255)
*Alexandros Theocharous,Holger Caesar,Ziqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于重建的框架CriticalScenes，将普通驾驶场景转化为逼真的安全关键交互，同时提供了避碰监督。实验结果显示，生成的监督提高了关键场景规划能力，但在不同规划架构间存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过引入CriticalScenes框架，解决普通驾驶数据中缺乏安全关键互动的问题，从而改善端到端驾驶规划中的避碰性能。

Method: 该方法通过重建普通驾驶场景，生成逼真的安全关键交互，提供避碰监督，构建了一个数据增强的方法。

Result: 实验表明，SparseDrive相对降低了46.5%的碰撞率，而UniAD仅在闭环中表现出适度的改进。然而，开放环中的显著改进并不必然转化为相同的安全性增益。

Conclusion: 研究强调了闭环评估的重要性，并指出了不同架构在闭环中表现出的差异，研究结果有助于更好地理解和改进端到端的驾驶规划。

Abstract: Safety-critical interactions are rare in recorded driving data, leaving end-to-end driving planners with limited exposure to evasive behaviour during training. This lack of critical supervision can lead to unsafe behaviour and, in rare instances, collisions. To address this gap, we introduce CriticalScenes, a reconstruction-based framework that transforms ordinary recorded driving scenes into photorealistic safety-critical interactions with corresponding collision-avoidance supervision. Across 53 reconstructed source scenes, 979 of 1,060 attempted interactions yield feasible threat trajectories (92.4\%), while the inserted threat actors remain detectable in more than 95\% of non-occluded observations within 5--35\,m. Experiments with two structurally different end-to-end planners show that the generated supervision improves critical-scenario planning, while closed-loop transfer varies across planner architectures. SparseDrive achieves a 46.5\% relative reduction in collision rate on an independent photorealistic closed-loop benchmark, whereas UniAD shows only a modest closed-loop improvement despite substantial open-loop gains. The results further highlight the importance of closed-loop evaluation, as improvements in open-loop trajectory prediction do not necessarily translate into equivalent safety gains.

</details>


### [6] [Same Tokenizer, Better Tokens: Choosing Image Tokens by MAP Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33261)
*Piotr Kubaty,Jacek Tabor,Łukasz Struski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了RecaP方法，通过平衡重建质量和序列的预测性来选择编码器代码，从而提高图像生成的质量和预测性，特别是在ImageNet数据集上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的向量量化分词器独立地将每个编码器特征分配给最近的编码，这种方法会导致序列无法提供比单独优化的更能有效重建图像的方案。为了提高图像生成质量和预测性，作者希望改进这一机制，引入RecaP方法。

Method: 作者提出了RecaP方法，这是一种新的编码选择策略，结合了两个关键目标：尽可能准确地重建图像和以预测自回归先验下的概率最大化其序列。具体地，他们将重构问题转化为一个局部目标优化问题，通过最小化重构损失来选择分词，并根据预先训练的自回归模型来调整分词选择。

Result: 实验证明，使用RecaP方法生成的图像在ImageNet数据集上的PSNR（峰值信噪比）表现优于使用传统最近邻方法生成的图像。LlamaGen GPT-B模型在使用RecaP生成的token进行训练时，相较于使用最近邻编码的token训练的模型，在特定数据集上的FID（Frechet inception distance）值较低，表明生成的图像质量有所提高。

Conclusion: 论文提出了一种新的图像生成方法RecaP，通过考虑图像重建质量和序列预测性来优化分词过程，能够在部分场景下游显现出生成高质量图像的能力。然而，实际应用中仍需要进一步验证其在更大规模数据集上和不同模型上的表现。

Abstract: Discrete visual tokens encode images for reconstruction and serve as training targets for autoregressive image generators. Vector-quantized tokenizers typically assign each encoder feature its nearest code independently. The decoder uses these codes jointly, so a sequence of individually nearest codes can reconstruct an image less faithfully than an alternative assignment. A local model of the reconstruction loss yields a closest-vector problem with a decoder-dependent target and metric. Reconstruction-only search improves PSNR across the frozen tokenizers we test. Different code sequences can achieve similar reconstruction quality, leaving room to favour more predictable ones.
  We introduce RecaP (Reconstruction and Predictability), which selects codes for a frozen tokenizer by balancing how faithfully they reconstruct the image with how likely their sequence is under a pretrained autoregressive prior. On ImageNet, LlamaGen GPT-B trained on RecaP tokens achieves lower early-checkpoint FID against training images than GPT-B trained on nearest-neighbour tokens. The difference is smaller against held-out images. On an ImageNet subset, the prior improves generation at comparable reconstruction FID. Our code is available at: https://github.com/gmum/recap

</details>


### [7] [JetDiffusion: Pushing the Speed Limits of Video Generation with NVFP4 Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33268)
*Yulun Wu,Zihan Min,Minshen Zhang,Kaiqin Kong,Ji Xie,Will Lin,Junda Chen,Wenxuan Tan,Aryan Kumar,Loay Rashid,Qingyu Yin,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了JetDiffusion，一种基于可训练的视频稀疏量化注意（VSQA）的视频生成系统，通过稀疏性和低比特量化减轻了长序列关注的计算瓶颈。该系统在不同硬件上将注意力加速了8-29倍，并通过结合VSQA与NVFP4线性量化和步骤蒸馏实现了进一步加速。通过实证研究，该研究开发了一种有效的分阶段训练方法，使模型在RTX 5090上实现了273倍的端到端视频生成加速，同时保持了可比的视频质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着视频生成需求的增加，长序列关注的计算瓶颈成为一个重大问题。为了提高生成效率，本文提出了JetDiffusion系统，通过引入稀疏量化注意力机制及与NVFP4线性量化和步骤蒸馏的结合，有效解决了这一问题。

Method: JetDiffusion系统结合了视频稀疏量化注意（VSQA）和NVFP4线性量化及步骤蒸馏。引入了定制的推理内核，利用原生NVFP4计算和内核融合技术。通过分阶段训练方法，实现模型对稀疏性、量化和少量步骤去噪的同时适应。

Result: 在Wan2.1-14B数据集上，使用JetDiffusion系统在RTX 5090显卡上实现了273倍的端到端视频生成加速，同时保持了视频质量的可比性。

Conclusion: JetDiffusion系统是一种高效的视频生成解决方案，通过稀疏量化注意力和低比特量化的结合，成功地缓解了长序列关注计算瓶颈，提高了生成效率，并且保持了视频质量。

Abstract: Attention over long sequences is a major computational bottleneck in video generation, which can be alleviated through sparsity and low-bit quantization. We propose JetDiffusion, a video generation system built on trainable Video Sparse Quantized Attention (VSQA), with custom inference kernels that combine native NVFP4 computation and kernel fusion. At 90% sparsity, VSQA accelerates attention by 8-29x compared with FlashAttention on different hardwares. To further accelerate video generation, we combine VSQA with NVFP4 linear quantization and step distillation. Although each technique can individually preserve generation quality, jointly adapting to all three remains challenging, particularly under aggressive NVFP4 quantization. Through empirical studies, we develop an effective staged training recipe that enables stable adaptation to quantization, sparsity, and few-step denoising. On Wan2.1-14B, the resulting model achieves 273x end-to-end video generation speedup on the RTX 5090 while maintaining comparable video quality.

</details>


### [8] [CoSMo: A Compact Spectral Space of Body Motion in Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33296)
*Maham Tanveer,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoSMo 是一个紧凑的体态运动频谱空间，在不需要训练的情况下，能够对比、编辑、转换、插值、组合、强度和细节缩放以及重新抽样。此编码器可通过 DINOv2 的特征在固定的时间基上利用离散余弦变换（DCT）压缩时间变化信息。实验结果表明，CoSMo 在运动传输等方面的性能优于其他方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前存在的一些基于帧的运动表示方法（如DisMo）在性能上存在局限性，在运动编辑、传输等方面不够灵活和精确。研究者希望通过提出一种新的编码方法（CoSMo），能在不需要额外训练的情况下，有效地进行运动的空间编辑和不同主体间的运动转移。

Method: CoSMo基于DINOv2的特征提取，在离散余弦变换（DCT）的时间基上进行特征变化压缩，最终将变化信息编码成384个数字。编码过程中，每个频率段的模式相同，使得不同时间点的代码能够对齐，并逐步从粗到细，便于进行混合或叠加。

Result: 实验结果显示，CoSMo在无额外运动训练的情况下，能够更准确地转移运动，特别是在自动评估指标和用户偏好测试中都优于对比方法（如DisMo）。对于不同主体间的人形运动，CoSMo的融合效果也显著优于DisMo。

Conclusion: 研究证明了CoSMo在保持主体间运动传输和编辑准确度上的优势，具有潜在的应用价值。

Abstract: We introduce CoSMo, a compact spectral space of body motion in video, in which each motion is a single code that can be compared and edited without training, transferred to a new subject, interpolated, composed, scaled in intensity and detail, or sampled anew. Our encoder expresses how a clip's DINOv2 features change over time in a fixed temporal basis, the discrete cosine transform (DCT), and compresses this change into 384 numbers. Because each frequency band is the same cosine pattern in every code, codes are aligned in time, ordered from coarse to fine, and can be blended or added without matching frames. What stays constant over a clip cancels, and keeping fewer frequencies leaves less of the performer and scene. The encoder is trained on real videos and on synthetic motions of several characters, and lightweight adapters let a frozen image-to-video model render any code with any subject. On unseen videos, our code ranks the same motion on other bodies higher than DisMo, a per-frame representation, while its nearest neighbors show the same performer about 40% less often with a seven times more compact code. In generation, without per-motion training, CoSMo transfers motion more faithfully than FlexiAct and a text-prompt baseline, at least as faithfully as DisMo on automatic metrics and clearly better between humanoid bodies, and its blends keep much more of both source motions than DisMo's. Users rank CoSMo first nearly twice as often as DisMo for motion transfer (59% vs. 30%), place it top-two 94% of the time, and prefer it in 76% of the interpolation comparisons.

</details>


### [9] [Large-scale Model Merging via Recovery-aware Task Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33315)
*Kaiyang Li,Shaobo Han,Boming Chen,Martin Renqiang Min,Shihao Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种名为RaTW的模型合并框架，旨在更均匀地保留每个任务的专家能力，特别是在大规模设置中。通过评估不同合并方法的效果，该研究还引入了ReForge++，一种结合RaTW和迭代模型优化的方法，进一步提高了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前模型合并方法在大规模设置中往往会存在能力保留不均的问题，导致某些任务表现不佳。为了应对这一挑战，研究者开发了RaTW框架来提高模型合并的均衡性。

Method: RaTW框架通过计算每个任务中专家正确预测但初始合并模型错误预测的比例，以此来衡量恢复机会，并据此调整任务权重，再进行模型合并。

Result: 在CLIPMerge-100基准测试中，与11种合并方法相比，RaTW在33个ViT-L/14设置和32个ViT-B/32设置中均提高了平均准确率和尾部20%的专家范化准确率。引入ReForge++后，其在所有设置中的平均准确率和尾部20%都超越了所有评估的基线，包括预算匹配的控制组。

Conclusion: 研究证明了RaTW在确保模型合并均衡性方面有效，并通过ReForge++展示了一种新的方法可以进一步优化模型合并结果。

Abstract: Model merging combines multiple task-specific expert models into a single model. However, merging preserves expert capabilities unevenly across tasks. This imbalance is particularly pronounced in large-scale settings, where some tasks can suffer severe capability losses even when average accuracy remains high. To address this imbalance, we introduce Recovery-aware Task Weighting (RaTW), a model merging framework that assigns larger weights to tasks whose expert capabilities are poorly retained by an initial merged model. We quantify these recovery opportunities as the fraction of a task's labeled validation samples that its expert predicts correctly but the initial merged model gets wrong, and use the resulting task weights to re-merge the experts. To evaluate merging at large scale, we construct CLIPMerge-100, a 100-task benchmark with task-specific experts on CLIP ViT-L/14 and ViT-B/32. Across eleven merging methods with 20, 50, and 100 tasks on two CLIP backbones, RaTW improves both mean accuracy and tail20 (the mean expert-normalized accuracy of the 20% worst-retained tasks) in all 33 ViT-L/14 settings and 32 of 33 ViT-B/32 settings. We further introduce ReForge++, which combines RaTW with iterative model refinement and outperforms all evaluated baselines, including a matched-budget control, in both mean accuracy and tail20 across all settings.

</details>


### [10] [What Does RL Post-Training Teach Text-to-Image Models? A Skill-Wise Analysis of Flow-GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33316)
*Cheng Zhang,Jiesong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过在不使用训练中使用的提示的情况下，比较强化学习（RL）后训练前后共享噪声种子的表现，该研究评估了RL对模型生成能力提升的具体贡献。研究发现，对于某些技能，RL提升了单个解码样本的效果，而在其他技能中，则是解锁了解决新问题的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人员探讨了强化学习后训练（RL post-training）在提升文本转图像模型（如Flow-GRPO）生成能力时的具体贡献，特别是在理解不同技能的提升机制方面。

Method: 实验通过比较Reinforcement Learning（RL）前后的文本转图像模型在不同技能下的表现。具体方法包括：（1）在未使用训练中用到的提示的情况下，比较共享噪声种子（shared noise seeds）在技能上的表现；（2）分析不同技能下提升的具体机制。

Result: 研究结果表明，对于四项技能而言，大部分提高来自于单个生成样本的质量提升（sharpness）；而对于空间关系技能（spatial relations），则是在解锁了解决新问题的能力（unlocking）。总体而言，与基线模型相比，后训练模型提升了31个基线样本等同的效果。

Conclusion: 研究认为，RL提升的效果并不均匀，对于不同的技能有不同的提升机制，部分提升来自对同一提示内部的更新，而另一部分提升则得益于提示之间的参数共享。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training has become a standard way to improve text-to-image flow models: Flow-GRPO lifts the GenEval score of SD3.5-M from 0.63 to 0.95, a gain read as the model having learned compositional generation. We ask how much of that gain the base model's own samples already contained, by measuring pass@k over shared noise seeds, one skill at a time, before and after RL, on prompts never used in training. The answer differs by skill. On four of GenEval's six skills, where 32 base samples already solve almost every prompt, the gain is sharpening: one post-trained sample is worth 4 to 10 base samples, and pass@32 barely moves. On spatial relations it is unlocking: pass@32 rises from 0.69 to 1.00, 128 base samples do not match one post-trained sample, and prompts the base model never solved in 32 samples are solved 93% of the time, as often as those it solved. The benchmark average, 31 base samples per post-trained sample, describes no skill. Relations carry 35% of the gain; of the other 65%, selecting among 8 base samples with the judge behind the reward recovers nine tenths. We propose an account of when each occurs: within a prompt, a group-relative update re-weights only the outcomes its samples contain, and a flow policy cannot widen the noise that produces them; across prompts, shared parameters let successes on some prompts repair a deficit common to many. The same anatomy applies to a text-rendering checkpoint. The account's predictions are tested with a checkpoint trained without KL, within-prompt diversity, and controlled training that holds object classes out, where sharpening comes first and transfers, and unlocking follows, on practised objects before unseen ones.

</details>


### [11] [StyleID++: Position-Decoupled and Structure-Coherent Style Retrieval across Diffusion Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33323)
*Chun Xie,Itaru Kitahara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过注意力注入实现无训练风格迁移，但在不同图像间存在位置信号泄漏的问题，导致内容和风格的错误匹配。StyleID++框架通过位置解耦检索和关联一致性细化两个模块，解决了这些问题，显著提高了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无训练风格迁移中的位置信号泄漏问题，提高风格迁移的效果和质量。

Method: 提出了两种技术模块——位置解耦检索和关联一致性细化，通过对注意力机制进行修改，减少了不同图像间的不必要的位置依赖性。

Result: StyleID++框架能够有效减少跨图像的位置泄漏，提高内容与风格的匹配度，并在多个评估指标上获得了最优或接近最优的结果。

Conclusion: 位置信号泄漏是无训练风格迁移中的一个重要问题，通过针对性地改进不同网络模型中的位置机制，可以显著提升风格迁移任务的效果。

Abstract: Attention injection offers a remarkably simple route to training-free style transfer: content queries retrieve style keys and values inside a pretrained diffusion model. We show that this operation also creates an overlooked failure mode. Position signals that are useful within one image become a spurious shared coordinate system across two spatially unrelated images, causing content tokens to favor style tokens at similar coordinates. This cross-image positional leakage appears in both convolutional U-Nets and diffusion transformers (DiTs), despite their different position mechanisms. Removing the positional shortcut exposes a second problem: unconstrained global retrieval lets related content tokens select incompatible style regions, producing fragmented appearance. We present StyleID++, a training-free framework that addresses the two problems jointly. Position-Decoupled Retrieval reduces coordinate-dependent matching through circular padding in U-Nets and injection-only RoPE removal in DiTs, while preserving native position modeling elsewhere. Affinity-Coherent Refinement then regularizes style assignments with the content self-attention graph, encouraging structurally related tokens to retrieve compatible style evidence. We quantify the leakage with a coordinate--centroid correlation and a permutation test on the style reference. The permutation error can be driven to zero in both backbones, which identifies the position mechanism as the carrier of the leakage, and StyleID++ obtains the best ArtFID of all methods we compare.

</details>


### [12] [Do Not Let the Prior Heal the Anomaly: Selective Anchoring for Detection from Incomplete Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33331)
*Nicolas Tricard,Stefan Brandstätter,Jason Chen,Zituo Chen,Linzheng Wang,Georg Langs,Sili Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种选择性锚定方法，该方法能够在保留可靠测量的图像组件的同时，让生成模型自由重建未解决的结构，从而提高了病变对比度保留和不确定性可靠性，在肿瘤检测方面取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前生成先验可能在恢复医学测量不完整的解剖细节时会抹除影像试图检测的病灶，即使这些测量包含病灶证据。

Method: 研究提出了一种选择性锚定方法，通过在训练模型时保护可靠的图像组件，同时允许生成模型自由重构未解的结构。

Result: 该方法在稀疏视角CT、加速脑MRI和光声成像中能提高病变对比度保留，并且能降低病灶内的重建误差。特别是对于加速MRI中的肿瘤检测，使用健康参考估计的确定性锚定方法使得AUC从78.4%提高至84.6%，病灶敏感性从38.8%提升到47.5%，适用于不同大小的肿瘤组别。

Conclusion: 该选择性锚定方法使生成模型能够重建未解决的解剖细节，同时保留影像学测量支持的病灶，并且能够在不同成像技术中提高检测病变的能力。

Abstract: Generative priors can recover anatomical detail from incomplete medical measurements, but may erase the very lesions that imaging aims to detect, even when the measurements contain evidence of them. We introduce selective anchoring that protects reliably measured image components from prior-driven changes, while allowing expressive generative models to reconstruct unresolved structure. Acquisition uncertainty in the protected components is either added deterministically to the downstream test or carried by the ensemble through stochastic perturbations of the readings. We implement this principle as a wrapper for pretrained diffusion and flow samplers and as AnchoredFM, a fast constrained flow model. Under a linear-Gaussian measurement model, the protected coefficients are unbiased in expectation and achieve nominal interval coverage across repeated acquisitions, independently of the learned prior. Across sparse-view CT, accelerated brain MRI, and photoacoustic tomography, selective anchoring improves lesion contrast preservation and uncertainty reliability, and can reduce reconstruction error within lesions. For tumor detection in accelerated MRI, with a healthy reference estimated from the measurements alone, deterministic anchoring raises mean AUROC across eight samplers from 78.4% to 84.6% and lesion sensitivity at 0.25 false-positive marks per slice from 38.8% to 47.5%, with gains in all four predefined tumor-size groups. At thresholds frozen on healthy validation data, mean sensitivity rises from 20.2% to 44.1% (37.6% with stochastic anchoring). These findings show how expressive generative priors can reconstruct unresolved anatomy without healing away the abnormalities that the measurements support.

</details>


### [13] [Temporal Weight Surgery: Repairing Video Grounding with LLM Editors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33339)
*Ali Asgarov,Chris Thomas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过Temporal Weight Surgery（TWS）方法，可以找到模型错误的具体位置并进行修正，提高视频中时间标识的准确性，且这些修正不会影响模型的运行速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的视频语言模型在找到正确时刻方面表现不错，但在时间标注上常出现错误。为了改善这一问题，作者提出了一种名为Temporal Weight Surgery（TWS）的技术。

Method: TWS方法主要通过普通LLM阅读模型的权重和激活值，定位错误来源并进行修正。在不使用梯度的情况下，进行多次独立会话来定位和修复错误。

Result: 在多种模型（GPT-6 Astra和Claude Opus 5.5）的实验中，定位并修正了模型错误，分别提高了7.9 mIoU和2.9 mIoU，甚至在某些情况下实现了18.3 mIoU的提升。修正仅涉及时间分隔符，而不影响时间分钟的准确性。

Conclusion: TWS方法有效地提高了模型在视频时间标注上的准确度，而不需要重新训练模型，并且对模型性能影响很小。

Abstract: A surgeon first finds what is wrong, then fixes only that part. We use this idea for video grounding, where video-language models often find the right moment in a long video but write its time wrong. In Temporal Weight Surgery (TWS), a general-purpose LLM reads the model's weights and activations, finds where the error arises and repairs it there, without gradients. Separate sessions find the same cause, and this diagnosis of where and why the model fails is our main contribution. Three GPT-6 Astra sessions trace a dropped digit in TimeLens-8B to one MLP layer, and the largest edit gains 7.9 mIoU on 1,215 unseen questions and 18.3 on videos of 30 minutes or more. Two Claude Opus 5.5 sessions trace minutes written as seconds in Qwen3-VL-8B to the separator and gain 2.9 and 1.3. Before any edit, its answers often have the right minute and the wrong separator, and fixing only the separator lifts their mIoU from 13.1 to 19.2. Every improving edit beats a random edit of its size, and the edited models run as fast as before.

</details>


### [14] [LGCount: Layout-Guided Diffusion for Zero-Shot Object Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33344)
*Adriano D'Alessandro,Ali Mahdavi-Amiri,Ghassan Hamarneh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于文本的结构化布局来指定计数目标，通过图像编辑扩散模型将其转换为密度图，从而克服了现有方法需要明确指定计数目标的限制。通过训练合成的反事实数据集，模型能在不重新训练的情况下根据不同类别、区域或粒度对单张图像进行计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于图像的计数方法通常要求用户指定明确的计数目标（如类别名称或标注框），这限制了其灵活性和适应性。为了提高计数的灵活性和对不同上下文的适应能力，研究提出了通过结构化布局（命名目标、区域和忽略干扰元素）来改进计数方法。

Method: 该方法的核心步骤包括：1) 使用prompt upsampler从图像和所需类别生成结构化布局；2) 使用编辑扩散模型将结构化布局转换为密度图；3) 通过训练合成反事实数据集，强制模型关注结构化布局，而不仅仅是原始密度图。

Result: 研究在FSC-147数据集上达到了零样本最佳性能，并在LOOKALIKES数据集上获得了最优报告结果。对于单张图像，使用该方法可以在不重新训练的情况下根据不同类别、区域或粒度进行计数。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种新的计数框架，通过结构化布局和反事实训练，显著提高了图像计数任务的灵活性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Class-agnostic counting requires the user to specify what to count, usually with a class name or a few annotated boxes. We propose a richer text-based specification: a structured layout that names the target, the region that holds it, the distractors to ignore. A prompt upsampler writes this layout from the image and the requested category alone, and an image-editing diffusion model, adapted into a single-step counter, turns it into a density map. Because counting datasets pair each image with a single valid density map, a counter trained on it learns to ignore the layout. We therefore train on synthetic counterfactuals in which one image admits several valid density maps, so that the layout cannot be ignored. On FSC-147 we improve on the previous zero-shot state of the art and we set the best reported result on LOOKALIKES while being told the least about each image. A single photograph can be counted by category, by region, or by granularity without retraining.

</details>


### [15] [DDxBasis: Explaining Frozen Foundation Encoder-based Medical Classification by Exact Differential Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33358)
*Luojie Huang,Gregory N. McKay,Nicholas J. Durr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DDxBasis 是一种新的解释方法，通过三个步骤将分类器的决策解释为诊断差异，实现了准确性和解释性的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的医学图像分类器无法像临床医生那样通过关键发现来解释诊断，导致解释不准确或不完整。

Method: DDxBasis 方法包括概念瓶颈、概念基础重整以及查询轴三个步骤，通过这些步骤实现分类器解释。

Result: DDxBasis 跨五组医学数据集，在冻结编码模型中表现最准确，并接近最佳的领域特定结果。

Conclusion: 这种解释方法能够准确而精确地解释分类器的决策，同时保持高的准确性而且具有可复制性，即使没有有序阶段或概念标签，依然能够揭示数据中的结构和异常。

Abstract: In differential diagnosis (DDx), a clinician explains a diagnosis by the few findings that separate it from the alternatives. Medical image classifiers cannot yet explain themselves this way. Existing explanations are either faithful but spread over hundreds of concepts, or compact but leave part of each decision unexplained. We present DDxBasis, which explains a classifier on a frozen foundation encoder as a differential diagnosis in three steps. First, a concept bottleneck reads all patch tokens at every encoder layer using gated, second-order slot attention. A concept basis then regroups the concepts into two-pole axes, each a differential between groups of diagnoses. Finally, we read a differential diagnosis from each axis through the typical presentation of its poles, the pertinent findings of the case, and counterfactual edits of borderline cases. Across five medical datasets, DDxBasis is the most accurate frozen-encoder model and close to the best published domain-specific results. Because every concept adds its own evidence, its reproducible concept basis splits each decision exactly, at no cost in accuracy. Without ordered stage or concept labels, the axes recover cell lineages and rare subtypes, agree with dermatologists' structure annotations, and expose planted and real shortcuts.

</details>


### [16] [Searching Ultra-Long Videos with Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33368)
*Ce Zhang,Baiqi Li,Seongsu Ha,Gedas Bertasius*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeLoVi框架通过使用预先训练的决策模型从超长视频中检索相关证据，相比生成式大语言模型更快速和经济，引入了DeLoVi-S和DeLoVi-E两种方法，分别通过生成短视频的字幕和自动策略优化来提高效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于相似性的检索方法虽然快速，但难于结合问题和视频内容进行推理；生成式大语言模型虽能推理，但生成过程慢且成本高。为了解决这些问题，提出了DeLoVi框架。

Method: DeLoVi-S: 为短视频生成字幕，结合决策模型评估字幕相关性，使用生成式大语言模型回答问题；DeLoVi-E: 自动策略优化，迭代改进记忆构建与检索策略。

Result: DeLoVi-S在三个超长视频理解基准测试（EgoLifeQA, EgoMemReason, MM-Lifelong）上达到最佳性能，DeLoVi-E通过策略优化减少了输入标记数量并提高了速度与准确性。

Conclusion: DeLoVi框架在超长视频理解任务中提供了更高的效率和性能，展示了在大规模视频数据检索中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Understanding ultra-long videos requires retrieving sparse, query-relevant evidence from extensive video histories. Existing similarity-based retrieval methods are efficient, but they encode the question and the video content independently and thus often miss evidence whose relevance requires reasoning over both. Generative large language models (LLMs) can perform such reasoning, but they typically generate text over multiple rounds of search, which makes each query slow and costly. In this work, we introduce DeLoVi (Decision Models for Long Video), a framework that leverages pretrained decision models to retrieve evidence from ultra-long videos. Like an LLM, a decision model reads the question together with the candidate evidence, so it can reason over both. Unlike an LLM, it directly outputs a relevance score without generating text, which makes it fast and inexpensive. We first present DeLoVi-S, a simple method that generates captions for short video clips and uses a decision model to rank those captions by their relevance to a question. A generative LLM then answers the question using the top-ranked captions. Despite its simplicity, DeLoVi-S achieves state-of-the-art performance on three ultra-long video understanding benchmarks, EgoLifeQA, EgoMemReason, and MM-Lifelong, and generalizes effectively to long-video understanding benchmarks, VideoMME-L and LVBench. However, DeLoVi-S scores every caption for every question, so its cost grows linearly with video length. To reduce this cost, we propose an automated policy evolution framework that iteratively refines the memory construction and retrieval policies around a fixed pretrained decision model. The resulting method, DeLoVi-E, maintains comparable performance while reducing input tokens by 34x on average compared with DeLoVi-S. It also runs over 3x faster than the best prior method on EgoLifeQA while achieving higher accuracy.

</details>


### [17] [Benchmarking Segmentation Models for Scalable Mangrove Mapping with Sentinel-1/2 Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33369)
*Damba Koné,Sara Mobsite,Joris Guérin,Élodie Blanchard,Thibault Catry*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究使用Sentinel-1雷达和Sentinel-2光学遥感影像，结合Pléiades高分辨率图像作为参考，通过四种基于CROMA和BigEarthNet预训练ResNet50的分割配置进行深度学习对比，发现CROMA + UPerNet性能最优，展示了多模态遥感数据预训练模型在监测红树林恢复中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红树林是沿海动态生态系统，受气候变化威胁，需要进行大规模监测。现有的高分辨率影像成本高且重访率低，而Sentinel系列卫星的影像虽免费但分辨率不足。因此，本研究旨在探讨使用多模态预训练模型在Sentinel数据上进行红树林变化检测的可能性。

Method: 本研究采用了四种基于CROMA和BigEarthNet预训练ResNet50的分割配置，包括CROMA + UNet、CROMA + DeepLabV3+、CROMA + UPerNet和BigEarthNet + UPerNet。在法属圭亚那、苏里南和阿玛帕地区构建了红树林制图数据集，并使用该数据集评估了每个配置的效果。

Result: 研究结果表明，CROMA + UPerNet的F1分数为0.9218，IoU为0.8549，表现最佳。其他配置的F1分数和IoU分别为：CROMA + UNet：F1=0.8452，IoU=0.7687；CROMA + DeepLabV3+: F1=0.8646，IoU=0.7972；BigEarthNet + UPerNet：F1=0.8693，IoU=0.8078。

Conclusion: 研究表明，预制的多模态遥感数据模型具有监测红树林恢复的潜力，可以通过大规模监测支持红树林恢复。

Abstract: Mangroves are dynamic coastal ecosystems that contribute to carbon storage, coastal protection and biodiversity conservation, but are vulnerable to climate change. Very-high-resolution imagery such as Pléiades enables detailed mangrove mapping but it is costly and have a low revisit rate. Large-scale monitoring requires free and regularly available data such as Sentinel-1 and Sentinel-2 but their spatial resolution poses challenges for detailed mangrove mapping. In this work, we benchmark deep learning approaches for mangrove mapping from Sentinel-1 radar and Sentinel-2 optical imagery using reference maps derived from very-high-resolution Pléiades imagery. We compare four segmentation configurations based on CROMA and BigEarthNet-pretrained ResNet50 representations with different segmentation decoders. We construct a mangrove mapping dataset covering French Guiana, Suriname, and Amapá. CROMA + UPerNet achieves the best performance, with an F1-score of 0.9218 and an IoU of 0.8549. These results highlight the potential of pretrained multimodal Earth observation representations and open Sentinel data to support mangrove resilience through scalable monitoring.

</details>


### [18] [MotifFlow: Training-Free, Self-Contained Style Transfer from Color to Motif in Rectified Flow Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33371)
*Chun Xie,Itaru Kitahara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MotifFlow 是一种无需训练的自包含框架，结合了风格注射和风格对齐指导，在 FLUX 模型中更好地实现了表达风格转移，通过调整潜在空间促使生成图像发展出风格的独特笔触和主题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的方法在 FLUX 中通过注意力注入进行风格转移时，虽然可以转移色彩但难以捕捉风格特有的笔触。为了弥补这一不足，我们分析并引入了 MotifFlow 框架。

Method: MotifFlow 不需要训练，结合风格注射与风格对齐指导，通过注意力输出之间的差异指导潜在空间更新，不依赖额外的图像编码器或训练适配器。

Result: 实验表明，MotifFlow 在 1,000 对内容-风格图像上，比基线方法更准确地匹配风格图像的局部纹理，并且生成的风格化结果更加一致。

Conclusion: 该设计在 FLUX.2 中同样有效。

Abstract: Expressive style transfer must reproduce a painting's palette, brushwork, and characteristic motifs while retaining the content image's structure. Attention injection is effective in U-Net-based diffusion models, but adapting it to FLUX often transfers color without capturing the style's distinctive brushwork. Our analysis across backbones links this gap to how injected features are integrated across neighboring spatial locations. We introduce MotifFlow, a training-free, self-contained framework that combines style injection with style-alignment guidance in FLUX. Injection supplies style features during generation but does not explicitly encourage the model's native attention output to align with them. We therefore use the discrepancy between native and injected attention outputs to guide latent-space updates, encouraging the evolving image to develop the style's distinctive brushwork and motifs. Style encoding, guidance, and synthesis all use the same frozen backbone, without auxiliary image encoders or trained adapters. A single strength parameter controls the transition from color transfer to pronounced brushwork and motifs, with additional support for regional stylization and style mixing. Experiments on 1,000 content-style pairs show that MotifFlow better matches the local textures of the style image than baseline methods and produces more consistent stylization than injection alone. The same design extends to FLUX.2.

</details>


### [19] [VersionGS: Compact and Motion-Robust Dynamic Gaussian Splatting via Versioned Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33374)
*Shihang Wei,Yichi Zhang,Fengqing Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VersionGS通过直接编码每一帧的Gaussian状态并仅使用运动估计来指导重建，解决了快动情况下现有方法重建质量下降的问题，尤其是舞蹈序列中，表现优于其他基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态Gaussian splatting方法在处理快动情况时重建质量降低，尤其是在有挑战性的舞蹈序列中，PSNR指标显示出显著差异。

Method: VersionGS方法基于直接编码每一帧的Gaussian状态，并利用运动估计来进行引导。它通过在一个共享字典中维护不变的Gaussian版本并在需要时添加新的版本，来实现版本的高效重复利用。同时引入选择性序列构造，通过光学流、照片差异或渲染错误来细化选择的Gaussian，并保留未改变的部分。字典随后经过修剪、模拟量化细化，最终被编码为图像。

Result: 在快速舞蹈序列上，VersionGS的PSNR提高了2.7 dB，且在最高度动的帧上PSNR减少不到0.3 dB。在N3DV和Technicolor数据集中，与最强基线TAD-GS相比，VersionGS所需的存储空间分别减少了6.0倍和5.7倍，同时还能达到更高的PSNR。此外，回放速度快，无需运动模型或神经网络支持，只需3DGS光栅化器。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法VersionGS能够显著改善高动态场景下的重建质量，具有良好的性能和效率，无需复杂的模型依赖，适用于实时应用。

Abstract: Recent dynamic Gaussian splatting methods encode temporal change, either as smooth functions of time or as constrained updates between frames. We observe that their reconstruction quality degrades significantly under fast motion. In challenging dance sequences, their PSNR drops by 1.1-4.0 dB on high-motion frames compared to low-motion frames. To overcome this limitation, we propose VersionGS, a method that directly encodes per-frame Gaussian states and uses motion estimation only to guide reconstruction. Within each group of frames, unchanged Gaussians keep their current versions in a shared dictionary, while modified Gaussians are appended as new versions. To maximize version reuse, we introduce selective sequential construction, which refines the Gaussians selected by optical flow, photograph differences or rendering errors and leaves the others unchanged. The dictionary is then pruned, fine-tuned with simulated quantization and encoded as images. Adjusting the pruning ratio trades off storage and quality without rebuilding the dictionary. On the fast dance sequences of DanceNet3D, VersionGS improves foreground PSNR by 2.7 dB over the best baseline, and its PSNR decreases by less than 0.3 dB on the highest-motion frames. On N3DV and Technicolor, it requires 6.0x and 5.7x less storage than TAD-GS, the strongest baseline overall, while achieving even higher PSNR. Finally, playback is fast and requires no motion model or neural network, only a 3DGS rasterizer.

</details>


### [20] [DreamTour: Layout Steering for Training-Free World Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33378)
*Srikumar Sastry,Meher Gitika Karumuri,Chenqi Li,Sarosij Bose,Mellon Zhang,Allen Tu,Ziyang Long,Yen-Liang Lin,Alexander Schwing,Nathan Jacobs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamTour 是一种无需训练的框架，能够从单张图像生成相机轨迹，同时更准确地控制相机运动。该方法首先通过冻结的图像填补先验将照片扩展到其原始视角之外，然后沿着预期的相机路径进行深度渲染以生成大约的目标视图。接着通过衰减的低频修正使早期视频潜变量与这些视图对齐，并使用自回归方法生成较长的视频片段，从而在整个轨迹中保持相机控制。实验结果表明，与现有的视频扩散和世界模型相比，DreamTour 能够提高方向准确性、行程校准，并且在保视频质量的同时增强相机控制和场景一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散和世界模型可以生成看起来很真实的视频，但通常不能可靠地匹配用户请求的相机方向和行程。因此，为了实现更准确和一致的相机控制，作者提出了一种无需训练的框架 DreamTour。

Method: 该方法首先利用冻结的图像填补先验来扩展照片的视图范围，然后使用深度渲染沿着预期的相机移动路径生成目标视图。接着将这些目标视图与早期视频潜变量通过衰减的低频修正对齐。为进一步的行程，采用自回归方法生成视频片段，以维持整个轨迹的相机控制。

Result: DreamTour 无论是在较短还是较长的轨迹上，都能够提高方向准确性和行程校准，同时依然保持与对照模型相当的视频质量。在 VBench 和 WorldScore 上的实验结果都证明了这一方法的有效性。

Conclusion: DreamTour 的提出表明，无需训练的空间引导能够为预训练的视频扩散和世界模型获得更准确和一致的相机控制，为实际应用带来了实用的技术支持。

Abstract: Video diffusion and world models generate plausible videos but do not reliably match the requested camera direction and travel distance. We present DreamTour, a training-free framework that generates camera tours from a single image with more accurate control over camera motion. DreamTour first uses a frozen image inpainting prior to extend the photograph beyond its original field of view while preserving the input pixels. Depth-based rendering of this extended scene along the requested camera path produces approximate target views that serve as spatial guidance. We introduce Layout Steering, which aligns early video latents with these views through decaying low-frequency corrections, leaving later denoising to refine appearance and dynamics. For longer tours, we autoregressively generate the video segments, repeating guidance construction from each segment's final frame to maintain camera control across the full trajectory. DreamTour improves directional accuracy and travel calibration on VBench while maintaining competitive video quality. On WorldScore, it strengthens camera control and scene consistency across chained movements. Together, these results demonstrate that training-free spatial guidance enables more accurate and consistent camera control in pretrained video diffusion and world models.

</details>


### [21] [ArtTet: Watertight Part Meshes of Articulated Objects from Rigid-Body Rules and Partitioned Marching Tetrahedra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33382)
*Sylvia Yuan,Dan Wang,Ravi Ramamoorthi,Xinrui Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ArtTet是一种从多视角观测的两个 articulated 状态重建艺术物体的方法，通过拆分物体为刚性部分并进行关节推断和四面体分割，生成互相不重叠的封闭部分网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ArtTet旨在解决当前方法无法有效分离物体不同部分的三角形、产生自交网格和空洞的问题，通过新的分割策略和优化方法提高重建的准确性和完整性。

Method: ArtTet采用分而治之的策略，首先通过动态和几何约束的关节推断将物体分割为刚性部分，然后使用多体注册恢复零件及其螺纹关节。接着，基于分割的四面体网格，对每个单元使用可微签距场优化各个部分网格。

Result: 在Articulate-100数据集上，使用ArtTet方法在所有对象中提供了最准确的轴方向、部分运动和部分几何信息，重建的零件网格几乎没有开放边界边。

Conclusion: ArtTet方法通过创新的分割和优化策略，解决了当前技术的常见问题，展示了优秀的重建效果。

Abstract: We present ArtTet, a method that reconstructs an articulated object from multi-view observations of two articulation states and outputs one watertight mesh per rigid part together with the joints that move them. Mesh splatting gives explicit triangles, but it treats the object as one scene. It extracts a single global surface and cannot keep the triangles of different parts apart; the mesh it exports is layered, self-intersecting, and full of holes. ArtTet follows a divide-and-conquer strategy instead. We first divide the object into rigid parts with dynamic- and geometric-constrained articulation and ownership inference. A multibody registration of the two states recovers the parts and their screw joints. Rigid-body rules on attachment, contact, and swept motion then correct joint types and part labels without any part annotation. We then divide space into part cells with an articulated tetrahedral partition, and conquer each cell with its own differentiable signed distance field on a shared tetrahedral grid. In these partitioned marching tetrahedra the fields are mutually exclusive by construction, so the extracted part meshes are closed and never overlap. All part meshes are optimized jointly from the images of both states through the recovered joints. On Articulate-100, ArtTet gives the most accurate axis directions, part motions, and part geometry over all objects among methods that use no part masks, and its part meshes have virtually no open boundary edges. We also show reconstructions of real captures from the SplArt dataset.

</details>


### [22] [Synchronizing Cameras via 3D Object Relationships](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33387)
*Sujong Shin,Sang-Il Choi,Kwang Moo Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过物体关系实现了多个动态事件视频的同步，利用3D物体关系作为稳定线索，结合深度估计和2D运动信息，通过鲁棒优化找到最佳的时间偏移量，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的像素级证据在动态场景下的运动快速和遮挡下难以可靠匹配，因此提出通过物体来帮助全局推理。

Method: 该方法通过给视觉语言模型几帧视频来识别场景中的物体，并通过开放词汇分割器跟踪和分割每个物体。使用预训练的深度估计器获取物体的3D位置，对于深度或分割失败的部分，则用2D运动补充，最后通过鲁棒优化找到各摄像机之间最佳的时间偏移量。

Result: 该方法在四个标准基准测试中表现优异，优于现有技术。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种基于物体关系的多视频同步方法，验证了物体作为稳定线索的有效性，并取得了很好的效果。

Abstract: In this paper, we synchronize multiple videos of the same dynamic event through object relationships. Driven by dynamic reconstruction and novel view synthesis, many recent synchronization methods rely on pixel-level evidence, such as point correspondences across views or the photometric losses of a reconstructed scene. In dynamic scenes, however, fast motion and occlusions make the moving regions that carry much of the timing information hard to match across views. We thus propose to bring back the traditional wisdom that objects are reliable cues for global reasoning about cameras. Measured in 3D, the relationships between objects, such as the distance between two people, are stable across all views up to scale; they can serve as cues for alignment. To compare these relationships across cameras, we only need the identity of each object in every view, as the cues are relational. We obtain these identities by giving a vision-language model a few frames of every camera and prompting it to identify the objects in the scene. We then segment and track each identified object through every video with an open-vocabulary segmentor. To obtain the 3D locations of objects, we use a pretrained depth estimator. Where depth or segmentation fails, we complement these 3D cues with 2D motion from point tracks. Finally, we synchronize the cameras by finding the temporal offsets at which the cues of different cameras agree best, first for each camera pair and then for all cameras jointly with robust optimization. We evaluate our method on four standard benchmarks, outperforming the state of the art.

</details>


### [23] [Measuring and Predicting Image-Token Repair Boundaries in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33388)
*Vishal Bhutani,Prasang Gupta,Sumanth Chundru,Kevin Paul*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究发现，对于退化图像，清洁图像的恢复效果取决于特定的编码器模型，其中12到23个检索到的标记能够在四个编码器模型中恢复一半的效果，而随机顺序需要四到十五倍的时间。Gemma模型相对于其他模型在注意力机制上有所不同，提供了非先验的一致性检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在探索如何通过清洁图像恢复视觉语言模型对退化图像的回答正确性，特别是在不同的编码器模型之间的差异。

Method: 使用合成的退化图像（模糊、噪声、低光）来测试四个不同的编码器模型。分析了不同恢复策略的效果，并对比了基于语言模型的不同方法之间的差异。

Result: 研究结果显示，12到23个排名靠前的标记可以在四个编码器模型中恢复一半的效果；而随机顺序则需要四到十五倍的时间。Gemma模型的注意力机制与其他模型存在显著差异，提供了一种非先验的一致性检查。

Conclusion: 实验表明，有效的图像恢复可以根据模型的具体表现进行预测，使用非先验的清洁图像灰度消融能够独立预测修复效果，而Gemma模型在注意力机制上的表现与MMLU和GQA不同，为模型的可靠性提供了额外的验证方法。

Abstract: Degraded images make vision-language models answer wrongly. Using synthetic paired blur, noise, and low light, we trace which clean image states can still repair those answers from the vision encoder through the language model. In four encoder models, 12 to 23 ranked tokens recover half of the full-patch effect, whereas random orders require four to fifteen times as many; encoder-free Gemma needs 22.2 versus 53.1. Full-block repair expires at a model-specific language-model crossing. The matched reverse-direction intervention is algebraically identical to text-token restoration, so its gap from image restoration measures a redundancy band rather than an independent predictor. A clean-image gray-ablation crossing independently predicts held-out repair with 1.53-layer error, against 3.65 for shared depth. Gemma MMBench has the only large attention-to-repair point gap; Gemma GQA does not. Restormer provides a non-oracle consistency check on two models.

</details>


### [24] [BreathSplat: Grounding One Smooth Motion in the Breathing Phase for 4D Cone-Beam CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33392)
*Sujong Shin,Sang-Il Choi,Kwang Moo Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种名为BreathSplat的方法，通过使用B样条在空间和时间上表示4D体积中的动态高斯分布，将呼吸相位与运动状态相结合，从而在模拟肺扫描和实患者扫描中表现优于现有动态方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的4D-CBCT重建和运动分析方法要么通过时间分箱，要么估计平滑动态运动，但没有将运动状态直接约束在呼吸相位上。本研究旨在通过将呼吸相位与运动状态结合，提高重建和运动的准确性。

Method: 本研究提出了一种通过B样条表示4D体积中动态高斯分布的方法，其中高斯分布的运动既在空间上，也在时间上。读取呼吸相位来自研究较多的常规代理来约束相同呼吸周期中同一解剖结构和运动。

Result: 在模拟肺扫描中，BreathSplat方法在重建和运动精度上优于最新动态方法，包括那些已知呼吸相位真实值的方法。同时在两个实际患者的扫描中，本方法还提供了比现有先进水平更清晰的重建。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的BreathSplat方法，在提高4D-CBCT重建和运动准确度方面取得显著进步，并在实际应用中也显示出更好的性能。

Abstract: Four-dimensional cone-beam computed tomography (4D-CBCT) images the anatomy of a breathing patient, but sees each breathing phase from only a few angles. Breathing, however, is a smooth motion that repeats; it has a phase. Existing methods exploit this either by reconstructing the anatomy as a function of the breathing phase, often through bins, or by estimating smooth dynamic motions in time. Yet none of these grounds one smooth motion in the phase. We thus propose to share projections across breathing phases with splines. Specifically, we represent the 4D volume with dynamic Gaussians whose motion uses B-splines in space and time. We ground the motion state in the respiratory phase, which we read from well-studied conventional surrogates. Projections at the same phase in different breathing cycles then constrain the same anatomy and motion. On simulated lung scans, our method, BreathSplat, outperforms recent dynamic methods in reconstruction and motion accuracy, including those given the ground-truth phase. We further show qualitatively, on two real patient scans, that our method provides clearer reconstructions than the state of the art.

</details>


### [25] [ArtKit: Motion-Driven Re-Segmentation and Link Re-Generation for Articulated Objects from RGB-D Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33403)
*Sylvia Yuan,Dan Wang,Vivian Yu,Ravi Ramamoorthi,Xinrui Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ArtKit 利用 RGB-D 视频自动生成具有完整部分的可拆分几何模型、运动树及其关节，并通过对比可视化来验证模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对于带有可视化的可拆分几何模型生成的框架限制了复杂物体的表示能力，特别是在关节和运动的分解上。

Method: 首先使用生成的部分先验提出完整分解的几何基础，然后将点轨迹归类到刚性轨迹中，将生成的部分划分为连杆。接着，通过可视化的能量公式评估旋转和棱柱关节的拟合，确保该模型可以解释整个视频的深度。还根据视频选择同步群集拟合或每帧链式拟合。

Result: 所提出的 ArtKit 在 Articulate-100 和 HArt 新基准上的表现优于最新基线方法，在对象和手部的轴和运动轴误差上提高了超过一半。

Conclusion: ArtKit 证明了其在实际和合成数据集上的有效性，并成功处理了多种物体（包括家用物品和机器人手部），通过验证几何图形的完整性，证明了其广泛的适用性。

Abstract: An RGB-D camera that films an object being moved, often from one viewpoint, sees one side of it and how its parts move. We present ArtKit, which takes such a video as input to create an articulated model with complete parts, a kinematic tree, and typed joints. Our central idea is generating the hidden geometry and verifying it against what the camera sees. A generative part prior proposes complete, part-decomposed geometry based on the first frame. Point tracks, grouped into rigid trajectories, split and merge the generated parts into links. We then fit revolute and prismatic joints, with a visibility-aware energy ensuring that the articulated model explains the depth observed across the video. A rule checks against the video to choose between synchronous group fits and per-frame chain fits, so that one pipeline serves a diverse range of articulated objects, such as household items and robotic hands. Finally, we regenerate each link of the chosen fit as one part and keep it wherever it agrees better with the observations. We evaluate on Articulate-100 and on HArt, a new benchmark of 60 PartNet-Mobility objects and 8 robotic hands and grippers rendered as RGB-D videos with ground-truth parts and joints. ArtKit reduces the axis and part motion errors of the strongest baseline by more than half on the objects, as well as the axis error on the hands. ArtKit also runs on real recordings and generated videos.

</details>


### [26] [A Semantics-First Approach to Multimodal Video Captioning with Frozen Encoders and Stabilized Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33409)
*Albert Chiwara,Erhan Guven*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为AVQFormer的音频-视 觉查询转换器，结合了自适应自我关键序列训练 (ASCST) 和熵正则化，旨在解决视频数据集中评估指标对视觉上下文依赖的问题。实验表明，仅保留视频内容的模型在CIDEr指标上显著提升，揭示了评估指标可能存在的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前，多模态大型语言模型通常依赖全局音频表示来弥补视觉编码器的不高效。本文提出AVQFormer旨在解决这一问题，通过特定的训练方法和架构设计，提升模型在视觉重点评估指标下的表现。

Method: AVQFormer采用一个2.7B参数的LLM，配备了一个固定长度的16个单元的语义对齐模块，并通过ASCST和熵正则化进行优化。此外，通过不同构架的比较实验来探索视觉评价指标的潜在偏见。

Result: 对于MSR-VTT数据集，AVQFormer在仅保留视频内容的模型上达到了63.0 CIDEr，尽管低于Qwen2.5-VL的67.4 CIDEr，但证明了对于参数量小于3B的LLM来说，这是一个强有力的基础模型。此外，实验证明CIDEr指标可能偏向于视觉上下文，可能无法全面捕捉多模态语义丰富性。

Conclusion: AVQFormer在解决视觉评价指标偏见方面展现潜力，未来的研究可以通过进一步优化模型架构和评估指标来更好地服务于多模态语义的理解与生成。

Abstract: We propose the Audio-Visual Query Transformer (AVQFormer), a highly efficient architecture built upon a 2.7B parameter LLM featuring a fixed-length $K=16$ semantic alignment module, stabilized via Adaptive Self-Critical Sequence Training (ASCST) with Entropy Regularization. Multimodal Large Language Models (LLMs) often rely on global audio representations to compensate for inefficient, frame-based visual encoders. While our Phase 1 prompted evaluations demonstrate that the Multimodal Base Model achieves strong deep semantic alignment (scoring 0.889 on BERTScore F1), optimizing this architecture for legacy metrics reveals a performance gap. We explore this gap as a hypothesis we term temporal alignment friction: a conflict where global audio representations disrupt the chronological sequence of visual frames, leading to penalties under visually-focused evaluation metrics. Consequently, by stripping away the audio, our Video-Only ablation conforms more closely to this rigid syntax, achieving 63.0 CIDEr. While trailing larger models such as Qwen2.5-VL (67.4 CIDEr), this establishes a strong baseline for sub-3B parameter LLM-based models. This indicates that CIDEr, as applied to datasets such as MSR-VTT, may exhibit a bias toward visually grounded syntax, limiting its ability to fully capture multimodal semantic richness.

</details>


### [27] [Beyond Random Starts: Amortized Initialization for Adversarial Attacks on Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33414)
*Haz Sameen Shahgir,Ali Asif Khan,K. M. Mehemud Azad,Dongwei Lyu,Zhipeng Wei,Rifat Shahriyar,Yufei Li,Xian Chen,Xiaohan Wei,Yunchen Pu,Fei Tian,Chonglin Sun,Frank Shyu,Sandeep Pandey,Luke Simon,Xi Liu,N. Benjamin Erichson,Yue Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPARK 使用循环扰动生成器在不需要对每个扰动进行优化的情况下，提供有效的初始扰动，从而提高黑盒视觉-语言模型对抗攻击的成功率并减少优化步骤，同时生成高效的独立对抗攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于梯度的转移性对抗攻击需要大量的优化步骤，SPARK旨在通过学习来提供更好的初始扰动，从而减少攻击所需的时间。

Method: SPARK 提出了一种轻量级的循环扰动生成器，该生成器同时依赖源图像和目标图像。生成器通过反复应用相同的网络来细化扰动，生成有效的对抗性扰动，无需通过替代视觉编码器计算梯度。

Result: SPARK 提供了有效的预热初始扰动，显著提高了攻击成功率，减少了所需优化步骤，并且这些改进适用于不同的攻击方法、视觉-语言模型和未见过的图像类别。此外，SPARK 还能够生成高效的独立对抗性攻击，计算成本远低于基于梯度的优化。

Conclusion: SPARK 将循环扰动生成器与基于梯度的对抗性攻击结合，提供了有效、高效的对抗性攻击方法，可以广泛应用于各种视觉-语言模型和不同的攻击方法。

Abstract: Gradient-based transferable adversarial attacks achieve state-of-the-art attack success rates against black-box vision-language models (VLMs). However, they require hundreds of costly optimization steps for each image pair. One possible reason is that these attacks typically start from random perturbations that contain no information about the source or target image. In this work, we study whether amortized adversarial generation can provide better initializations for gradient-based attacks. By learning across many image pairs, amortization allows a generator to produce adversarial perturbations for new inputs without optimizing each perturbation from scratch. We introduce SPARK, a lightweight looped perturbation generator conditioned on both the source and target images. SPARK repeatedly applies the same network to refine a perturbation, producing effective adversarial attacks in a few forward passes without requiring gradients through surrogate vision encoders at inference time. We show that these perturbations provide effective warm starts for existing gradient-based attacks, substantially improving attack success rates while reducing the number of optimization steps required. These improvements generalize across different attack methods, VLMs, and unseen image categories. SPARK also produces effective standalone attacks at a fraction of the computational cost of gradient-based optimization. Code and model weights are available at https://github.com/Patchwork53/SPARK.

</details>


### [28] [Anatomy-Aware Pseudo-Labels and Homotopy-Annealed Hausdorff Supervision for Semi-Supervised Pancreas Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33425)
*Alireza Kheyrkhah,Amir Hossein Saleknia,Reza Azad,Sina Houshmand,Dorit Merhof,Ulas Bagci,Alaa Sulaiman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种新的方法，通过Anatomical Rectification和Boundary Grafting来改进胰腺CT图像分割任务，并通过Homotopy-Annealed Hausdorff（HAH）监督进一步提高准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提高胰腺CT图像分割的准确性，尤其是针对单个连续器官且断开部分在解剖学上不可能的情况，研究者提出了新的分割方法和监督策略。

Method: 引入了Anatomical Rectification和Boundary Grafting，以及Homotopy-Annealed Hausdorff（HAH）监督来进行图像分割。这些方法分别通过重新标记不可能的组织片段和移植已有标签表面区域到未标记区域来改进分割结果。HAH监督则通过软化预测与注释的距离来提高预测的可靠性。

Result: 在NIH和MSD Task07数据集上，该方法显著提高了分割指标，尤其是在较低标注样本的情况下。特别是在PanTS-SSL评估协议下，该方法在100个标注样本时超过了对照基线，并在250个标注样本时达到85.29%的Dice系数。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的方法通过改进分割方法和引入新的监督策略，在不完全标注数据中显著提高了胰腺CT图像分割的精度。

Abstract: Confidence-gated co-training assumes that pseudo-label errors are uncertain and concentrated at organ boundaries. We argue that for pancreas CT, where the target organ is a single connected structure and detached fragments are anatomically implausible, the dominant failures arise at three levels: where the organ is, what its boundary should look like, and how a correct boundary is penalized. Anatomical Rectification relabels implausible fragments of a teacher prediction as confident background, without adding any loss or parameter; an audit confirms that its rejections are overwhelmingly detached hallucinations and that thin, tail-like slices are not eroded. Boundary Grafting transplants labelled surface regions with their annotation into unlabeled volumes, replacing unreliable boundary pseudo-labels with ground truth. The residual error is coherent surface displacement, weakly penalized by permutation-invariant Dice and cross-entropy losses. Homotopy-Annealed Hausdorff (HAH) supervision anneals a distance-weighted penalty from an annotation-anchored objective that is convex in the prediction to a Hausdorff surrogate, admitting the prediction-dependent distance term only after the predicted surface becomes reliable. On NIH pancreas CT, under the standard semi-supervised split, the full method reaches 84.60% Dice and 4.97 voxels 95HD, improving all four metrics over compared methods; on MSD Task07, 78.90% Dice and 4.19 voxels 95HD. We further contribute PanTS-SSL, a semi-supervised evaluation protocol derived from the public 9,901-case PanTS corpus, with a curated 1,015-volume main pool, labelled tiers of 50, 100 and 250 volumes, an 80-case tumor track, shift probes for acquisition and anatomy change, and a fixed 901-volume test set. On it, the method exceeds the co-training baseline at 100 labels, scales to 85.29% Dice at 250 labels, and reaches 85.50% Dice at 100 labels with HAH.

</details>


### [29] [ViCARD: Visual Credit Assignment with Reward-Gated Distillation for Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33437)
*Zijian Huang,Jiasi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViCARD通过结合每个标记的视觉监督和支持正确性的奖励，提高了细粒度视觉推理性能，同时保留了广泛的视觉推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法面临由于感知错误导致的缺乏学习信号和广播优势的问题，而视觉自蒸馏虽然提供了密集的指导，但学生学习仅从标记分布中很难产生正确的最终答案。

Method: ViCARD利用裁剪后的教师模型为学生提供每个标记的监督，同时评估答案的正确性。如果所有样本轮次都失败，它会采样额外的裁剪教师答案，并仅在该答案正确时激活策略梯度的离策训练。

Result: 与基于策略梯度的RL和蒸馏基线相比，ViCARD在细粒度的视觉推理性能上表现出显著改进，同时也保持了广泛的视觉推理能力。

Conclusion: ViCARD通过细粒度的视觉信用分配和正确性的奖励机制，有效地解决了视觉推理中的问题，并展示了在视觉推理任务中的优越性。

Abstract: Visual reasoning requires models to identify relevant visual evidence and use it to derive correct answers. Outcomebased reinforcement learning (RL) faces two key challenges in this regime: it yields no learning signal when perceptual errors cause all sampled responses to fail, and it broadcasts a uniform advantage across every token within a response. Conversely, while visual self-distillation offers dense guidance from evidence-centered image crops, student learning from token distributions alone often fails to elicit correct final answers. Motivated by an audit of training rollouts that exposes these complementary failure modes, we introduce ViCARD (Visual Credit Assignment with RewardGated Distillation), which integrates per-token visual supervision with verified correctness rewards. For each rollout group, we leverage a teacher with its input image cropped to the relevant visual area, and use this to provide per-token supervision to the student while also valuing correctness. If all sampled rollouts fail, ViCARD samples an additional crop-teacher answer and activates on-policy distillation only if that answer is correct. Together, these mechanisms provide fine-grained visual credit within informative reward groups and a learning signal for otherwise uninformative all-fail groups. Extensive comparisons across model scales demonstrate that ViCARD improves fine-grained visual reasoning performance over RL and distillation baselines, while retaining broad visual reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [30] [HumblePRM: Improving VLM Abstention in Ambiguous Situations through Process Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33452)
*Saket Reddy,Ke Yang,ChengXiang Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为HumblePRM的新方法，将多模态推理分为感知和校准两个阶段，并通过独立评估每个阶段的表现来提高模型在模糊输入上的不确定表态率，同时保持清晰图像上的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法在模型表达自信时确保其依据实际看到的内容，或者在缺乏明确结论时无法有效评估模型的不确定性。此外，标准后训练使用仅监督最终决策的结果奖励模型，可能导致模型产生不合理的自信。

Method: 研究将多模态推理过程分为感知和校准两个阶段，通过引入HumblePRM过程奖励模型独立评估每个阶段的表现，侧重于同意证据和表达信心而不是结果的正确性来奖励模型。

Result: 使用HumblePRM进行训练增加了模糊输入上的不确定性表态，而对清晰图像的准确性影响不大。此外，研究者还发现这种方法在Gemma 4 E4B大型边缘模型上也有效。

Conclusion: 研究得出结论，过程奖励监督是一个有前途的方法，可以促进更加可靠、更好地校准不确定表态的视觉语言模型。

Abstract: Reliable deployment of Vision Language Models (VLMs) requires that a model's expressed confidence remain grounded in what it can actually see, and that it recognize when the visual evidence is too ambiguous to commit to a confident response. Standard post-training uses outcome reward models (ORMs) that supervise only the final decision, rewarding a confident answer regardless of whether the image supports one, while existing methods either tend to detect uncertainty post-hoc or assume an objective verdict always exists. We instead split multimodal reasoning into two stages: perception, where the model must factually describe the scene, and calibration, where it must judge whether that evidence is sufficient to commit to a conclusion. We introduce HumblePRM, a lightweight process reward model (PRM) that scores each stage independently, rewarding agreement between evidence and expressed confidence rather than outcome correctness alone. Training with HumblePRM increases abstention due to uncertainty on ambiguous inputs considerably while leaving accuracy on clear images roughly unchanged. We also find that the improvement transfers to Gemma 4 E4B, a larger, popular edge model. Our findings show that process reward supervision is a promising route to VLMs with better calibrated abstention, ensuring more reliability rather than unpredictable behavior.

</details>


### [31] [Recovering What Matters, Transferring Only What Is Reliable: Toward Precise Reference-Based Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33455)
*Sihan Ji,Wenxin Chen,Yue Shi,Jiezhang Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了PreciseRef框架，解决传统RefSR方法中存在的感知合理性和内容保真度问题，通过引入Asymmetric Twin-Attention实现细粒度的参考信息检索，结合Dual-Scale Reference Gating确保信息传递的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RefSR方法在感知合理性和内容保真度方面存在缺陷，PreciseRef框架旨在解决这些问题。

Method: PreciseRef框架采用了Asymmetric Twin-Attention (ATA)，使目标图像和参考图像能够在统一的空间中参与注意力机制，同时保留参考图像的形状。还引入了Dual-Scale Reference Gating（DRG），包括Semantic Relevance Gating (SRG)用于过滤全局无关的参考上下文，Bidirectional Consistency Gating (BCG)用于抑制不可靠的局部对应关系。

Result: 实验结果表明，PreciseRef方法在字符准确性和代码解码率方面分别达到了85.6%和76.3%，远超现有方法中的17.4%和0%。此外，方法还在CUFED5和WR-SR上实现了最先进的感知质量。

Conclusion: PreciseRef框架通过引入Asymmetric Twin-Attention和Dual-Scale Reference Gating，在参考内容恢复的准确性和抗参考不匹配鲁棒性方面显示出优越性。

Abstract: Reference-based super-resolution (RefSR) aims to recover missing details from high-quality references. However, existing methods face two fundamental challenges. First, perceptual plausibility does not guarantee content fidelity. Existing methods often produce visually convincing details without faithfully recovering reference-specific information, such as text, codes, and identity cues. Second, faithful reference reading does not guarantee reliable information transfer. Increasing reference utilization also exposes the restoration process to misleading content, as global semantic relevance does not necessarily imply local correspondence consistency. To address these challenges, we propose PreciseRef, a reference-aware generative framework for faithful and reliable RefSR. At its core, Asymmetric Twin-Attention (ATA) allows target and reference images to participate in a unified attention space while the reference keeps its own shape. By letting their image tokens interact directly through joint attention, ATA facilitates fine-grained reference information retrieval without explicit image alignment. To ensure reliable information transfer, we introduce Dual-Scale Reference Gating (DRG), which combines Semantic Relevance Gating (SRG) to filter globally irrelevant reference context and Bidirectional Consistency Gating (BCG) to suppress unreliable local correspondences. Furthermore, we establish VeriRef, a benchmark evaluating exact reference-dependent content recovery and robustness to mismatched references. Extensive experiments show that our method achieves 85.6% character accuracy and 76.3% exact code decoding, compared with 17.4% and 0% for existing methods, while effectively suppressing erroneous reference transfer. It also achieves state-of-the-art perceptual quality on CUFED5 and WR-SR.

</details>


### [32] [Playfulness as Feedback for Agentic Game Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33457)
*Weijia Mao,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Zhen Fang,Danze Chen,Dongxing Mao,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为Play2Evolve的游戏开发框架，通过逐级优化功能、视觉和游戏机制，以及结合视频生成模型自动创建可互动的静态和动态资产，以提高游戏的质量和可玩性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽然能够生成可运行的游戏，但未能确保优秀的玩家体验。为此，研究者提出了Play2Evolve框架，旨在改善游戏的核心功能、视觉设计和丰富游戏玩法。

Method: Play2Evolve框架包含初始化、细化和进化三个阶段。通过游戏测试和诊断功能、视觉展示和游戏机制中的问题，利用玩家反馈改进Bug和资产，并进一步提出新的游戏和美学目标以促进游戏的持续进化。此外，框架结合视频生成模型自动生成静态和动态资产，并将其与互动游戏玩法集成。

Result: 实验结果显示，Play2Evolve框架的性能在GameCraft-Bench中达到60.05，比Game Initialization高出10.24，比Play2Code高出7.59。人类对其总体质量评价达到了3.67，相较于Game Initialization的2.38和Play2Code的2.43有了显著提高。

Conclusion: Play2Evolve框架通过结合视频生成模型和游戏测试反馈，显著提高了游戏的开发质量和可玩性，并达到了当前技术的领先水平。

Abstract: Coding agents can generate runnable games from natural-language descriptions. However, a runnable game does not guarantee an excellent gameplay experience for players. In our view, a playful game should provide 1) reliable core functionality, 2) appealing visual design and assets, and 3) rich gameplay that supports exploration and progression. How agents can improve these aspects of playfulness remains underexplored. In our work, we propose Play2Evolve, a recursive game development framework with three stages: Initialization, Refinement, and Evolution. Using playfulness as feedback, agents playtest the game and diagnose issues in its functionality, visual presentation, and gameplay. Their feedback guides bug fixes and asset improvements. It also helps agents propose new gameplay and aesthetic goals for continued game evolution. To enrich game materials, we combine a video generation model with code to create appealing static and dynamic assets and integrate them into interactive gameplay. Finally, Play2Evolve achieves state-of-the-art performance among the evaluated methods, scoring 60.05 on GameCraft-Bench compared with 50.81 after Game Initialization and 52.46 for Play2Code. Human overall-quality ratings reach 3.67, compared with 2.38 and 2.43, respectively. Our code will be released at https://github.com/showlab/Play2Evolve.

</details>


### [33] [FoamSurf: A Voronoi-Surfel Representation for Differentiable Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33462)
*Grzegorz Wilczyński,Julia Farganus,Krzysztof Byrski,Rafał Tobiasz,Mikołaj Zieliński,Dominik Belter,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FoamSurf 是一种混合表示法，使用平面高斯surfels 表示表面，使用空间填充Voronoi单元表示介质。该方法可以沿任意光线复合两者，训练先验条件使它们分离，无需分割掩码。该方法在实际水下场景、合成场景和浓雾场景中实现了与最新技术相当的图像质量和最准确的表面-介质分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法在表面渲染中可能会留下介质痕迹，而神经领域虽然能够更准确地分离表面和介质，但训练速度慢且难以编辑。FoamSurf旨在克服这些限制，提供高质量的图像和独立的表面与介质编辑功能。

Method: FoamSurf 使用平面高斯surfels 表示表面，使用空间填充Voronoi单元表示介质。通过单个可微分光线追踪器沿任意光线复合两者，利用训练先验条件保持它们独立，而无需使用分割掩码。

Result: FoamSurf 在实际水下场景、合成场景和浓雾环境中达到了与最先进的方法相当的图像质量，并且能够准确分开表面和介质。此方法支持独立移除、编辑和样式化回收的组件，同时可以插入网格对象并重现适当的衰减和散射效果。

Conclusion: FoamSurf 提供了一种有效的方法，可以解决表面渲染和介质分离的问题，以及实现了高质量图像的自编辑功能。

Abstract: Scenes inside a scattering medium contain solid surfaces, such as the seabed or rocks, and a semi-transparent medium, such as water, fog, or smoke. Recent Gaussian-based methods produce realistic images of scenes in scattering media, but traces of the medium can remain visible in surface-only renders. Neural fields separate surfaces and medium more accurately, but they are slow to train and difficult to edit. Both approaches evaluate the medium only along camera rays, so its effects along reflected and refracted rays are not modeled. These limitations prevent reliable surface recovery, independent editing of surfaces and the medium, and consistent rendering of inserted objects. We present FoamSurf, a hybrid representation that models surfaces with planar Gaussian surfels and the medium with space-filling Voronoi cells. A single differentiable ray tracer composites both along arbitrary rays, while training priors keep them separate without segmentation masks. Across real underwater scenes, synthetic scenes, and dense fog, FoamSurf achieves image quality on par with the state of the art and the most accurate surface-medium separation among the compared methods. The recovered components can be removed, edited, or stylized independently, and the same reconstruction supports inserted mesh objects with attenuation and scattering along both direct and reflected or refracted rays. Together, these capabilities enable scene restoration, editing, and composition within a single reconstructed model.
  Project page: https://grzegorzwilczynski.com/foamsurf/

</details>


### [34] [OrdiHand-GS: Ordinal-Guided Tracking and Hand-Refined Gaussian Avatars for Digital Signers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33465)
*Mingxuan Ma,Ronglai Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为OrdiHand-GS的方法，用于解决双手交叉时表达手部动画追踪中的深度顺序模糊和手部外观模糊问题。方法通过视频深度估计的相对深度和显式手部大小投票来确定手掌面的前后关系，增强了追踪准确性，并通过残差头精细调整手部UV高斯的位置，最终在GUAVA框架中实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服表达手部动画追踪中双手交叉导致的深度顺序模糊和手部外观模糊的问题，提高手语表达的准确性和视觉效果。

Method: OrdiHand-GS 方法通过以下步骤解决双手交叉产生的问题：(1) 使用视频深度估计的相对深度与显式手部大小投票来建立手掌面的前后关系；(2) 通过残差头调整手部UV高斯的位置；(3) 在GUAVA框架下具体实施该方法，优化表达手部动画追踪和渲染。

Result: 与匹配的无约束细化方法相比，该方法在前臂秩序准确性上提高了44.4%，但增加了0.39像素的再投影误差；相比之下，随机手语对照组没有显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: OrdiHand-GS 方法显著提高了表达手部动画的准确性，尤其是在前臂秩序准确性和视觉效果上，为手语表达的未来发展提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Digital signers, also known as signing avatars, convey sign language through animated virtual humans, where visual fidelity and linguistic intelligibility hinge primarily on the hands. When one hand crosses over the other, two failure modes dominate. First, monocular expressive-human tracking suffers from ordinal depth ambiguity: either hand can satisfy the same 2D landmarks, and the baseline tracker orders wrists at chance level on 365 annotated overlapping frames from How2Sign. Second, hand appearance remains blurred in neural rendering even after geometry is corrected. We propose OrdiHand-GS to address these errors separately. Relative depth from a video depth estimator and apparent hand size vote, with confidence weighting and abstention, for a front-back relation, which enters expressive-human tracking as a hinge on median hand-surface depth. A source-conditioned residual head then refines the local positions of hand-associated UV Gaussians with the avatar backbone and renderer frozen. We instantiate the framework with GUAVA. Against matched unconstrained refinement, wrist-order accuracy rises from 32.9% to 77.3% at a cost of 0.39 pixels of reprojection error, while a random-sign placebo gives no balanced-accuracy gain. A 2x2 factorial evaluation attributes most of the interaction-rendering improvement to tracking-stage refinement, with a smaller additional benefit from the residual.

</details>


### [35] [SplatWAM: Splatting World Action Models for Real-Time 3D Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33467)
*Frank Tsu-Fang Lu,Karthik Sundar Senthilkumar,Christopher Thirgood,Oscar Mendez,Simon Hadfield*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SplatWAM是一种生成式世界模型，它可以以固定预算的高斯令牌编码变量数量的未摆布视图，并直接在紧凑的3D高斯基元上学习动作条件下的动力学。使用对数欧几里得协方差坐标和可微渲染监督，SplatWAM在单个动作的前向传递中预测未来的高斯状态，在场景生成和机器人操控任务中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型的局限性在于它们在像素或潜在空间中预测未来观察值，这限制了它们直接代表真实三维场景演化的潜力。SplatWAM的目标是克服这些限制，通过学习动作条件下的动力学直接在紧凑的3D高斯基元上操作，从而提高模型在场景生成和机器人操控任务中的表现。

Method: SplatWAM包括一个标量子化器SplatWAM-T，它可以编码未摆布的视图到固定预算的高斯令牌中；以及一个动力学模型SplatWAM-D，它可以在单个动作的前向传递中通过对数欧几里得协方差坐标和可微渲染监督预测未来的高斯状态。然后，通过渲染监督对模型进行训练，确保每个预测状态可以从任意视角重新绘制，从而构建共享的3D表示。

Result: 在OGBench Scene上，SplatWAM以约80 FPS的速度渲染新型视图的12倍，具有更高(2.0 dB)的PSNR。SplatWAM-P（一种轻量级的流动匹配策略头）进一步与动态模型一起训练，在8个任务套件中实现了无学习价值函数的高斯令牌策略在不依赖像素或潜在空间情况下超过基于像素的策略24.8个百分点。在LIBERO数据集上，我们的~1B模型超过了WorldVLA (~7B)和OpenVLA (~7B)模型，同时与没有大型机器人预训练的OpenVLA-OFT相匹配。

Conclusion: 通过对比实验证明，SplatWAM在高维场景生成任务以及3D动态模型的训练中具有显著优势，未来可进一步应用于更多任务和场景中。

Abstract: World models typically predict future observations in pixel or latent space, limiting their ability to directly represent true 3D scene evolution. We present SplatWAM (Splatting World Action Model), a generative world model that learns action-conditioned dynamics directly over compact 3D Gaussian primitives. A tokenizer SplatWAM-T first encodes a variable number of unposed views into a fixed budget of Gaussian tokens. A dynamics model SplatWAM-D then predicts future Gaussian states in a single forward pass per action, using log-Euclidean covariance coordinates and differentiable rendering supervision to facilitate learning in an explicit 3D space. Each predicted state can be rasterized from arbitrary viewpoints, enforcing a shared 3D representation across views by construction. On OGBench Scene, SplatWAM renders novel views 12x faster than video world models (~80 FPS on an RTX 5090) with up to 2.0 dB higher PSNR. We further introduce SplatWAM-P, a lightweight flow-matching policy head jointly trained with the dynamics model. Across 8 task suites, model-free Gaussian-token policies outperform pixel-based counterparts by up to 24.8 percentage points. SplatWAM-P improves over Latent Particle World Model (LPWM) by 12 percentage points and matches HIQL without a learned value function. On LIBERO, our ~1B model outperforms WorldVLA (~7B) and the robot-pretrained OpenVLA (~7B), while matching OpenVLA-OFT when both are trained without large-scale robot pretraining. Code and checkpoints will be released.

</details>


### [36] [Streaming Tax: Can Streaming VLMs Replace Their Offline Parents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33474)
*Zhi Yang,Junzhou Chen,Jindong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了评估流式视觉语言模型在离线任务上表现的Streaming Tax框架，发现流式模型在离线任务上保留了一定的理解能力，但在信任度和泛化能力上存在显著差距，提出了Stream++插件以提升流式模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究流式视觉语言模型是否能在不牺牲离线任务性能的情况下保持对用户的交互能力。

Method: 设计并应用Streaming Tax框架，评估多个流式模型和离线模型在15个基准上的表现。

Result: 发现流式模型在线下任务上的理解能力基本保持不变，但在信任度和泛化性方面存在大量缺陷。

Conclusion: 提出Stream++插件以部分解决流式模型在离线下任务中的不足，提高其功能性和安全性。

Abstract: Most vision-language models (VLMs) specialize in offline video understanding; in contrast, their post-trained versions, streaming VLMs, can perform live interactions. We investigate whether streaming VLMs can replace their offline parent models on offline tasks. Such replacement could reduce deployment costs and improve efficiency. We propose Streaming Tax to systematically evaluate streaming VLMs on offline tasks. Our exploration spans three core dimensions: understanding, trustworthiness, and generalization across 15 benchmarks. Our findings reveal a critical gap between offline capability preservation and practical replaceability. This gap emerges at two stages: streaming adaptation can alter trustworthy behavior even when offline understanding is largely preserved, while the native streaming protocol can introduce further performance losses. Our analyses show that some of these losses arise from restricted access to historical visual evidence and failures to deliver usable responses. Importantly, regarding safety, streaming models often leave both harmful and legitimate requests unanswered rather than genuinely refusing unsafe ones. We then propose Stream++, a training-free plugin to improve streaming VLMs on offline tasks.
