We found that the proposed method is able to upgrade the performance of each of the EfficientNet architecture. Several smaller architectures are even able to outperform the larger architecture that was trained using the standard transfer learning method.

</details>


### [108] [UPP-Gauss: Ultrasound Propagation Physics-informed Gaussian Splatting for B-mode Novel-View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33920)
*Guil Jung,Seok-Hwan Oh,Myeong-Gee Kim,Young-Min Kim,Hyeon-Jik Lee,Sang-Yun Kim,Hyeok-Jun Kwon,Hyuk-Sool Kwon,Hyeon-Min Bae*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UPP-Gauss 提出了一种基于物理的模型，通过隐式高斯表示声学传播来合成 B-模式超声图象，表现出高效且高质量的重建效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确合成B-模式超声图象需要考虑声波在组织中传播以及在探头不同姿态下产生的视觉变化。UPP-Gauss 方法旨在通过整合这些声学交互作用来提高图像合成的准确性。

Method: UPP-Gauss 采用物理启发的高斯散点方法，通过采样三个3D高斯场，结合声波传播来构建声学图，并进而合成B-模式图。

Result: UPP-Gauss 方法在三个具有不同声学特性的数据集上，均表现出最高的重建质量。相对于基线模型，平均提升了1.43 dB 的 PSNR。

Conclusion: UPP-Gauss 方法能通过显式建模超声波传播，实现准确且高效的B-模式多视角合成。

Abstract: Accurate novel-view synthesis of B-mode ultrasound images requires modeling acoustic propagation through tissue and the resulting appearance variations across probe poses. We propose Ultrasound Propagation Physics-informed Gaussian Splatting (UPP-Gauss), which integrates these acoustic interactions into an explicit Gaussian representation. UPP-Gauss constructs acoustic maps by sampling three 3D Gaussian fields using the probe pose and acoustic beam propagation direction, then combines these maps according to ultrasound propagation to synthesize B-mode images. Across three acoustically diverse datasets, UPP-Gauss achieves the best reconstruction quality with efficient rendering, improving PSNR over the best baseline on each dataset by 1.43 dB on average. These results demonstrate accurate and efficient B-mode novel-view synthesis through explicit Gaussian modeling of ultrasound propagation.

</details>


### [109] [Counter-DECEIVR: Counterfactual Explanations for Deepfake Detectors using Detector-Guided Rectified Flow Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34032)
*Mariano V. Ntrougkas,Vasileios Mezaris,Ioannis Patras*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Counter-DECEIVR 是一种无需训练生成器或调整检测器的新框架，通过使用现成的深度假信息检测器的梯度，在矫正流模型的速率场中引导潜在变量，针对显著区域进行局部编辑，从而生成反事实例子。该方法在两个假信息检测器和两个广泛使用的取证数据集上超越了先前的反事实生成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度伪造检测器容易被新型伪造技术欺骗，且往往具有黑盒性质。Counter-DECEIVR 方案则通过使用现成的假信息检测器，无需训练生成器和调整检测器，即可生成有效的反事实例子，帮助检测器解释其决策过程，增强用户信任。

Method: Counter-DECEIVR 通过利用检测器的解释图谱，重点编辑显著区域并将低级特征的重要性转化为局部化的反事实编辑。这种方法使得反事实生成变得更加精准和高效，依赖单一参数来控制编辑强度，避免了针对每实例或每检测器进行拟合以及扩散训练。

Result: 该方法在两个深度伪造检测器和两个广泛使用的纵深伪造取证数据集上，优于包括基于训练的方法在内的先前的反事实生成方法。

Conclusion: Counter-DECEIVR 提供了一个创新性的框架来对抗深度伪造检测器的欺骗，并通过可解释的方式增强用户对检测器的信任，有望成为评估和改进深度伪造检测器的有效工具。

Abstract: Deep-learning-generated media are increasingly passed off as real, driving an arms race between image generators and deepfake detectors. Trust in the latter is limited by their often black-box nature; and, while region-based explanations can sometimes be extracted, these become less useful as generation quality improves. Counterfactual examples, which show the minimal change that flips a detector's decision, reveal what evidence drives a verdict, helping analysts decide whether to trust it. This work introduces Counter-DECEIVR (DEtector-guided reCtifiEd flow InVeRsion for counterfactuals), a novel training-free framework that generates counterfactual examples. Counter-DECEIVR steers latents in the velocity field of a rectified flow model using gradients from off-the-shelf deepfake detectors, without training a generator or adapting the detector; unlike prior methods, it requires no per-instance or per-detector fitting and no diffusion training on the target data. It also leverages the detector's explanation maps to concentrate editing to salient regions, turning low-level feature importance into localised counterfactual edits. A single parameter controls the strength of the edits. Across two deepfake detectors and two widely used forensics datasets, Counter-DECEIVR outperforms prior counterfactual generation methods, including training-based ones. Code will be made available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [110] [Hijacking 3D Generation: Targeted Adversarial Attacks against Image-to-3D Models via Prefix Trajectory Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34075)
*Yilong Yang,Jiale Teng,Yule Liu,Yang Liu,Xinlei He,Zhuo Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PTA3D是一种针对Image-to-3D模型的高级目标攻击方法，通过Prefix Trajectory Alignment (PTA)和Preprocessing Aware Optimization (PAO)解决了现有漏洞的挑战，使得攻击者能够更有效地生成违规的3D几何结构，尤其是在黑盒环境中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 攻击者利用已有的Image-to-3D服务生成含违反内容政策的3D模型，尽管服务使用了检查工具但仍然可能存在安全风险。

Method: PTA3D方法的核心在于利用Prefix Trajectory Alignment (PTA)和Preprocessing Aware Optimization (PAO)技术。PTA逐步对齐攻击轨迹的前置轨迹输出与目标轨迹的输出，以控制最终的几何结构而不优化完整生成轨迹；PAO则使对抗优化暴露于多种图像变换，减少对源服务与目标服务差异的敏感性。

Result: 在三个开源Image-to-3D模型和三个在线服务上的实验结果表明，PTA3D在开放环境中能够实现41.5%-88.5%的成功率，在白盒条件下成功率更是达到了90.8%-94.6%，显著优于现有攻击方法。

Conclusion: PTA3D作为一种新型针对Image-to-3D模型的攻击方法，有效地解决了之前的多项挑战，具备较高的攻击成功率，为该领域的安全防护提供了新的思考方向。

Abstract: Online services that deploy Image-to-3D models enable users to generate high-quality 3D assets from a single image. However, the generated 3D geometry can violate content policies and cause broader security risks. To prevent such content, these services typically use policy-restricted inspectors to examine uploaded images before generation. Since the inspector examines only the visible content of the input, a visually benign image can still induce the generation of policy-restricted 3D geometry. However, turning this vulnerability into an effective attack is non-trivial in the black-box setting: global backpropagation through the long DiT generation trajectory is difficult to fit, while different image preprocessing pipelines across services can substantially reduce transferability. In this paper, we present PTA3D, a targeted adversarial attack against Image-to-3D models that addresses these challenges through Prefix Trajectory Alignment (PTA) and Preprocessing Aware Optimization (PAO). PTA progressively aligns the prefix DiT outputs of the adversarial trajectory with those of the target trajectory, where the core geometric structure is formed, allowing the final geometry to be controlled without optimizing through the complete generation trajectory. PAO exposes adversarial optimization to diverse image transformations rather than a single preprocessing pipeline, reducing sensitivity to the mismatch between surrogate and target services. We establish a prefix-to-output bound and a preprocessing-aware transfer bound to theoretically characterize the effectiveness of the two designs. Experiments on three open-source Image-to-3D models and three online services show that PTA3D achieves 41.5%-88.5% ASR on online services and 90.8%-94.6% ASR in white box settings, substantially outperforming existing attacks.

</details>


### [111] [BayesPath: Reliability-Aware Bayesian Reasoning for Whole-Slide Pathology Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33935)
*Bryan Wong,Xun Xu,Jongwoo Kim,Huazhu Fu,Nancy F. Chen,Mun Yong Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BayesPath 是一个无需训练的全切片病理图像 (WSI) 代理，通过将病理学家的扫描-查看-评估-决定过程建模为带有可靠性的贝叶斯推理，从而提高诊断准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无需训练的 WSI 代理缺乏明确的推理框架，导致错误多次重复时仍作为独立的诊断证据。BayesPath 旨在通过引入可靠的贝叶斯推理来改善这一问题。

Method: BayesPath 使用带有可靠性的贝叶斯推理框架，通过更新与候选解决方案的显式信念来指导下一步观察并决定何时停止。算法包含可靠性校正的似然性因子，折扣相关观察，并捕捉未标注诊断中的混淆。

Result: 在五项 MultiPathQA 任务中，包括切片分类和切片 VQA，BayesPath 达到了最高的平均准确率 58.6％，其次是 34.1％。在相同的观察下，贝叶斯更新的性能优于大语言模型的聚合。

Conclusion: BayesPath 的信念分布也比朴素贝叶斯更准确，因此不确定的情况可以交给病理学家处理。该方法提供了一个可解释的证据链，表明每项观察如何影响信念的形成。

Abstract: Diagnosing whole-slide images (WSIs) requires hypothesis-driven reasoning: pathologists form a differential diagnosis, seek discriminative evidence, weigh its reliability, and stop once it suffices. However, existing training-free WSI agents lack an explicit framework for such reasoning. They typically use a large language model (LLM) to aggregate the region descriptions of a multimodal LLM (MLLM), without a belief over the candidates or regard for the descriptions' reliability. Consequently, a repeated model error counts as independent evidence for an incorrect diagnosis. We introduce BayesPath, a training-free WSI agent that casts the pathologist's Scan-Look-Weigh-Decide process as reliability-aware Bayesian reasoning. An explicit belief over the candidates is updated with each observation through a reliability-aware likelihood and guides where to look next and when to stop. This likelihood corrects for the models' answer bias, discounts correlated observations, and captures diagnostic confusions estimated without labels. The updates form an interpretable evidence trail of how each observation shapes the belief. On five MultiPathQA tasks spanning slide classification and slide VQA, BayesPath achieves the highest average accuracy among training-free agents, 58.6% against 34.1% for the next best, with large gains on slide classification. On identical observations, Bayesian updating outperforms LLM aggregation on average, even with a larger LLM. BayesPath's belief is also better calibrated than naive Bayes, so uncertain cases can be deferred to pathologists. Code is available at https://github.com/bryanwong17/BayesPath

</details>


### [112] [WorldBridge: Bridging Scattered Views for Coherent 3D World Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34079)
*Weilong Yan,Jingyu Hu,Hongli Xu,Yifan Zhan,Beibei Lin,Anbang Wang,Qinqian Lei,Jingwei Huang,Robby T. Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldBridge 提出了一种框架，用于从稀疏且视觉上不重叠的图像中生成一致且可控的3D世界。通过全局场景上下文编码和参考注意力机制，以及可插拔的潜空间参考一致性策略，方法能够生成高质量的3D世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的几何感知方法无法解决从少量或几乎没有视觉重叠的图像生成连贯3D世界的挑战。现有基于图像和视频的方法可能生成未见过的区域，但会跨视图产生不一致的几何结构。WorldBridge 提出了一种新框架，旨在通过全局场景上下文和参考注意力机制解决这些问题。

Method: WorldBridge 通过编码稀疏参考到全局场景上下文来引导跨视图的几何结构和外观生成。它包含时空参考注意力机制，以增强目标轨迹上的一致性并支持轨迹外的灵活空间参考。引入了一种插拔式的潜在空间参考一致性策略，通过回投影指导测试时优化以提升参考的一致性。此外，还创建了WorldBridge 基准套件，以评估从稀疏采样的低重叠参考资料生成3D世界的性能。

Result: 质性和量化结果表明，WorldBridge 方法能够超出现有最先进的解决方案，实现可控且连贯的3D世界生成。

Conclusion: WorldBridge 提供了一种有效的框架，能够从稀疏且视觉上不重叠的图像生成高质量的3D世界。所提出的基准套件为该领域的进一步研究提供了参考基准。

Abstract: Generating coherent and explorable 3D worlds from scattered images with little or no visual overlap is a fundamental and long-standing challenge in computer vision, which existing geometry-aware methods fail to address. Meanwhile, existing image- and video-based methods may generate unseen regions but produce inconsistent geometry across views. To bridge this gap, we present WorldBridge, a framework for coherent and controllable 3D world generation under scattered images with little or no visual overlap. WorldBridge encodes scattered references into a global scene context to guide geometry and appearance generation across views. Our temporal and spatial reference attention mechanism further enhances the fidelity of temporal references along the target trajectory and supports flexible spatial references outside it. A plug-and-play latent-reference consistency strategy improves reference fidelity through reprojection-guided test-time optimization. We also introduce the WorldBridge benchmark suite to evaluate 3D world generation from scattered, low-overlap references, which is rarely explored by current benchmarks. Both qualitative and quantitative results demonstrate that our method enables controllable and coherent 3D world generation, surpassing the existing state-of-the-art solutions. Our project page is available at https://davidyan2001.github.io/WorldBridge/.

</details>


### [113] [TAT-Det: A Dataset for Testicular Appendage Torsion Detection and Segmentation in Ultrasound Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33940)
*Ying Jiang,Juntao Jiang,Linxuan Fan,Lingling Li,Yaqi Wang,Xiuzhen Yang,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang,Jingjing Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了TAT-Det数据集，包含2600张睾丸附属蔓状静脉丛扭转（TAT）病人的超声图像，用于TAT检测和细粒度边界描绘，为儿科急救超声分析提供数据支持，并评估了YOLO11m和RWKV-UNet在检测和分割任务中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏用于急性睾丸疼痛诊断的标准化数据集，尤其是对于睾丸附属蔓状静脉丛扭转（TAT）的诊断。因此，研究团队创建了TAT-Det数据集，旨在改善目前缺乏的数据资源，提高儿科急诊超声分析的准确性。

Method: 该数据集包含2600张超声图像，由3名独立的儿科超声诊断专家进行注解，并通过多阶段流程进行确认和修正。基于此数据集，研究团队使用了YOLO11m和RWKV-UNet等代表性的深度学习模型来搭建基线模型，分别评估了检测和分割任务的性能。

Result: YOLO11m在超声图像物体检测中表现最佳，RWKV-UNet在像素级分割任务中表现出色。

Conclusion: TAT-Det数据集的建立为儿科急诊超声自动化分析提供了基础，旨在推动相关人工智能方法的发展和评估。

Abstract: Background: Testicular appendage torsion (TAT) is the leading cause of acute scrotal pain in prepubertal children. Yet, its diagnosis remains challenging due to nonspecific clinical manifestations and the absence of standardized datasets for pediatric acute scrotal emergencies.
  Results: We present TAT-Det, an expert-annotated ultrasound dataset specifically designed for TAT. TAT-Det comprises 2,600 ultrasound images from 1,203 patients collected at the Children's Hospital, Zhejiang University School of Medicine. Each TAT-positive image is accompanied by lesion-level bounding boxes for detection and pixel-level segmentation masks for fine-grained delineation. Annotations were produced through a multi-stage workflow involving independent annotation by board-certified pediatric sonographers, consensus review, and refinement. Baseline benchmarks for object detection and semantic segmentation using representative deep learning models are further provided, with YOLO11m achieving the best detection performance and RWKV-UNet the best segmentation performance.
  Conclusions: TAT-Det addresses the lack of publicly available resources for pediatric emergency ultrasound and establishes a foundation for developing and evaluating artificial intelligence methods for automated TAT analysis.

</details>


### [114] [A Computational Model of Continuous Recognition Memory for Natural Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33947)
*Sheng Zhao,Yijin Wang,Bei Xiao,Yuhao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种计算模型来模拟人类连续识别记忆，该模型通过感知空间中的随机变量编码每个图像，并通过其分布的变化来判断图像是否被再次识别，实现了对人类观察者在识别任务中的试次响应的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解人类连续识别记忆有助于机器视觉系统预测人们可能记住哪些图像，并生成更具记忆性的图像。此外，它还可以用于诊断记忆障碍并开发改善记忆的干预措施。

Method: 该模型通过将每个图像编码为感知空间中的一个独立随机变量来模拟连续识别记忆。随着记忆的消退，随机变量在其空间内的分布发生变化。模型通过从以前编码图像的分布中抽取样本并将它们与当前图像进行比较来决定当前图像是否已被识别。

Result: 模型能够在记忆任务中准确预测人类观察者的试次反应，预测效果优于参数量在百万级别的先前方法。

Conclusion: 该模型成功地模仿了人类连续识别记忆的能力，具有参数效率高、预测能力强等特点，为理解人类记忆提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Recognizing an image we have seen before is a basic function of human visual memory. Understanding human continuous recognition memory (CRM) can help machine vision systems predict which images people are likely to remember and generate more memorable images. It can also inform the diagnosis of memory impairments and the development of interventions to improve memory. This paper proposes the first computational model for CRM. The model captures three key components of continuous recognition memory (CRM): image encoding, memory decay, and decision making. It encodes each image as a distinct random variable in a perceptual space. As the memory of an image fades, the distribution of its random variable drifts within that space. To decide whether the current image has been seen before, the observer retrieves samples from the distributions associated with previously encoded images and compares them with the current image. Our model predicts human observers' trial-by-trial responses in CRM tasks within the variability observed across human participants. Our model is parameter efficient, requiring only four shared parameters and two parameters per observer. It can accurately predict the memorability of an image no worse than prior methods that require millions of parameters.

</details>


### [115] [FoundSurface: Feedforward Ray-Based 3D Scene Surface Reconstruction from Unposed Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34128)
*Shenxing Wei,Jinxi Li,Zihui Zhang,Ajay Kumar,Wei Wang,Bo Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新的管道，可以直接从未校准图像中恢复连续且高保真的3D表面，通过结合点图表示和射线表示，提高了几何精度和细节保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的端到端点图方法虽然规避了相机姿态的需求，但产生的是离散式的稀疏表示，不足以满足对连续、高保真3D表面的需求。

Method: 论文提出了一种通用管道，首先使用预训练的点图模型提取显式几何先验，然后通过鲁棒的片段投票机制选择几何相关的参考视图，接着使用跨注意力回归器连接目标查询射线和参考特征以估计连续的粗表面，最后使用射线束模块聚合局部3D特征，提取高频细节和分析性法线。

Result: 该方法在一个包含9个不同数据集的大型数据集上进行了训练，并展示了出色的零样本泛化能力，在6个未见的数据集上均超越了现有最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 整体来看，该工作对3D表面重建领域的贡献在于提出了一种新颖有效的基于点图和射线相结合的方法，显著提升了重建质量。

Abstract: Accurate 3D surface reconstruction from unposed images remains a fundamental challenge in computer vision. Recent feed-forward pointmap methods eliminate the need for camera poses but inherently produce discrete, sparse representations. In this paper, we present a generalizable pipeline that directly recovers continuous, high-fidelity 3D surfaces from unposed images by seamlessly integrating pointmap- and ray-based representations. Our approach first extracts explicit geometric priors using a pre-trained pointmap model. A query-view-centric selection module then identifies geometrically relevant reference views via a robust surfel voting mechanism. Next, a cross-attention regressor bridges target query rays with reference features to estimate a continuous coarse surface. Lastly, a raylet-based module aggregates local 3D features to carve out high-frequency details and analytical normals. Trained as a single foundation model on 9 diverse datasets, our method demonstrates exceptional zero-shot generalization on 6 unseen datasets, clearly surpassing state-of-the-art baselines in geometric accuracy and detail preservation.

</details>


### [116] [Where to Approximate: Understanding and Accelerating Looped Multi-View Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33948)
*Siyuan Zhang,Yuhan Chen,Yangyang Ning,Nan Wang,Jun Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过在Déjà View 和堆叠重建变换器中使用配对干预，研究了近似误差的影响，提出了一个新的高效多视图重建方法，该方法在重建过程的早期降低空间分辨率并提前聚合键和值，交替使用注意力-输出重用和全注意力，最后通过轻量级自我校准进行调整。该方法在五个重建基准测试中比标准16步Déjà View模型快1.63倍，且具有更优的几何精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高多视图重建的效率而不牺牲重建质量，特别是在需要实时处理的场景中。

Method: 该方法专注于研究近似误差如何影响重建过程，并基于此开发了一种新的多视图重建策略。首先，通过降低分辨率和提前聚合键和值来优化计算过程，其次，通过交替使用注意力-输出重用和全注意力方式来动态调整计算策略，最后通过轻量级自我校准来进行最终优化。

Result: 实验结果显示，该方法在五个多视图重建基准测试中，相对于官方16步Déjà View模型提高了1.86倍的平均姿态AUC@°̄3，并且在匹配延迟的情况下，该方法超越了一个使用完整重构块训练的基础模型4.73点的AUC@°̄3，同时提高了相应的几何精度指标。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的方法通过减少计算开销和优化信息流，使得多视图重建过程更高效，同时保持并提升了重建质量，在实际应用中具有重要的价值。

Abstract: Looped multi-view reconstruction refines scene features by repeatedly applying a shared transformer block. Making these updates cheaper requires knowing how an approximation at one step affects the rest of the refinement. We study this with paired interventions in Déjà View and stacked reconstruction transformers, separating the error an approximation injects, how that error propagates, and how it affects the output. In Déjà View, approximation errors are attenuated more strongly than matched random perturbations under a scale-adjusted state-error measure, and a local Jacobian decomposition separates the contribution of channel gating from the response to spatial error structure. Spatial approximations made early benefit from subsequent refinement, whereas cross-step reuse depends on which quantity is cached and how reuse steps are spaced. At equal final norm, approximation errors change the predicted rotations 1.55 times as much as random errors. These findings guide our proposed method, which reduces spatial resolution and pools keys and values early in refinement, then interleaves attention-output reuse with full attention, and is adapted through lightweight self-distillation. Across five reconstruction benchmarks, the balanced configuration runs $1.63\times$ faster than the official 16-step Déjà View model with a 1.86-point reduction in average pose AUC@$3^\circ$. At matched latency, it exceeds the interpolated curve of a trained baseline using full refinement blocks by 4.73 points in AUC@$3^\circ$ and improves the corresponding geometry metrics.

</details>


### [117] [Lost in the Grid: Where Multimodal LLMs Fail Between Seeing and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34152)
*Luoxi Zhang,Tianyi Tan,Jiawei Liu,Xingyu Zeng,Min Yang,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GridProbe 是一个诊断基准，能够确定失败发生在视觉感知还是规划过程中，并通过针对特定阶段的微调进行验证。该基准应用于Sokoban和迷宫游戏，揭示了性能差异的根本原因，并展示了优化视觉模块可以显著提升模型在未见过的任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要报告端到端的成功率，但隐藏了失败的具体原因，是视觉感知、规则应用还是多步规划。GridProbe 目的是通过引入特定阶段的探测器来解决这一问题。

Method: GridProbe 通过添加额外的探测器（识别、定位、单步预测、状态跟踪）到端到端的解题器中，对Sokoban和迷宫游戏进行评估，以确定模型在哪个阶段遇到瓶颈，并通过针对性的微调来验证诊断结果。

Result: 研究发现，专有模型的主要限制在于多步规划，而开源模型主要受限于规则应用或视觉网格索引，即行和列的计数。在视觉受限模型上仅微调视觉模块可以将交互式Sokoban成功率提高3.5倍，并且可以零样本迁移至未见过的迷宫游戏。

Conclusion: GridProbe 的结果表明了在视觉感知和规划阶段的具体限制因素，并提供了一个可用于识别和提高模型性能的基准方法。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel at static visual understanding and are increasingly applied to spatial planning, where a model interprets a visual layout and outputs an action sequence to reach a goal. Grid games are a common testbed for this ability because their rules are explicit and a solver can verify every move. Yet existing benchmarks mostly report end-to-end success rates, which hide whether a failure stems from visual perception, rule application, or multi-step planning. We introduce GridProbe, a diagnostic benchmark that 1) locates the failing stage between visual perception and planning and 2) verifies the diagnosis by targeted fine-tuning. On Sokoban, GridProbe pairs end-to-end solving with four probes (recognition, localization, single-step prediction, and state tracking) and gives every board both as an image and as an equivalent text grid. Evaluating seven MLLMs on Sokoban and a maze game reveals distinct bottlenecks behind similar success rates: proprietary models are limited by multi-step planning, whereas open-source models are limited by rule application or by visual grid indexing, i.e., counting rows and columns. Planning failures also hide perception failures: when planning is easier, the visual cost of the same model grows from 7.6 to 37.7 points. The diagnosis identifies what to train: fine-tuning only the vision module of the perception-limited model on board reading raises its interactive Sokoban success 3.5-fold (5.8% to 20.4%) and transfers zero-shot to the unseen maze game (single-turn success 13.8% to 67.1%), whereas the same data does not help a rule-limited model and training on solver solutions brings no gain. We will release the benchmark and code at https://github.com/suat-llm/GridProbe.

</details>


### [118] [Where Does Free-Text 3D Grounding Fail? An Error Analysis of Report-Sentence Segmentation in Chest CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33958)
*Abdullah Arif Sheikh,Kamalaker Dadi,Raju S. Bapi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 当前3D标注模型在肺部CT报告实体定位上表现欠佳，主要问题是预测区域过大，临近病灶误判以及小病灶和弥漫病灶难以准确定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析肺部CT报告中自由文本实体的3D定位模型表现，以发现当前模型的弱项和改进方向。

Method: 通过分析公开发布的VoxTell模型在ReXGroundingCT验证集上的预测结果。

Result: 模型成功率较低，主要错误类型包括目标过大、临近病灶误判、小病灶和弥漫性病灶难以准确定位。

Conclusion: 进一步改进需要集中在病灶大小估计和小病灶敏感性上，而非依赖于解剖后处理。

Abstract: Free-text 3D grounding, segmenting in a CT volume the region described by one sentence of the radiology report, could link reports to images at scale, but it is unclear where current models fail. We analyse the publicly released VoxTell model on all 381 findings of the 200-scan ReXGroundingCT validation set, where it grounds 53.5% of findings. Its failures are rarely about which lesion or side is meant. No miss lies on the side opposite to the one named, and findings are almost never confused with one another. Instead, predictions are too large, land on a neighbouring lesion on the correct side, or are missing for small findings, and diffuse findings are rarely grounded. Removing location words halves performance, to the level of label-only prompting, and anatomical rules derived from the sentence never improve the full-sentence output. The same pattern holds on the hidden 300-scan test set. Further progress therefore depends on extent estimation and small-lesion sensitivity rather than on anatomical post-processing.

</details>


### [119] [Beyond Logits: Coherent Hallucination Mitigation via Attention Contrastive Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34207)
*Yujia Chen,Rui Sun,Huayu Mai,Wangkai Li,Zhangyu He,Bingzhou Wang,Aibing Li,Wenzhang Sun,Tianzhu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Attention Contrastive Decoding (ACD)的方法，它通过将部分对比操作移至注意机制来缓解幻觉生成问题，并通过自适应减法策略（ASS）在保持语义平滑的同时减少幻觉，从而在不施加限制性惩罚的情况下实现更可靠和连贯的多模态生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Contrastive Decoding (CD)方法虽然能减轻模型的幻觉现象，但同时也破坏了生成内容的连贯性，因此需要额外不可靠的惩罚来抑制幻觉，这限制了模型的生成能力。

Method: ACD方法通过将对比操作从logit级转移到注意力机制中，并引入了自适应减法策略（ASS），根据注意力图来选择性地减少幻觉相关模式，同时增强关键视觉信息的权重，从而实现更为连贯的多模态生成。

Result: 实验结果表明，将ACD与现有的CD方法结合（例如VCD+ACD），可以显著提高生成内容的连贯性，减少幻觉现象，无需施加限制性惩罚，从而使得模型的生成更为可信。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法在不破坏生成连贯性的前提下有效减轻了LVLM的幻觉问题，并在实际应用中表现出更好的连贯性和可靠性，为提高多模态模型的生成质量提供了新途径。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) demonstrate impressive multimodal capabilities, yet suffer from hallucination - generating factually inaccurate content. Contrastive Decoding (CD) mitigates this by contrasting amateur and expert branches at the logit level. However, our investigation reveals that such logit-level interventions fundamentally compromise generation coherence, necessitating restrictive penalty constraints unrelated to hallucination suppression.
  We introduce Attention Contrastive Decoding (ACD), a training-free plug-in that complements logit-level CD by relocating part of the contrastive operations to the attention mechanism. Operating at an earlier stage of the forward pass, ACD performs smooth semantic-preserving interventions through an Adaptive Subtraction Strategy (ASS), which attenuates hallucination-associated attention patterns while amplifying critical visual information. Extensive experiments demonstrate that combining ACD with existing CD methods (e.g., VCD+ACD) produces substantially more coherent outputs with further reduced hallucinations, eliminating restrictive penalties while enabling trustworthy multimodal generation.

</details>


### [120] [GeoCAM: Self-Supervised Sonar-Optical Representation Learning via Geographic Overlap Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34218)
*Fadi Abdeladhim Zidi,Andrea De Salve,Giuseppe Celeste,Abdellah Zakaria Sellam,Paolo Spagnolo,Cosimo Distante*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoCAM 提出了一种用于侧扫声纳和水下光学成像配对数据的地理导向自监督学习框架，通过集合注意力聚合器、几何加权 InfoNCE 和跨模态掩码重建分支等组件，有效提高了跨模态检索和对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法充分利用实际声光调查中的地理元数据和一对多对应关系，限制了从大量未标记的海洋数据中学习既有意义又几何一致的表示。GeoCAM 旨在通过地理导向的方式解决这一问题。

Method: GeoCAM 包含三个主要组成部分：集合注意力聚合器对多个光学观察进行自适应汇总，形成单个声纳图样级表示；几何加权 InfoNCE 目标将 GPS 获取的足迹重叠转化为柔软的正监督，鼓励物理约束下的跨模态对齐；可选的跨模态掩码重建分支进一步增强跨境模式一致性。

Result: 在 BenthiCat SSS-CAM 数据集上的实验表明，GeoCAM 能有效用于识别描述同一海底区域的声纳和相机样本，相比强对比度基准提升了跨模态检索和对齐效果。

Conclusion: GeoCAM 为声纳和光学成像跨模态无监督学习提供了新的方案，适用于海洋底栖环境的理解。

Abstract: Cross-modal self-supervised learning for paired side-scan sonar and underwater optical imagery is a promising direction for benthic habitat understanding, but it remains underexplored because existing methods do not fully exploit the one-to-many correspondences and geographic metadata available in real opti-acoustic surveys. This limits the ability to learn representations that are both semantically meaningful and geometrically consistent from abundant unlabeled marine data. To address this problem, we present GeoCAM, a geography-guided framework that learns a shared embedding space for sonar tiles and their associated optical views without semantic annotations. GeoCAM achieves its goal by combining three components: (i) a Set Attention Aggregator that adaptively summarizes multiple optical observations into a single tile-level representation; (ii) a geometry-weighted InfoNCE objective that converts GPS-derived footprint overlap into soft positive supervision; and (iii) an optional cross-modal masked reconstruction branch that encourages physically grounded cross-modal alignment. Experiments on the BenthiCat SSS-CAM dataset show that GeoCAM can be effectively exploited to learn which sonar and camera samples describe the same seabed patch, improving cross-modal retrieval and alignment over strong contrastive baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/FadiZidiDz/GeoCAM.

</details>


### [121] [FasterGRN: Reducing the Refinement Redundancy for Accelerating Generative Refinement Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33982)
*Ziqian Zhang,Pengcheng Wang,Yulong Chen,Yaxing Wang,Senmao Li,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究通过分析生成过程的特性提出了一种名为FasterGRN的无训练加速方法，该方法通过对GRN网络进行阶段划分并采用Adaptive Retention Expansion (ARE) 和 Selective Token Freezing (STF) 策略，实现了图像生成速度提升2.03倍，同时保持甚至提高了图像保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了加速Generative Refinement Network (GRN)，因为其需要对整个视觉表示进行迭代处理，导致了巨大的计算负担。

Method: 通过分析GRN的生成过程，观察了阶段特性、双分支相似性及关键的精炼冗余特性，提出了FasterGRN加速方法，该方法首先将GRN划分为语义确立阶段和细节精炼阶段，并通过Adaptive Retention Expansion (ARE) 和 Selective Token Freezing (STF) 优化策略减少了精炼步骤和节省了计算量。

Result: 在GenEval和DPG-Bench基准测试中，FasterGRN实现了图像生成速度提升2.03倍，且在COCO数据集上的测试显示其在图像保真度方面甚至超过了原始的GRN模型。

Conclusion: FasterGRN提供了一种有效的加速路径，适用于需要高保真度图像生成的场景，其在满足高效同时保持高质量方面具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Departing from the paradigm of diffusion and autoregressive models, Generative Refinement Networks (GRN) enables high-quality image generation through iterative refinements. However, this repeated processing over the entire visual representations results in substantial computation overhead. Furthermore, the binary representations and global refinement mechanism in GRN disable the direct application of acceleration strategies developed for other generative models. To accelerate GRN model, we first observe several key properties by analyzing the entire generative process. That includes the stage characteristic, dual branch similarity and more importantly, the $\textbf{refinement redundancy}$. Motivated by the observations, we present $\textbf{FasterGRN}$, a training-free acceleration method for the Generative Refinement Network. $\textbf{FasterGRN}$ first divides the GRN into the semantic establishment stage and detail refinement stage according to its semantic establishment. For alleviating the refinement redundancy mainly resulting from the detail refinement stage, we propose Adaptive Retention Expansion ($\textbf{ARE}$) and Selective Token Freezing ($\textbf{STF}$) to shrink refinement steps and reduce token computations, respectively. In the experiments on GenEval and DPG-Bench benchmarks, our method $\textbf{FasterGRN}$ achieves a $2.03\times$ sampling speedup while largely preserving image generation performance. Our evaluation on COCO datasets additionally demonstrates that $\textbf{FasterGRN}$ even outperforms the original GRN in image fidelity.

</details>


### [122] [StateWeaver: Multi-Agent Coordination of Story-State Transitions for Long-Horizon Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34290)
*Modi Jin,Xuying Zhang,Yijing Guo,Haoran Li,Yanwen Ma,Jing Wang,Chenyu Ge,Ming-Ming Cheng,Haoyang Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StateWeaver结合主动状态和分层协作，改进了视频生成的一致性和事件驱动状态变化的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频生成模型在保持长视频一致性和正确描绘事件驱动的状态变化方面存在不足。StateWeaver旨在解决这些问题。

Method: StateWeaver通过引入主动状态和分层协作来连接审查驱动的视觉参考更新与跨剪辑状态继承。主动状态将审查中识别的状态差异转化为具体的视觉资产需求，指导参考构建、验证和再生，同时保持身份和已正确的内容，并修复目标属性。分层协作通过任务、剪辑和修复轮次隔离执行环境，并通过分层记忆组织这一过程。

Result: StateWeaver在多个基准测试中优于现有方法，能够在预定义和自主规划的故事板下生成具有持续跨剪辑一致性的长视频。

Conclusion: StateWeaver通过主动状态和分层协作显著提高了生成长视频时的一致性和事件驱动状态变化的正确性。

Abstract: Modern video foundation models generate high-quality short clips, but preserving long-video consistency and correctly depicting event-driven state changes remain challenging. Existing agentic workflows face these challenges because continual planning and repair accumulate context, while reference updates remain limited. We introduce StateWeaver, which combines Active States and Hierarchical Collaboration to connect review-driven visual reference updates with cross-shot state inheritance. Active States translate state discrepancies identified during review into concrete visual asset requirements that guide reference construction, validation, and regeneration, preserving identity and already-correct content while repairing target attributes. Confirmed generation results are linked to the corresponding asset states, story time, and reference versions for verification and reuse by subsequent shots. Hierarchical Collaboration isolates execution contexts by task, clip, and repair round, and uses hierarchical memory to organize this process across multiple sets. Across multiple benchmarks, StateWeaver outperforms existing methods and produces long videos with sustained cross-shot consistency under predefined and autonomously planned storyboards.

</details>


### [123] [geo-eval-harness: An Evaluation Harness for Geospatial Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33990)
*Nils Lehmann,Caleb Robinson,Isaac Corley,Jennifer Marcus,Inbal Becker-Reshef,Juan M. Lavista Ferres,Hannah Kerner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个公开的框架 geo-eval-harness，用于控制模型比较。作者评估了100个GFMs和通用视觉模型在多种数据集上的性能，并展示了不同模型在特定任务下的表现差异。此外，作者发现更大的模型并不总是更好的选择，并且翻译推理计算到大面积地图工作负载时，小的准确率差异可能导致巨大的部署成本差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GFMs研究受到不一致评估设置的困扰，导致难以区分模型性能与评估选择之间的关系。为此，研究者提出了geo-eval-harness框架，以提供一个可控、可复制的评估环境，从而增强评估结果的公正性和一致性。

Method: 研究者通过使用geo-eval-harness框架，对100个GFMs与通用视觉模型进行了一系列控制实验。实验覆盖了多个分类和分割数据集，并使用了多种探针和固定的编码器来进行评估。

Result: 实验结果显示，通用视觉骨干网络在RGB分类和高分辨率分割任务中表现良好，但在多光谱任务中GFMs表现出色。此外，研究发现模型大小与分割性能的相关性较弱，并且小的准确率差异可能导致较大的部署成本差异。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在明确且固定设置下进行模型比较的重要性，并建议报告准确率之外的不确定性以及部署计算资源。geo-eval-harness框架简化了可控制实验的过程，使其变得容易且可完全复现，研究者已在GitHub上发布了代码、配置参数和实验结果。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation model (GFM) research papers are plagued by inconsistent evaluation settings (e.g., different pre-processing and probes), making it difficult to separate performance from evaluation choices. We propose geo-eval-harness}, an open-source framework for controlled model comparisons. We use this harness to evaluate 100 GFMs and general vision models on 6 classification and 14 segmentation datasets, using multiple probes on frozen or low-rank-adapted encoders. We show that under controlled evaluation, general vision backbones are competitive on RGB classification and high-resolution segmentation, but GFMs shine on multispectral tasks. When we compare any two of the top-ten classification models on a single dataset, their 95% confidence intervals overlap 73% of the time. We show that bigger models are not always better: inference FLOPs correlate weakly with segmentation performance. Moreover, translating inference compute into large-area mapping workloads shows how small accuracy differences have outsized deployment cost differences: with frozen encoders on SpaceNet2, a 0.9-point mIoU difference corresponds to 5.9x the compute and days of additional projected GPU time. Our findings stress the importance of comparing models under explicit and fixed settings, and reporting uncertainty and deployment compute alongside accuracy. Our geo-eval-harness makes controlled experiments easy, fast, and fully reproducible. We release the code, configurations, and results at https://github.com/torchgeo/geo-eval-harness.

</details>


### [124] [WebVibe: Multimodal Interactive Website Generation via Reverse Synthesis and Visual Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34292)
*Tianxiang Jiang,Sheng Xia,Yilin Long,Kanghui Tian,Linquan Wu,Yongheng Zhang,Daixian Liu,Di Yin,Yi Wang,Yu Qiao,Qinglong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了WebVibe和Interactive-SDPO来解决多页路由和状态化组件过渡的问题，通过反转合成过程和使用回放确认状态生成WebVibe-32K语料库，并引入Interactive-SDPO为InteractiveCoder提供零推理成本的标记级监督，显著提高了生成交互式网站的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在处理多页路由和状态化组件过渡时存在瓶颈，本文旨在解决这些问题，提高生成交互式网站的能力。

Method: 提出了WebVibe和Interactive-SDPO两个新方法。WebVibe通过浏览器验证跨页面和页面内交互，在回放确认状态下生成多模态输入以构建语料库。Interactive-SDPO通过条件回放轨迹和状态转换蒙太奇提供标记级监督。

Result: 通过使用WebVibe和Interactive-SDPO，InteractiveCoder在公共Vision2Web L2数据集的单次运行中几乎提高了三倍的功能得分，并在WebVibeBench基准测试中大幅超越所有基线。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法在生成交互式网站方面取得了显著的改进，展示了其在多态交互中的优势。

Abstract: Although multimodal large language models translate single screenshots into static HTML, generating interactive functional websites from design specifications still struggles with multi-page routing and stateful widget transitions. Examining existing training pipelines reveals two bottlenecks: (1) in forward data synthesis (X -> Y), writing the specification X before code execution leaves unimplemented or altered requirements in X as supervision noise; and (2) in reinforcement learning, scalar rewards discard the rich state transitions observed during replay. Based on these findings, we introduce WebVibe and Interactive-SDPO to train InteractiveCoder. WebVibe reverses synthesis into Y -> X by verifying cross-page and in-page interactions in the browser before deriving multimodal inputs from replay-confirmed states to build the 31,542-site WebVibe-32K corpus with reusable execution contracts. Interactive-SDPO then conditions a weight-sharing self-teacher on the replay trace and a 2x2 state-transition montage to provide token-level supervision at zero inference-time overhead. On the public Vision2Web L2, one-shot InteractiveCoder-9B nearly 3x its base model's functional score (20.2 to 55.2 FS) and matches a 1T-parameter coding agent in a single pass with 111x fewer total parameters and 78x fewer tokens. On our WebVibeBench, InteractiveCoder-4B and 9B further outperform all evaluated baselines by over 23 points.

</details>


### [125] [ScaleCurve: From Constant Scale to Consistent Motion for World-Space Human Mesh Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34005)
*Sanghyeon Lee,Jong Taek Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScaleCurve 是一种用于估计人体与地面几何关系的时间变化度量校正方法，通过优化不一致的观测值并耦合相邻的尺度估计，使校准可以沿摄像机路径自适应地调整，而不自由地跟随测量噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决人类网格恢复中出现的空间一致性问题，尤其是在相机空间人体姿态估计准确的情况下，仍可能丢失全局和局部尺度一致性。

Method: ScaleCurve 通过比较预测的足部几何形状与局部拟合的地面平面来构建尺度观察值，并利用关节时序优化减少不一致的观测值，同时耦合相邻的尺度估计，从而实现摄像机路径上的自适应校准。

Result: ScaleCurve 在 EMDB-2 和 SLOPER4D 数据集上达到了最先进的性能，且在三种不同的人体姿态估计模型（HMR backbone）上表现出一致的改进效果。

Conclusion: ScaleCurve 提供了一种无监督的方法来估计摄像机路径上的度量校正值，适用于多种人体姿态估计模型，无需重新训练。

Abstract: World-space human mesh recovery can lose spatial consistency even when camera-space body estimates are accurate. A single sequence-wide scale resolves global scale ambiguity but cannot correct local scale distortion along a reconstructed camera path. We present ScaleCurve, a framework that estimates time-varying metric corrections from human-ground geometry. We construct scale observations by comparing predicted foot geometry with locally fitted ground planes. Joint temporal optimization downweights inconsistent observations and couples neighboring scale estimates, allowing calibration to adapt along the camera path without freely following measurement noise. We scale camera translation increments and integrate them into a calibrated trajectory. With this camera fixed, we refine the lifted body motion using 2D image evidence and pretrained 3D dynamics, while restricting global trajectory rescaling. ScaleCurve achieves state-of-the-art performance on most evaluated metrics on both EMDB-2 and SLOPER4D. Consistent gains across three HMR backbones demonstrate its compatibility with diverse body estimates without retraining. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [126] [Manga Page Parser: Comprehensive Visual Parsing through Local Story Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34006)
*Yingxuan Li,Jiafeng Mao,Arata Furukawa,Mayu Otani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为MangaParser的框架，能够独立分析漫画跨页画面，通过结合人物聚类、讲话者鉴别和讲话者与角色绑定等方式，形成可复用的结构化故事表示，且无需额外训练或微调。实验证明该解析器在多个骨干网络上优于专门针对漫画的模型。并且，这种统一的表示方法能够支持故事问答，为漫画翻译、内容检索和问答奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的漫画解析方法往往依赖于人为标注的数据，难以处理复杂的叙事结构，而MangaParser框架能够提供一个统一的、可复用的故事表示，从而提升漫画的理解和应用。

Method: MangaParser框架通过结合人物聚类、说话者鉴别和说话者与角色绑定等多模态对话，在共享的结构化故事记忆引导下，进行无额外训练或微调的联合推理。

Result: 实验结果显示，MangaParser在多个骨干网络上的表现优于专门针对漫画的模型，且提出的统一表示方法能够支持故事问答，为后续的漫画翻译和内容检索等应用提供了基础。

Conclusion: MangaParser提供了一种新的漫画解析框架，通过多模态对话的方式实现了无需额外训练或微调的联合推理，并展示了该方法在漫画理解和应用方面的重要潜力。

Abstract: Understanding manga requires connecting who appears and who speaks with the events, character states, and relationships that unfold across panels. We study manga visual parsing through local story understanding: analyzing each double-page spread independently to capture and organize its story information in a reusable structured representation. We propose MangaParser, an agentic reading framework that jointly performs character clustering, speaker diarization, and speaker-to-character binding within a shared multimodal conversation, without additional training or fine-tuning. The agent uses structured story memory to guide character and speaker identification, inspecting relevant image regions when uncertain and refining its predictions and story records together as evidence accumulates. Quantitative evaluations show that our parser substantially outperforms manga-specific models across multiple backbones. Parsing examples illustrate how a unified representation links the character and speaker predictions with narrative content and supporting evidence. We further test reusability through story question answering using outputs generated before the questions are provided. The results confirm output reusability, offering a foundation for manga translation, content retrieval, and question answering.

</details>


### [127] [Rethinking Unsupervised Cross-Modal Homography Estimation:Spatially Aware Representations and Geometric Consistency Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34021)
*Zhuoran Yang,Junchen Yu,Runmin Zhang,Linshan Li,Chenghao Zhang,Hui-liang Shen,Si-Yuan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoCo 提出了一种无需交叉模态变换标签的几何感知一致性约束的单视角透视图估计框架，在四个数据集上显著降低了平均关键点对误差，GeoCo-D 进一步减少了参数和计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无监督跨模态透视图估计难题，通过分离模态迁移和透视图估计，结合几何感知和一致性约束提高准确对齐。

Method: GeoCo 使用 GeoCo-D 两阶段渐进蒸馏方法，首先通过一个中间模型学习跨模态匹配而无需模态迁移，然后转移预测和特征知识到学生模型，避免了实时提取视知觉基础模型。

Result: 在 GoogleMap、OPT-SAR、FSD 和 VIS-IR 数据集上，GeoCo 的均值平均角误差比最佳基线分别降低了 17.4%-46.7%，GeoCo-D 在使用 98.5% 更少参数和 70.4% 更少 FLOPs 的情况下仍优于所有对比基准。

Conclusion: GeoCo 和 GeoCo-D 通过改进几何表示和一致性约束，在无监督场景透视图估计中取得了显著进展。

Abstract: Unsupervised cross-modal homography estimation is challenging because appearance differences obscure shared geometric structures. Separating modality transfer from homography estimation helps reduce these differences, but accurate alignment still requires effective geometric representations and consistency constraints on the two subtasks. We propose GeoCo (Geometry-Aware and Consistency-Constrained Homography Estimation), a framework that addresses these two requirements without cross-modal transformation labels. Its homography estimator, RVHomo, adapts hierarchical features from a frozen vision foundation model (VFM) and fuses them with local CNN features to capture structural context and spatial detail. Two geometric consistency constraints encourage modality transfer to preserve geometry and homography predictions to remain consistent under synthetic geometric transformations. Through two-stage progressive distillation, we further obtain GeoCo-D, which uses the lightweight RVHomo-D estimator. An intermediate model first learns to perform cross-modal matching without modality transfer, then transfers prediction and feature knowledge to the student, eliminating online VFM extraction. Experiments on GoogleMap, OPT-SAR, FSD, and VIS-IR show that GeoCo reduces mean average corner error by 17.4-46.7% relative to the best-performing baseline on each dataset. GeoCo-D also outperforms all compared baselines while using 98.5% fewer parameters and 70.4% fewer FLOPs than the complete GeoCo teacher.

</details>


### [128] [SemProxy: Semantic 3D Proxies for Controllable Agentic 3D Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34414)
*Shuyuan Zhang,Changjian Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 文章介绍了一种名为SemProxy的轻量级中间表示，它使用户能够直接指定物体部分和空间布局，同时将几何细节留给编码代理处理。该研究开发了ProxyStudio用户界面，将文本、图像参考和交互式3D代理编辑整合到代理建模工作流中，通过用户研究评估了SemProxy在支持可控的人-代理协作3D建模方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的3D建模工作流主要依赖于文本提示和参考图像，难以精确传达空间意图并且需要多次修正。SemProxy通过提供一种直观的方式让用户直接指定物体部分和空间布局，促进人类与编码代理之间的有效交互。

Method: 该方法基于3D有标签边界框的语义3D代理，使其可以直接被用于现有编码代理而无需额外的训练或修改。

Result: 研究展示SemProxy在不同编码代理下的物体和场景级别的建模任务中的灵活性，并通过用户研究评估了其在支持可控的人-代理协作3D建模中的效果。

Conclusion: 文章得出结论，SemProxy作为一种轻量且直观的中间表示，有效提高了用户与编码代理系统的交互效率，并且表明人-代理合作在3D建模任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly capable of generating 3D models, yet enabling users to effectively control the modeling process remains challenging. Existing workflows rely primarily on text prompts and reference images, which often struggle to convey precise spatial intentions and require iterative corrections. We introduce SemProxy, a lightweight intermediate representation that bridges intuitive human interaction and agentic 3D modeling. Instantiated as labeled 3D bounding boxes, semantic 3D proxies allow users to directly specify object parts and spatial arrangements while leaving geometric details to coding agents. These proxies can be serialized into structured text and rendered as images, making them directly accessible to existing coding agents without additional training or modifications. We further develop ProxyStudio, a user interface that integrates text, image references, and interactive 3D proxy editing into agentic modeling workflows. We demonstrate the versatility of SemProxy across object- and scene-level modeling tasks with different coding agents, and evaluate its effectiveness in supporting controllable human-agent collaborative 3D modeling through a user study.

</details>


### [129] [MoSk: A Unified Motion-Skeleton Foundation Model for Human Motion Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34022)
*Yun Chen,Munchurl Kim,Jeonghyeok Do*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoSk 是一种新的基础模型，能够整合动作和骨架数据集，通过自我监督的掩蔽动作建模学习共享表示。MoSk 在多种设置中的动作识别表现强劲，并显著改善了跨多个基础模型的动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的人体动作理解与生成主要依赖于较小且通常是在受控环境中采集的骨骼数据集。MoSk 的引入旨在弥补这一不足，通过结合大规模动作数据和骨骼数据的优势，提供更广泛的动作表示，同时保持对动作识别任务的相关性。

Method: MoSk 通过自我监督的掩蔽动作建模学习共享表示，引入了关节名称嵌入以跨不同骨骼格式提供一致的解剖参考，并采用了跨注意力解码器。该模型通过特征重建来重构被遮蔽的动作，其中关节名称嵌入确定目标关节。

Result: 实验证明 MoSk 跨多种设置中的动作识别性能出色，特别是在线性评估和微调的情况下。MoSk 还在不同基础模型上显著提升了动作生成，减少了 MotionMillion 中的 FID，未条件生成降低了 9.75，文本到动作生成降低了 3.83。

Conclusion: MoSk 是一种创新的基础模型，实现了对大规模动作数据和骨骼数据的整合，提升了动作识别和生成的能力。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) have advanced visual understanding and generation by providing semantically rich representations. However, this role of FMs remains underexplored in human motion. Large motion datasets offer diverse movements at scale, but often lack action labels for systematically evaluating learned representations on understanding tasks. Skeleton datasets enable such evaluation through established recognition protocols, yet are typically smaller and focus on predefined actions captured in controlled environments. To combine these strengths, we present MoSk, to the best of our knowledge, the first Motion-Skeleton foundation model to integrate motion and skeleton datasets. MoSk learns shared representations through self-supervised masked motion modeling. We incorporate joint-name embeddings to provide a common anatomical reference across various skeleton formats. A cross-attention decoder reconstructs masked motion from the encoder's features, with joint-name embeddings specifying the target joints. Experiments demonstrate strong action-recognition performance across diverse settings, including linear evaluation and fine-tuning. MoSk also substantially improves motion generation across multiple backbones through representation alignment, reducing FID on MotionMillion by up to 9.75 for unconditional generation and 3.83 for text-to-motion generation.

</details>


### [130] [One Representation to Rule Them All -- Open-Vocabulary Change Detection from Text or Visual Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34430)
*Blaž Rolih,Matic Fučka,Filip Wolf,Luka Čehovin Zajc*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了OVTSID3框架，无需训练即可在统一的表示空间中实现区域分组、概念识别和时间比较，支持零样本文本查询或单次视觉提示。该框架在两个建筑和两个自然灾害基准测试中优于所有现有无训练开放词汇变化检测方法，提升了平均F1值8.9个百分点，同时支持语义变化检测并在SECOND上达到最新技术水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖于文本描述将不同模型分开处理，限制了目标概念的表达方式，且检测依赖多个中间预测，容易引入误差。为了克服这些问题，研究提出了OVTSID3框架。

Method: OVTSID3在DINOv3的表示空间中统一了区域分组、概念识别和时间比较。该框架支持零样本文本查询或单次视觉提示两种概念指定模式。

Result: OVTSID3在两个建筑和两个自然灾害基准测试中优于所有现有无训练开放词汇变化检测方法，提升了平均F1值8.9个百分点，并支持语义变化检测，在SECOND上达到最新技术水平。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了统一表示在文本或示例引导的变化检测中的灵活性，证明无需针对特定任务进行模型训练也能实现有效的变化检测。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary change detection (OVCD) aims to localise user-specified changes without retraining for predefined categories. Existing approaches typically rely on textual descriptions and combine separate models for region proposal, semantic recognition, and temporal comparison. This limits how target concepts can be expressed and makes detection dependent on several intermediate predictions. We introduce OVTSID3, a training-free framework that unifies region grouping, concept identification, and temporal comparison in the representation space of DINOv3. OVTSID3 supports two complementary modes of concept specification: zero-shot textual queries or one-shot visual prompting through a masked reference pair. OVTSID3 outperforms all prior training-free OVCD methods on two building and two natural-disaster benchmarks, raising the average F1 by 8.9 points. OVTSID3 also supports semantic change detection and achieves state-of-the-art on SECOND. These results demonstrate the flexibility of a unified representation for text- or example-guided change detection without task-specific model training.

</details>


### [131] [Most of the Way There: Turning a Strong Video Prior into an Action-Conditioned World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34435)
*Zanyi Wang,Xia Chen,Liu Liu,Xianglong Guo,Dengyang Jiang,Haodong Li,Tingyang Xiao,Wenhao Hu,Zhizhong Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Dianjing 通过专注于高噪声水平下的训练和采用统一的物理动作条件来利用强大的视频先验模型，实现无需大量机器人特定预训练的高效世界模型预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成器虽然能够产生视觉上合理的未来场景，但对特定动作的物理后果缺乏预测能力。ACWMs 提供了一种通过模拟机器人动作影响的途径，但在传统训练方式下，调整和适应成本高昂。因此，需要一种高效的方法来将强大的视频先验模型与物理动作预测相结合。

Method: Dianjing 使用统一的物理动作条件，专注于高噪声水平下的训练，并结合本体模型视频基础模型 MiniMax H3 进行训练。

Result: Dianjing 在 WorldOracle 上四个测试项目中获得了最佳分数，在 AgiBot-Long 上排名第二。表明强视频先验模型在物理动作地面的真实世界预测方面已经带来了大部分潜力。

Conclusion: 本研究发现，有效的物理动作接地可以帮助改善视频先验模型的预测效果，进而促进实际应用。

Abstract: Modern video generators excel at producing visually plausible futures guided by text and image prompts. Yet their emphasis on semantic plausibility does not guarantee fidelity to the physical consequences of specific actions. Action-conditioned world models (ACWMs), in contrast, aim to simulate how the world responds to robot actions, enabling scalable policy evaluation and improvement without costly real-world rollouts. The challenge is not merely to generate a plausible future, but to predict what follows from a specific physical action. As video priors grow stronger, we ask how little adaptation is truly needed to bridge this gap. We introduce Dianjing, a minimal recipe that preserves and unlocks the capabilities of strong video priors through unified physical action conditioning, training concentrated at the high noise levels where motion and layout, and thus the consequences of actions, are decided, and native history conditioning. Built upon MiniMax H3, a 33B video foundation model, Dianjing freezes the pretrained backbone and trains only 0.34% of its parameters using approximately 65 hours of robot video across two embodiments, without additional robot-specific pretraining or camera calibration. Controlled ablations show that where training is allocated across noise levels matters more than how actions enter the network. On WorldOracle, which evaluates predictions against paired real-world executions, Dianjing achieves the best scores on four of the five evaluation tracks and ranks second on AgiBot-Long. Our findings suggest that strong video priors already bring us most of the way to world modeling; lightweight physical action grounding can unlock their potential for real-world prediction.

</details>


### [132] [ContextHands: Completing Hand Motion with Learned Context and Joint-Specific Body Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34027)
*Taewoo Kim,Seunghun Koh,Hyosup Shin,Sungho Jo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ContextHands 是一种基于可观察到的身体序列和文字描述的手部动作生成方法，通过联合训练方法学习手部上下文特征，提升了手部动作的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高生成的人类动作的真实性和表达性，特别是在手部动作方面，ContextHands方法尝试通过连接身体动作和语言描述的上下文来填充未观察到的手部动作。

Method: ContextHands 方法包括一个身体编码器和一个扩散生成器。身体编码器收集关节和身体部分特征，并允许每个手关节查询这些特征来获取特定于关节的肢体信息。扩散生成器结合了这些特定于关节的身体信息和学习到的手部上下文信息。所有组件都进行联合训练。

Result: 实验结果显示，ContextHands 在竹笋2 数据集上的手部动作生成精度高于现有的手部动作生成方法和一般的动作生成方法。在抓取和使用工具的定性示例中，ContextHands 更接近参考动作的手部姿势。

Conclusion: 本研究引入了 ContextHands 方法，通过联合训练，实现了对手部上下文特征的学习，从而提高了手部动作的生成准确性。

Abstract: Detailed hand motion contributes to the realism and expressiveness of generated human motion. Completing these details from body motion and language requires connecting the context of an action to the articulation of the hands. We introduce ContextHands, which completes hand motion from an observed body sequence and text descriptions assigned to hands and time intervals. Our approach learns probabilistic hand features from the descriptions and body motion, using auxiliary hand prediction to connect these features to hand articulation. A body encoder preserves joint and body-part features across multiple temporal scales, allowing each hand joint to query these features for joint-specific body information. The diffusion generator combines this joint-specific body information with the learned hand context. Both encoders and the generator are trained jointly, so the final generation objective also shapes the context features. Experiments on the OakInk2 dataset show that ContextHands generates more accurate hand motion and achieves lower Fréchet Hand Distance than existing hand-motion and general human-motion generation methods adapted to the task. In qualitative examples of grasping and tool use, ContextHands more closely reproduces the detailed hand poses in the reference motion. We will release our code and pretrained models to support further research.

</details>


### [133] [ProxyGenBench: Benchmarking Proxy-Guided Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34457)
*Zhenchen Tang,Haolin Lu,Songlin Yang,Xiaotong Zhao,Ran Zhang,Bo Peng,Shuai Li,Haotian Fan,Haobin Zhong,Yanni Zhang,Songlian Li,Ning Zhang,Xun Gao,Alan Zhao,Jing Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究提出了ProxyGenBench基准，旨在评估视频生成器在保留结构意图、填补缺失内容、修正代理错误以及将文本、图像和音频条件与正确实体和事件绑定方面的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频生成器虽然可以生成逼真的视频，但在控制场景展开方面仍存在挑战。研究人员需要一个平台来综合评估生成器在这些复杂要求下的性能。

Method: 该研究引入了一个名为ProxyGenBench的基准平台，涵盖从60个场景中提取的312个评估案例，这些案例涉及三个方面的情景任务，并控制了代理精度、文本细节和多模态输入的变化。使用五种不同的视频生成器进行实验，并通过验证的特定案例清单进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，代理可以提高时空控制，但更高的代理细节可能会伴随较差的内容补充，同时严格遵循结构并不保证正确的绑定。

Conclusion: 研究认为未来的视频生成器需要在结构保真度、内容填充和多模态绑定方面取得均衡，才能在实践中达到最佳效果。

Abstract: Recent video generators produce realistic videos, yet controlling how scenes unfold remains challenging. Structural proxies, such as animated 3D blockouts, guide generation by specifying scene layout, motion, camera behavior, and event order. However, these coarse inputs leave details unspecified and may contain artifacts that should not be reproduced. Successful proxy-guided generation must therefore Preserve intended structure, Complete missing content and correct proxy artifacts, and Bind text, image, and audio conditions to the right entities and events. We introduce ProxyGenBench to study these interacting requirements, with 312 evaluation cases from 60 scenes across three scene tasks and controlled variations in proxy fidelity, text detail, and multimodal inputs. We evaluate five video generators using verified, case-specific checklists. Experiments show that proxies improve spatiotemporal control, but greater proxy detail can coincide with poorer completion, while strong structural adherence does not ensure correct binding.

</details>


### [134] [Angular Interpolation in Sparse-View Low-Dose CT Reconstruction: Placement Effects and Data-Consistency Step-Size Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34508)
*Yitian Tang,Yuncheng Zhang,Hualiang Wang,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究表明，双域网络在稀疏视角低剂量CT中结合了投影和图像处理方法。通过在稀释扫描数据中应用无参数插值，显著减少了噪声缺失视角的平均绝对误差。在控制分支宽度、深度和训练计划时，不同配置的双域网络展示了不同性能。总体而言，图像基体插值位置和网络架构会影响结果。文中还提出了NK标准化的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在探索并优化稀疏视角低剂量CT中的插值技术和双域网络架构，以提高图像质量和重建效率。

Method: 采用合成平行束数据进行实验验证，分别对参数自由插值和双域网络的不同配置进行了评估。

Result: 实验表明，在60个视角下，无参数插值相较于零填充能降低99.7%的噪声无视角平均绝对误差；全视角滤波反投影与插值滤波反投影相比，峰值信噪比提高1.27 dB。在数据一致性双域网络中，不同配置影响了基体的不同性能；2x2配置使得初始图像基体提高了1.98 dB，而插值图像基体则降低了2.16 dB；插值后的U-Net模型在60个视角和10%剂量下优于图像基体优化的变体，提高了0.96 dB，而在更高视角情况下则表现出色。

Conclusion: 研究强调了插值技术的合理应用和双域网络架构的设计对稀疏视角低剂量CT重建效果的重要性，提出了一种新型的NK标准化方法以优化CT投影的步骤校准。

Abstract: Dual-domain networks combine projection and image processing for sparse-view low-dose computed tomography (CT). We quantify the contribution of angular interpolation before learning and distinguish its roles in sinogram initialisation and the image residual base. On synthetic parallel-beam data, parameter-free interpolation reduces noise-free missing-view mean absolute error by 99.7% relative to zero filling at 60 views. Full-view filtered back-projection (FBP) of the jointly learned completed sinogram adds 1.27 dB of peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) over interpolated FBP. In a data-consistency dual-domain network (DC-DuNet), a 2x2 configuration ablation controls branch width, depth and training schedule while varying these placements and their associated input channels. After 20 epochs, the PSNR contrasts are +1.98 dB for sinogram initialisation and -2.16 dB for the interpolated image base; the image-base ranking also reverses during training. The sinogram-initialised variant exceeds an image-only U-Net by 0.96 dB at 60 views and 10% dose, and by 1.28 dB on independently generated synthetic data. The U-Net leads at higher view counts and with a shared interpolated image base. Without retraining, the variant retains a 0.12 dB advantage on held-out CHAOS abdominal CT under simulated degradation; the image-base contrast rounds to zero. The implemented projection/backprojection pair has empirical dominant gain of approximately 0.85NK, where N is image-grid width and K acquired view count. These results distinguish interpolation placement from network architecture and support NK normalisation for step calibration.

</details>


### [135] [Understanding Diffusion Acceleration: Methods, Trade-Offs, and Open Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34056)
*Xiaofeng Mao,Fu-yun Wang,Le Zhuo,Zhengkai Jiang,Shaohao Rui,Weijie Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文讨论了四类加速生成模型推理的方法，并通过图像和视频基准展示了不同生成步骤的质量-成本权衡、求解器和时间步长计划的交互作用、特征缓存的影响，以及首帧延迟与持续流媒体吞吐量之间的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速基于扩散和流的生成模型的推理过程，以减轻在交互式和边缘应用中的延迟和能耗。

Method: 本文通过提出一组通用的符号，比较了学习目标、采样轨迹和每个去噪器周期内的工作负载变化。此外，还研究了图像和视频基准，分析了单一步生成、多步生成之间的质量-成本权衡，以及求解器和时间步长计划的相互影响。

Result: 通过分析一个和 few-steps 生成方法，以及对特征缓存、首帧与持续流媒体吞吐量之间的权衡。展示了不同采样方法对生成质量的影响。

Conclusion: 本文提出了视觉本原架构、压缩后蒸馏、求解器感知的 few-steps 训练以及语义轨迹对齐与因子化分布匹配的开放问题。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-based generative models support high-quality image, audio, and video synthesis. Sampling can require dozens to hundreds of denoiser evaluations, with each pass becoming more expensive as resolution, video length, or model size increases. This cost affects latency and energy use in interactive and edge applications. We examine four approaches to accelerating inference: distillation, consistency training, sampling methods, and denoiser design. We express training objectives, transition maps, and numerical updates in a common notation to compare changes to the learning target, the sampling trajectory, and the work performed within each denoiser pass. Published image and video benchmarks illustrate the quality--cost trade-offs of one- and few-step generation, the interaction between solvers and timestep schedules, the effect of feature caching, and the difference between first-frame latency and sustained streaming throughput. We close with open problems in visual-native architectures and post-compression distillation, solver-aware few-step training, and semantic trajectory alignment with factorized distribution matching.

</details>


### [136] [PURA: Video Token Pruning via Unified Spatiotemporal Redundancy Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34521)
*Yuyao Sun,Hongli Yuan,Shuang Li,Tao Deng,Deqing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PURA 提出了一种无需训练的方法，通过联合时空冗余评估去除视觉令牌，实现了在不同视频具有不同空间和时间冗余的场景下对视觉令牌进行剪枝，同时通过协调剪枝策略提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频令牌压缩方法通常基于预定义的处理顺序或固定的空间和时间压缩分配比率，这限制了它们适应不同视频中时空冗余变化的能力。PURA 方法旨在解决这一问题，通过在不依赖于训练的情况下，根据时空冗余量度对视觉令牌进行联合修剪。

Method: PURA 方法首先为每个令牌构建空间和时间参考候选集，然后选择在两个维度中具有最低表示误差的参考。该方法将表示误差与令牌的重要性结合，以确定每个令牌的删除成本。此外，为了避免逐步删除最低成本令牌所花费的时间开销，PURA 引入了协调剪枝策略，该策略会迭代地将兼容的低成本删除组合成批次，一次性执行并更新剪枝状态，直到达到目标预算。

Result: PURA 方法已在三个大型视频语言模型和五个视频理解基准上进行了广泛的实验，并证明了它在任务性能和推理效率之间实现了良好的权衡。

Conclusion: PURA 方法通过其联合时空冗余评估策略和协调剪枝策略，展示了其在提供高效的视觉令牌剪枝方面具有优势，有效应对了不同视频中的时空冗余变化问题。

Abstract: Video large language models incur substantial inference costs from long visual token sequences. Existing video token compression methods mitigate these costs by exploiting spatial redundancy within frames and temporal redundancy across frames. However, many approaches impose predefined processing orders or fixed allocation ratios for spatial and temporal compression, limiting their ability to adapt to the varying balance of spatiotemporal redundancy across videos. To address this limitation, we propose PURA, a training-free method that prunes visual tokens through unified spatiotemporal redundancy assessment. For each token, PURA constructs spatial and temporal reference candidates and selects the reference with the lowest representation error across the two dimensions. The representation error is combined with token importance to determine each token's removal cost. To avoid the time overhead of removing the lowest-cost token one by one, we introduce Coordinated Pruning, which iteratively groups mutually compatible low-cost removals into batches for simultaneous execution and updates the pruning state after each batch until the target budget is reached. Extensive experiments on three video large language models and five video understanding benchmarks demonstrate that PURA achieves a favorable trade-off between task performance and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [137] [Surf-Style: Surface-Based Scene Stylization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34062)
*Wanrong Hong,Ancheng Lin,Mukesh Prasad,Jun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Surface-based Scene Stylization (Surf-Style) 通过重建 3D 场景中的物体表面实现几何一致性与局部风格化，优于现有方法，提供更可控的风格化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 3D 造型与风格化方法难以解决视角间的一致性问题，而 Surf-Style 提出了一种新颖的方法来解决此问题。

Method: Surf-Style 通过重构 3D 场景中的对象表面，定义了可在 3D 空间中学习的外观模型，并通过调整该模型来复制参考图像的视觉风格。这种方法能够确保在不同视角下的一致性，并且允许针对特定表面区域进行可控的风格化。

Result: 实验结果表明，与现有方法相比，Surf-Style 在一致性方面表现出色，并且提供了更强大的可控风格化能力。

Conclusion: Surf-Style 为 3D 场景提供了更为一致且可控制的风格化方案，适用于增强现实、虚拟现实及其他视觉计算应用领域。

Abstract: Consistent and controllable 3D stylization is critical for visual computing applications such as immersive visualization, augmented and virtual reality content creation, and artistic scene rendering. While 2D image stylization has advanced significantly, extending these methods to 3D remains challenging due to the need for cross-view consistency, where stylized renderings must align across all viewpoints. Applying 2D stylization independently to each rendered view often results in inconsistencies across perspectives. Existing methods attempt to improve consistency by leveraging implicit radiance field representations, which model scene appearance as a function of spatial locations. However, these methods struggle with ambiguity in updating colors, as the indirect dependence between pixel appearance and learnable scene parameters makes it difficult to enforce consistent stylization during optimization. This work presents Surface-based Scene Stylization (Surf-Style), which reconstructs object surfaces in a 3D scene and uses these surfaces to achieve geometric consistency and localized stylization. Surf-Style defines a learnable appearance model over 3D space, which is modified to replicate the visual style of a reference image. Compared to state-of-the-art methods, our method achieves superior consistency and offers greater flexibility in controllable stylization, including the ability to target specific surface regions and combine different styles.

</details>


### [138] [SPARC: Geometry-Guided Attention Pruning for Long-Horizon Camera-Controlled Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34064)
*Zesong Yang,Weikai Chen,Liyuan Cui,Yuan Li,Jiaxin Huang,Lutao Jiang,Runze Zhang,Yingda Yin,Xiaoyang Huang,Kai Yan,Keyang Luo,Xin Wang,Hujun Bao,Zhaopeng Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPARC框架利用几何指导的注意力修剪机制，在不引入额外的控制或记忆模块的情况下，实现精确的摄像机控制和忠实的场景回忆，同时大幅减少注意力计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受重建已观测内容和生成新区域不对称上下文需求的驱动，SPARC框架旨在通过几何对应注意力偏差和顺序不变的历史定位，实现稳定且一致的长期时空跨接。

Method: SPARC框架通过限制观测令牌到几何上对应的先前历史令牌和局部时空上下文来重构观测内容，同时保留对未观测区域的密集注意力。

Result: SPARC框架在摄像机控制、场景回忆和视觉保真度方面表现出色，结合四步蒸馏后，推理速度提高超过50倍。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了激进的注意力修剪在提高摄像机匹配性、回忆一致性和视觉保真度方面的有效性。

Abstract: We present SPARC, a geometry-guided attention pruning framework that turns the native self-attention of pretrained video diffusion transformers into a unified mechanism for camera control and persistent scene memory. Our key insight is that attention connectivity can simultaneously serve as geometric conditioning for camera control and spatial addressing for historical memory retrieval. Motivated by the asymmetric contextual requirements of reconstructing observed content and synthesizing novel regions, SPARC restricts observed tokens to geometrically corresponding historical tokens and local spatiotemporal context, while preserving dense attention for unobserved regions. Correspondence-aware attention biasing and order-invariant historical positioning further enable stable retrieval across long temporal gaps. Without introducing additional learnable control or memory modules, SPARC achieves precise camera adherence and faithful scene revisitation while substantially reducing attention computation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of aggressive attention pruning in improving camera adherence, revisit consistency, and visual fidelity, with an inference speedup of over 50x when combined with four-step distillation. Project page is available at https://zju3dv.github.io/SPARC.

</details>


### [139] [HAS: Hierarchical Adaptive Frame Sampling for Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34544)
*Yuyao Sun,Daiqi Shi,Shuang Li,Liao Zhang,Yang Cai,Deqing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HAS框架提出了一种两阶段自适应帧采样方法，通过DPP进行基础帧选择，并根据查询需求进行局部和全局上下文的适应性增强，从而有效满足不同查询的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的帧采样方法在处理长视频时存在局限性，无法同时满足不同查询需求，因此需要一种新的方法来处理这一问题。

Method: HAS框架采用两阶段自适应帧采样方法，首阶段利用DPP选择查询相关的非冗余帧作为基础帧，第二阶段根据查询需求进行局部和全局上下文的动态适配。

Result: HAS方法被集成到三个主流的LLM模型中，并在Video-MME、LongVideoBench和MLVU基准测试中表现出一致的优越性。

Conclusion: HAS方法通过多层次的帧采样和动态适配，有效满足了不同查询的需求，提升了模型在处理长视频任务时的表现。

Abstract: Due to context-length constraints, most MLLMs cannot process full-length videos and therefore rely on sampling a subset of frames as input. However, existing sampling methods, ranging from uniform sampling to relevance-based selection, are often driven by a single sampling principle and thus struggle to accommodate the heterogeneous evidential requirements of different queries: some demand a holistic understanding of how the video unfolds over time, while others hinge on fine-grained events within a short temporal window. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{Hierarchical Adaptive Frame Sampling (HAS)}, a two-stage frame sampling framework. In the first stage, \emph{Backbone Frame Construction}, we apply a Determinantal Point Process (DPP) to sample frames that are both query-relevant and non-redundant. These selected frames capture key moments across the video and form the backbone of the sampling set, providing a foundation for subsequent enrichment. In the second stage, \emph{Adaptive Contextual Enrichment}, we analyze the temporal distribution of the backbone frames to infer the query's requirements and adaptively allocate the remaining frame budget between Local and Global Context. The Local Context enriches the backbone with fine-grained temporal dynamics and short-range causal relations, whereas the Global Context connects temporally isolated evidence and provides a holistic view of the entire video to support global understanding. Through such a hierarchical two-stage process, HAS effectively addresses diverse query requirements. Incorporated into three leading MLLMs, it demonstrates consistently superior performance across the Video-MME, LongVideoBench, and MLVU benchmarks.

</details>


### [140] [When to Respond, Where to Ground: Streaming 3D Affordance Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34067)
*Wenting Luan,Weilong Yan,Siqi Lu,Ming Li,Beibei Lin,Yongbin Zheng,Robby T. Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamAff 提出了一种基于证据的响应策略，在持续场景观察中联合决定响应时机和功能部件定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D用途接地方法通常假设完整场景观察，而基于视觉-语言模型缺乏细粒度的3D功能定位能力。作者旨在解决在连续场景观察中同时确定回应时间和功能性部分定位的问题。

Method: StreamAff 提出了一个基于证据的响应策略，从接地模型自身视觉证据中做出两个决策。通过评分视觉片段的组合支持，并与等待选项进行权衡，决定何时回应；最高评分片段选择在该帧上进行接地。引入了有条件回顾政策，允许模型在请求时刻从之前的观察作出回应或等待新证据。提出了三维地面分割方法，在选定帧的3D点上对答案中提到的部分进行分段。

Result: StreamAff 在15秒流中实现了30.14%的StreamIoU，相较于强基线的相对改进为40.6%。

Conclusion: StreamAff 和 StreamFun3D 提供了一种评估响应时间和接地的基准，强调了时间和正确性对于三维用途接地的重要性。

Abstract: Given a continuous RGB-D stream and a language request, streaming 3D affordance grounding requires an agent to decide when to respond and where to locate the requested functional part using only observations available so far. Existing 3D affordance grounding methods typically assume complete scene observations, while streaming vision-language models lack fine-grained 3D functional grounding. Our goal is to jointly determine response timing and functional-part localization during continuous scene observation. We propose StreamAff, an evidence-guided response strategy that makes both decisions from the grounding model's own visual evidence. The model scores the visual patches of the frames it reads: their combined support, weighed against a wait option, decides when to respond, and the highest-scoring patch selects the frame to ground in. A conditional look-back policy lets the model answer from an earlier observation at the moment of the request or wait for new evidence, and zoom-and-refine grounding segments the part named in the answer on the 3D points of the selected frame. We further introduce StreamFun3D, a benchmark derived from real-world 3D scenes that evaluates response timing and grounding across four evidence settings: Current, History, Future, and Unobserved, together with StreamIoU, a metric that credits a mask only when the response is timely and grounded in a valid source frame. We adapt offline 3D affordance grounding methods and build external-decider baselines that pair vision-language models with our mask decoder. StreamAff achieves 30.14% StreamIoU on 15-second streams, a 40.6% relative improvement over the strongest of these baselines.

</details>


### [141] [Recalling the Past to Answer the Present: Context-Aware Multi-Evidence Reasoning for Streaming Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34545)
*Cui Yakun,Tiantian Geng,Huixiang Kong,Xingqun Qi,Sirui Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的基准StreamRecall，用于评估视频问答中的上下文相关多证据推理能力，以及EviStream机制以实现实时视频理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频问答基准主要集中在静态场景下进行评估，而真实的应用场景通常需要在线解答上下文相关的实时查询，因此需要一个新的基准和新的机制来填充这一空白。

Method: StreamRecall包含4,983个问题和462个视频，每个查询都有时间对齐的问题时刻和精确的时间证据注释。为了诊断模型推理，提出了证据利用级联，包括证据定位、配对、链条完成和答案推导。为了支持高效的长期流式推理，提出了EviStream机制。

Result: 实验表明当前的视频语言模型在流媒体环境下与真实的性能要求之间存在显著差距，这证明了StreamRecall作为一个发展基于证据的流视频智能的基础的重要性。

Conclusion: StreamRecall基准和EviStream机制为开发应对现实流媒体场景的视频语言模型提供了重要的参考框架。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in video understanding, yet existing video question answering (VQA) benchmarks mainly evaluate models in offline settings. Real-world streaming scenarios instead require models to answer context-dependent queries by retrieving and reasoning over evidence distributed across the video history. We introduce StreamRecall, a benchmark for context-aware Multi evidence reasoning in streaming video QA. StreamRecall requires models to retrieve, associate, and reason over temporally scattered evidence to answer queries at the appropriate moment. It contains 4,983 questions over 462 videos, with context-aligned query times and fine-grained temporal evidence annotations. We further propose the Evidence Utilization Cascade to diagnose model reasoning through evidence localization, grounding, chain completion, and answer derivation. To enable efficient long-term streaming reasoning, we present EviStream, a training-free causal visual memory mechanism that preserves reasoning-critical evidence under a fixed KV budget. Experiments reveal substantial gaps between current VLMs and realistic streaming requirements, establishing StreamRecall as a foundation for developing evidence-aware streaming video intelligence.

</details>


### [142] [Sage-Med: Scaling Medical Multimodal Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34575)
*Sage-Med Team,:,Ming Hu,Cheng Tang,Wei Li,Rongbin Li,Feilong Tang,Junru Hu,Hongze Zhu,Qiujuan Yan,Huanze Tang,Shaocong Wei,Siyuan Yan,Min Cen,Zhongxing Xu,Zhonghua Wang,Lie Ju,Zhen Yu,Wenjie Li,Shiyan Su,Jing Hao,Jingyong Liang,Diping Song,Fang Yan,Chenglong Ma,Junzhi Ning,Haochen Xue,Zanting Ye,Chenrun Wang,Jinjie Wei,Yi Xin,Jianghan Shen,Siqi Luo,Jiyao Liu,Huidong Xie,Jun Zhao,Rulin Zhou,Longquan Jiang,Ziyan Huang,Ying Chen,Hanxiao Zhang,Junyang Wu,Mouxiao Bian,Jiashi Lin,Chenyu Wang,Qi Qin,Jie Huang,Yiling Zhou,Jiawei Chen,Kefan Wang,Jingjing Hua,Yaoyu Liu,Huihui Xu,Tianbin Li,Jin Ye,Ziyang Chen,Shujian Gao,Zilin Lu,Jiangtao Yan,Changkai Ji,Wenhao Tang,Xinyi Zeng,Yuxing Chen,Hongwei Yang,Rongzhao Zhang,Jinru Ding,Yingcheng Wu,Zhenfei Yin,Jinzhe Li,Lu Lu,Yun Zhong,Zhishan Lin,Bingli Wang,Xiaohan Ren,Jianmin Qian,Penghao Zhao,Zhongbo Tian,Zhenbang Zhou,Peiling Dai,Mengjing Zhao,Jingyu Zheng,Pinan Liu,Shiwei Li,Victoria Mar,Cristina Vico-Alonso,Qiang Wu,Xianming Liu,Lingtong Huang,Dili Song,Minghui Zhang,Wenlong Zhang,Qipeng Guo,Yuqiang Li,Nanqing Dong,Yun Gu,Qi Duan,Hongliang Ren,Hongming Shan,Yin Xie,Xiang An,Benyou Wang,Yueming Su,Yefeng Zheng,Zongyuan Ge,Chun-Mei Feng,Jie Xu,Yu Qiao,Junjun He,Yirong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为Sage-Med-Preview的大型医疗多模态语言模型，该模型在多模态任务中表现出色，特别是在医学理解和语言能力方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗多模态大型语言模型（MLLM）常在面向范围广泛的临床任务时表现不佳，因此为了探索医疗多模态智能的实际规模扩展，进行了深入研究。

Method: Sage-Med-Preview模型通过阶段特定的筛选超过3亿2千多万候选记录和一个包含医学预训练、阶段监督微调和自适应强化学习的渐进培训课程进行构建。

Result: Sage-Med-Preview模型在238项临床任务上表现优异，尤其在公共的31个多模态基准测试中，平均得分为58.26%，并且在多个任务上甚至超过了具有更多参数的其他先进模型。

Conclusion: 研究表明，虽然在复杂医学推理和多步骤临床工作流方面仍然存在挑战，但该报告为可扩展的临床多模态智能提供了实证证据、有用的数据和培训指南，并建立了严格的基准。

Abstract: Establishing effective scaling laws for medical multimodal large language models (MLLMs) remains a persistent and fundamental challenge. Our systematic evaluation shows that existing medical MLLMs tend to underperform frontier general-purpose MLLMs across a range of medical tasks, while scaling model size primarily improves text capabilities rather than multimodal perception, and enabling reasoning modes fails to close this visual gap. To investigate how medical multimodal intelligence scales in practice, we introduce Sage-Med-Preview, a 35B-parameter medical multimodal model with 3B activated parameters, built by combining stage-specific curation over 322.7 million candidate records with a progressive training curriculum spanning medical pretraining, staged supervised fine-tuning, and adaptive reinforcement learning. Across this curriculum, progressive supervised refinement and adaptive reinforcement learning yield consistent stage-wise gains, substantially advancing both specialized visual understanding and medical language capabilities. Evaluated against 77 competing models across 238 clinical tasks spanning 61 public and 13 private multi-center benchmarks in more than 40 modalities, Sage-Med-Preview achieves an average score of 58.26% on the 31 public multimodal benchmarks. It outperforms the state-of-the-art open-source medical model Hulu-Med-235B-A22B at 50.99% and the general scientific model Intern-S2-Preview at 52.24%, and approaches the advanced proprietary model GPT-6.2-Sol across multiple tasks such as comprehensive multimodal understanding, report generation, and visual grounding. Although substantial challenges remain in complex medical reasoning and multi-step clinical workflows, this report provides empirical evidence, actionable data and training recipes, and a rigorous benchmark toward scalable clinical multimodal intelligence.

</details>


### [143] [Projector Subspace Optimization for Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34078)
*Hao Yu,Quan Cheng,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PROUD的方法，旨在通过优化更新方向进行持续子空间优化框架，以提高视觉-语言投影器终端层的适应性，从而在连续任务中实现更高的准确率并减少遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态持续指令调优方法在序列任务适应上效率低下，且现有技术在参数和路由上的开销较大。本文提出了一种新的方法，以便更好地解决这个问题。

Method: PROUD 方法通过结合保留的输入统计和初始梯度来分析性地提取一个在预算内的、公理正交的子空间，该子空间最大化任务的初始一阶收益。在训练过程中，该方法通过精确的Frobenius惩罚来正则化更新，以确保子空间内的更新，最后无附加推理或路由开销地融合这些更新。

Result: 在UCIT和CoIN上跨多个大规模多模态语言模型构建物进行的实验表明，PROUD 方法优于现有无重放基线，在连续任务准确率上表现更优，同时显著减少遗忘现象。

Conclusion: PROUD 方法提供了一种有效的框架来优化终端投影层的持续子空间，可在少量可训练参数下显著提升多模态语言模型在顺序任务中的适配能力并减少遗忘。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance through instruction tuning, yet adapting them to sequential tasks requires Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning (MCIT). Existing replay-free methods expand prompts or routed LoRA experts across multi-layer decoders, incurring heavy parameter and routing overhead while shifting internal feature distributions. Instead, we identify the final linear layer of the vision-language projector as a high-capacity, coordinate-stationary bridge, where sequential updates easily induce interface drift by displacing historical visual tokens. While low-rank subspace adaptation offers a compact remedy, selecting directions solely from historical statistics or task gradients sacrifices plasticity or stability, and unconstrained multi-step updates still accumulate large drift. To resolve both issues, we propose Projector Refinement through Optimized Update Directions (PROUD), a continual subspace optimization framework for the terminal projector layer. At task onset, PROUD combines retained input statistics with initial gradients to analytically extract a metric-orthonormal subspace maximizing first-order task gain under a drift budget. During training, it regularizes updates within this fixed subspace via an exact Frobenius penalty before losslessly merging them with zero inference or routing overhead.
