Experiments on UCIT and CoIN across MLLM backbones show that PROUD outperforms recent replay-free baselines in continual accuracy while curbing forgetting with a fraction of trainable parameters.

</details>


### [144] [VisualMontage: Manipulating the Beliefs of Vision-Language Agents with Authentic Image Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34603)
*Jinwei Hu,Lai Wei,Zhuoyun Li,Youcheng Sun,Yi Dong,Xiaowei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究引入了一个新的基准数据集，提出了一个模拟视觉认知共谋攻击的多智能体框架，评估了这种认知威胁，并发现在多种视觉-语言模型中该攻击策略的跨模型可转移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于视觉-语言模型（VLMs）的代理越来越多地解释社交媒体内容，他们在分析中出现的错误可能会为谣言注入合法性并扩大其传播。因此，提出了研究视觉认知共谋问题，即协调发布的帖子结合真实图像和局部真实标题来诱导VLM代理推理和接受攻击者选择的目标声明。

Method: 研究提出了VisualMontage框架，进行了大量的实验，评估了多种视觉-语言模型在该威胁下的表现，研究了模态效果、生产团队组件和下游防御措施。

Result: 在12种视觉-语言模型上进行的大规模实验中，展示了一种跨模型转移性的攻击策略，对于私有模型的平均攻击成功率达到了61.93%，对于开放权重模型达到了68.71%。生成的攻击推送中有92.65%通过了基于模型的验证审核。即使在下游审查后，攻击成功率在不同配置中仍然从35.3%到76.7%。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了一种新的社交媒体解释的认知威胁，表明需要评估真实帖子如何联合塑造代理的推理和结论，并激励采用策略来防范这种认知威胁。

Abstract: As agents based on vision-language models (VLMs) increasingly interpret social-media content, errors in their analysis may lend credibility to rumors and amplify their spread. In this paper, we study the problem of visual cognitive collusion, in which coordinated posts combine authentic images and locally faithful captions to induce VLM agents to infer and accept attacker-chosen target claims through their own reasoning. To enable systematic evaluation of this cognitive threat, we introduce a new benchmark comprising 544 target cases and 33.2K candidate images across eight application domains. We further propose VisualMontage, a multi-agent framework for simulating such attacks through narrative planning, image selection and ordering, and caption assignment under iterative review. We evaluate this threat through extensive experiments across 12 vision-language models, examining modality effects, production-team components, and downstream defenses. Using identical attack feeds across models without victim-specific optimization, we demonstrate cross-model transferability, achieving mean attack success rates of 61.93% for proprietary models and 68.71% for open-weight models. Moreover, 92.65% of generated feeds pass independent model-based validity auditing. Even after downstream review, residual attack success rates range from 35.3% to 76.7% across the evaluated configurations. Our additional controlled comparisons further reveal that captions can improve model-rated posting naturalness while reducing attack success. Together, these findings reveal a novel cognitive threat in social-media interpretation and motivate safeguards that assess how authentic posts jointly shape agents' reasoning and conclusions.

</details>


### [145] [Carrying Feature Ablations Across Sparse Autoencoder Seeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34637)
*Karl Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过对比不同随机种子训练的稀疏自编码器(SAE)之间的匹配特征，以评估特征去除的跨种子一致性，并提出了一种新的测试方法来发现模型中的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索通过稀疏自编码器匹配的特征在不同训练条件下是否稳定，以及特征去除的影响是否具有跨种子一致性。

Method: 1. 使用稀疏自编码器匹配不同随机种子下训练的模型的特征；
2. 分别去除一个特征及其匹配的特征，在验证集上评估两个去除操作的输出变化是否一致；
3. 探讨保持层贡献信息比直接使用方向更准确地预测哪些去除操作会产生一致变化；
4. 验证其方法对独立测试图像是否有效。

Result: 在三种设置中，发现12%-70%的特征对具有跨种子一致性；在随机方向保持不变和后处理匹配打乱的情况中，这一比例为0%；在新提出的方法中，尽管存在一些错误预测，但层贡献比方向更准确地预测保持一致性。

Conclusion: 研究人员发现了评估模型中特征去除操作的一致性和准确性有效的新方法。这种方法比直接使用训练时的方向预测更有优势。同时也表明，某些预测仍然可能存在错误，这需要进一步优化以提高准确率。
该发现对验证模型的稳定性和鲁棒性具有一定启示意义。

Abstract: A vision encoder turns an image into one vector per patch, refined layer by layer. A sparse autoencoder (SAE) writes each vector of one layer as a sum of learned directions, most with a coefficient of zero. Researchers call each direction a feature and edit the encoder by removing one feature. An SAE trained from another random seed learns different features. Earlier work matches the two SAEs' features one to one by how closely their directions agree. Matched features can point alike yet respond to different image parts, so removing them may change the encoder differently. We remove a feature and, separately, its match, then compare the changes in the encoder's output on the same held out images. When the second change nearly reproduces the first, we say the removal carries across seeds. A setting fixes a DINOv2 or SigLIP layer and its SAE feature count. At our tolerance, the removal carries for 12% to 70% of matched pairs across three settings. The share is 0% in two controls: an SAE whose directions stay random and, post hoc, matches shuffled among trained features. Post hoc, predicting which removals carry is more accurate with layer contributions than with directions. Some incorrect predictions remain. One persists on independent images. So pairs can be screened at the layer. Measuring the output detects errors this screen misses.

</details>


### [146] [TaCo: Rethinking Correlation for Visual Tracking on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34084)
*Qingmao Wei,Fagui Liu,Quan Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TaCo 提出了一个结合卷积神经网络和传统相关匹配的方法，以保持速度和准确性，特别适用于低功耗边缘硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在弥补传统相关匹配在边缘硬件上速度慢和准确度低的不足，特别是在使用注意力机制的轻量级跟踪器存在内存瓶颈的情况下。

Method: TaCo 通过将目标描述符与搜索特征进行逐帧运算，并使用目标框对滤波器进行对齐，确保搜索特征与目标形成更精确的匹配。这种方法在卷积网络中固定不变，从而实现了低功耗硬件上的高效跟踪。

Result: 实验结果显示，TaCo 算法在 Rockchip RV1126B NPU 上以 184 FPS 达到 62.3% 的 LaSOT AUC，在 Raspberry Pi 5 CPU 上达到 96 FPS。此外，TaCo-T 达到了比 HiT-Tiny 和 AsymTrack-T 更高的准确性，同时速度至少提高了 3 倍。

Conclusion: TaCo 提供了一种更高效、更准确的跟踪方法，特别适合边缘计算和低功耗设备应用。

Abstract: Lightweight trackers have replaced cross-correlation with attention, and on low-power edge hardware they pay for it, since memory-bound softmax, normalization and reshaping operators of attention cost far more time than their arithmetic suggests. Benchmarking efficient trackers on an edge Neural Processing Unit (NPU) and an Arm CPU under one numerically validated protocol, we find that correlation trackers are the fast ones, but they lag in accuracy unless they adopt a ViT backbone, which brings attention back. We revisit what correlation lacks. Template--search interaction serves two roles: conditioning the search features on the target and matching them against it spatially. Attention does both, whereas correlation only matches, and does so on features extracted without knowledge of the target. We present TaCo (Target-aligned Correlation), which supplies the missing conditioning without attention by letting the template enter the per-frame network only as operands computed once per video. A descriptor of the target scales and shifts the search features channel by channel inside the backbone, and kernels aligned with the target box, each with a weight predicted from the template, correlate with them in one matrix product. With the backbone fixed, our correlation remains essential while incurring negligible NPU overhead, offering a better speed-accuracy trade-off than the attention-based interactions we tested. TaCo-T achieves 62.3% LaSOT AUC at 184 FPS on the Rockchip RV1126B NPU and 96 FPS on a Raspberry Pi 5 CPU, 3.0x and 3.5x the NPU speed of HiT-Tiny and AsymTrack-T at higher accuracy. TaCo-B reaches 65.6% at 50 FPS on the NPU, more than twice the speed of HiT-Base, AsymTrack-B and LightFC-vit, again at higher accuracy. Code and models are available at https://github.com/TsingWei/TaCo-Tracker.

</details>


### [147] [Motion Beyond Pixels: Learning Future Visual Representations for Object-Centric Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34644)
*Chengzhi Zhao,Juelin Li,Zhennan Wang,Sheng Wang,Zhuoman Liu,Yuhao Lu,Jiongxu Chen,Haitang Feng,Yanjie Li,Lin Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MotionRep通过联合生成未来DINO表示和3D物体流，同时保留语义和几何信息，改进了物体中心动力学建模。它仅需三步去噪即可实现优于基于像素的方法，特别是在3D物体流准确性和对未见场景的泛化能力上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的许多基于体感的模型在处理RGB重建时，未能明确优先考虑与运动生成相关的语义和几何信息。这些工作动机在于改进物体中心动力学建模，旨在同时捕捉视觉表示和3D几何信息。

Method: 提出了DINO-VAE进行特征压缩和语义几何信息保留，通过联合注意力机制使统一扩散变换器预测未来视觉表示和3D物体流。采用非对称模态特定噪声调度，确保视觉流在联合去噪时领先于3D物体流，从而促进逐步细化的未来视觉表示指导流动生成。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界的操作基准测试中，MotionRep在3D物体流准确性和对未见过场景的泛化能力上优于基于像素的方法，仅需三步去噪。

Conclusion: 这些结果支持了预测性视觉表示作为物体中心动力学建模的有效未来状态空间。

Abstract: Many embodied world models predict future states in latent spaces optimized for RGB reconstruction, which do not explicitly prioritize object-level semantics and geometry relevant to motion generation. We present MotionRep, a visual representation world model that jointly generates future DINO representations and 3D object flow, coupling semantic and geometric information with object-centric dynamics. To make high-dimensional DINO features amenable to generative modeling, we introduce DINO-VAE, which combines temporal and channel compression with latent regularization to produce compact latents while preserving semantic and geometric information. Conditioned on an initial observation, object query points, and a language instruction, a unified diffusion transformer predicts future visual representations and 3D object flow through joint attention. MotionRep employs asymmetric modality-specific noise schedules to keep the visual stream ahead of 3D object flow during joint denoising, allowing progressively refined future visual representations to guide flow generation. Experiments on simulated and real-world manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that MotionRep outperforms pixel-based baselines in 3D object flow accuracy and generalization to unseen scenes, while requiring only three denoising steps for inference. These results support predictive visual representations as an effective future-state space for object-centric dynamics modeling.

</details>


### [148] [From Diagnosing Alignment to Joint Supervision in Multimodal Draft Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34091)
*Haozhe Du,Runze Liu,Jiaming Fan,Yitong Tao,Shuxia Lin,Chonghan Liu,Daming Cao,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过诊断多模态草稿（MM）和纯文本（TXT）监督对对齐效果的影响，并提出了一种名为MNRS-Joint的联合监督方法，以优化生成模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明，尽管多模态（MM）和纯文本（TXT）监督都能提高生成模型的效果，但二者如何结合以及对齐效果如何改进尚不清楚。因此，本文旨在探索不同监督方式对生成模型的影响，并提出一种有效的优化方法。

Method: 通过对比有无视觉输入的预测结果来诊断草稿目标对齐情况，进一步分解分布误差；采用控权继续训练来评估视觉响应风险对各风险组别性能的影响；引入MNRS-Joint方法，根据草稿的风险分布进行联合监督。

Result: 研究发现高风险组的表现优于随机组和低风险组，视觉响应风险比总体风险更能指导MM训练；MNRS-Joint方法在三种不同设置下表现优异，尤其是在LLaVA-1.5-7B数据集上的指标显著提升。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种新型的联合监督方法MNRS-Joint，能够有效提高生成模型的生成长度和生成速度，特别是在面对视觉响应风险时表现出色。

Abstract: Multimodal drafts learn from multimodal (MM) and text-only (TXT) supervision, yet their effects on alignment and how to combine them remain unclear. We diagnose draft-target alignment through paired predictions with and without visual input, decomposing distributional error into text-conditioned and visual-response components. Drafts with similar text-conditioned alignment can still differ markedly in visual-response risk and acceptance. Controlled continuation training shows that high-risk groups outperform random and low-risk groups, and visual-response risk guides MM training more effectively than aggregate risk. Building on these diagnostics, we propose MNRS-Joint, which configures joint supervision from a given draft's component-risk distributions, using modality-specific mean risks to determine the MM:TXT mixture. Across three warm-ups, its distinct mixtures fall in high-performing regions of the ratio sweeps. On LLaVA-1.5-7B, MNRS-Joint improves mean accepted length from 3.35 to 3.53 and mean speedup from $2.04\times$ to $2.16\times$ over composition-matched random training under greedy tree decoding. The gains also hold under stochastic chain decoding.

</details>


### [149] [Find the Clue, Not the Scene: Rethinking Reasoning for Entity Recognition in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34681)
*Kyungmin Min,Soyeon Kim,Junseok Kim,Kyomin Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究分析了基于思考的视觉语言模型（VLMs）在知识基础视觉问答任务中的表现，发现实体识别是瓶颈，实体识别依赖于图像中的特征线索而非一般描述。研究提出了ClueRL方法，通过增强模型对这些特征线索的关注，使模型能够更好地利用现有知识，从而在多种基准测试上表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的VLMs在回答基于知识的视觉问题时并未深究如何进行实体识别与召回。该论文旨在填补这一空白，深入分析每个步骤的表现并提出改进方法。

Method: 研究通过分析多个VLMs模型在ViQuAE任务上的表现，进行定量分析，并提出了一种新的方法——ClueRL。该方法通过增强学习策略，集中模型对特征线索的关注度。

Result: 实验结果表明，实体识别是主要瓶颈，通过ClueRL方法，模型在实体识别基准测试上显著提高了性能。此外，通过寻求特征线索的方式，模型在知识问答和跨模态推理任务中的表现也有所提升。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，增强模型对图像中特定特征线索的注意力可有效改进实体识别和知识问答。ClueRL方法不仅在训练任务上表现出色，其特征线索寻求行为甚至扩展到了更广泛的任务，证明了该方法的泛化能力。

Abstract: Answering a knowledge-based visual question requires first recognizing the entity in the image and then recalling knowledge about it. Thinking vision-language models (VLMs) reason for thousands of tokens before answering, yet it remains underexplored how well such thinking VLMs perform each of these two steps. We therefore analyze thinking VLMs on this task and make two key observations. (1) Recognition is the bottleneck, since 76.3% of errors across four models on ViQuAE stem from misrecognizing the entity, and 84.9% of these failures are resolved once the entity name is provided in the question. (2) Recognition is driven by entity-recall clues rather than by general descriptions of the image. An entity-recall clue is a distinctive visual detail that helps the model recall the entity, such as keys, a traditional attribute of Saint Peter. Based on these findings, we propose ClueRL, a reinforcement learning approach that extends Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with an advantage localized to the entity-recall clue in the thinking trace. Rather than injecting new entity knowledge, ClueRL changes how VLMs think: it leads them to attend more closely to visual details and spot these clues, helping them use the knowledge they already have. Consequently, it outperforms both GRPO and a perception-reward baseline on three entity recognition benchmarks. Moreover, this clue-seeking behavior generalizes beyond entity recognition, the only task used for training. Since recognition is the bottleneck, the gains carry over to knowledge-based visual question answering. They even extend to general multimodal reasoning, raising Qwen3.5-4B by 4.4 and 4.0 points on the ScienceQA and MMMU subsets of MMStar, respectively.

</details>


### [150] [Exploring the Design Factors of Pixel-space Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34093)
*Mang Ning,Le Zhang,Albert Ali Salah,Itir Onal Ertugrul,Jintao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EiT通过探索像素空间扩散的关键设计因素，引入了一种U型像素扩散变换器，以此提高图像和视频生成的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过借鉴Denoising Autoencoders (DAE) 的设计理念，并结合像素和潜在空间的扩散机制，EiT 提出了一种新的模型架构，旨在提高图像和视频生成的质量。

Method: EiT 结构采用了U型设计，通过像素尺寸上的瓶颈来实现显式的压缩，并增加网络深度以增强模型的表示能力。此外，EiT 还通过三个不同的层和时间步长阶段来定义表示动力学：去噪/局部特征扩展，基底提升，以及后验投影。

Result: 在 ImageNet 上，EiT 在生成质量方面优于 JiT，特别是在 $256 	imes 256$ 和 $512 	imes 512$ 尺寸上，EiT 在 FID 测试中表现更优异，分别为 1.61 和 1.73，而 JiT 分别为 1.85 和 1.99。此外，通过简单的将补丁大小扩展到时间维度，EiT 还在 Kinetics-600 数据集上实现了对视频潜在扩散的超越，表现出优秀的 FVD 性能。

Conclusion: EiT 模型提供了一种新的图像和视频生成方法，通过优化通道瓶颈和增加网络深度，显著提高了生成质量，并且展示了其在不同应用中（如视频生成）的广泛适应性。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce EiT by exploring the key design factors of pixel-space diffusion from three perspectives: model architecture, representation, and frequency domain. Inspired by Denoising Autoencoders (DAE) which perform denoising through a bottleneck, we construct a U-shaped pixel diffusion transformer that performs explicit compression along the channel dimension. In terms of representation, we find that both pixel and latent diffusion transformers exhibit a three-stage representation dynamics across layers and timesteps: denoising/local feature expansion, basis lift-up, and posterior projection. Moreover, earlier denoising and a longer basis lift-up stage are associated with better generation, which EiT naturally achieves through its channel bottleneck and increased network depth. From the frequency perspective, we establish the equivalence between pixel-space and frequency-space diffusion when keeping the network operating in pixel space. On ImageNet $256\times256$ and $512\times512$, EiT consistently outperforms JiT (Just image Transformers) in generation quality. Specifically, our EiT achieves better FID than JiT: 1.61 vs. 1.85 at $256\times256$, and 1.73 vs. 1.99 at $512\times512$. Remarkably, EiT can be seamlessly applied to video pixel diffusion by simply extending the patch size to temporal dimension and outperforms video latent diffusion in FVD evaluated on Kinetics-600 (resolution 256), under comparable total network parameters. Code is available at https://github.com/forever208/EiT

</details>


### [151] [FoMNet: Adaptive Kernels Meet Contextual Matching for Cross-Modal Homography Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34096)
*Zhuoran Yang,Zhenyu Yi,Runmin Zhang,Linshan Li,Yiming Li,Xiaokai Bai,Xiaohan Zhang,Hui-liang Shen,Si-Yuan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FoMNet提出了一种结合 Vision Foundation Model (VFM) 特征的空间自适应与上下文相关性精炼的新方法，通过 Patch Feature Upsampling (PFU) 和 Contextual Correlation Refinement (CCR)，有效提升了跨模态仿射变换估计的精度，并通过FoMNet-D版本降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多尺度细化方法在可靠对应方面的精度有限，因为匹配特征难以区分接近的候选者。为此，需要一种能够结合视觉基础模型特征的空间自适应和上下文相关性精炼的方法来提高跨模态仿射变换估计的准确性。

Method: FoMNet结合了学习的VFM特征空间自适应和上下文相关性精炼，包括Patch Feature Upsampling (PFU)和Contextual Correlation Refinement (CCR)来改进候选分数，以及FoMNet-D采用双立方插值和多尺度特征关系蒸馏来减少计算成本。

Result: 在五个跨模态数据集上的实验表明，FoMNet在平均角误差（MACE）方面优于其他评估方法，减少误差高达72.7%，而FoMNet-D在保持竞争力的同时将推理延迟减少了34.0%。

Conclusion: FoMNet及其简化版本FoMNet-D为跨模态仿射变换估计提供了有效的方法，通过结合视觉基础模型的特征空间自适应与上下文相关性精炼，提升了准确性并降低了计算成本。

Abstract: Cross-modal homography estimation requires reliable correspondences despite appearance differences between sensing modalities. Multi-scale refinement progressively reduces the search range, but its accuracy remains limited by the ability of matching features to distinguish nearby candidates. The vision foundation model (VFM) provides complementary representations, yet integrating these features through fixed interpolation leaves their spatial aggregation independent of image content and the correspondence objective. We propose the Foundation Model-Enhanced Matching Network (FoMNet), which combines learned spatial adaptation of VFM features with contextual correspondence estimation. Patch Feature Upsampling (PFU) progressively predicts location-specific kernels to aggregate neighboring features on upsampled grids, preparing VFM representations for fusion with CNN features at different matching scales. Contextual Correlation Refinement (CCR) then jointly processes source descriptors and local correlations with spatial context to refine candidate scores before each homography update. To reduce upsampling overhead, we develop FoMNet-D, which replaces PFU with bicubic interpolation and uses multi-scale feature-relation distillation to recover accuracy. Experiments on five cross-modal datasets show that FoMNet achieves the lowest mean average corner error (MACE) among the evaluated methods, reducing MACE by 10.6%-72.7% relative to MCNet. FoMNet-D retains competitive accuracy while reducing inference latency by 34.0% relative to FoMNet.

</details>


### [152] [When Position Favors the Wrong Part: Evaluating Left-Right Discrimination in Semantic Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34100)
*Pingan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究分析了在图像部分位置冲突时，密集描述符的表现。研究发现，在部分位置一致时，MARCO的表现优于DINOv2，但在部分位置冲突时，DINOv2和MARCO的表现都明显下降。通过改变特征图的上采样和池化方法，MARCO在某些条件下表现有所改善。利用奇数分数规则进行候选选择能提高匹配准确率，特别是在部分位置冲突时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在揭示图像部分位置冲突对基于密集描述符的匹配算法的影响，尤其是验证描述符是否能在部分位置冲突的条件下区分正确的部分，并考察这些描述符如何帮助选择匹配坐标。

Method: 研究团队使用SPair-71k数据集测试了不同条件下的表现。首先比较了原始描述符与它们的偶数和奇数部分，在水平镜像和固定主干网络与辅助器的条件下进行了特征上采样实验。此外，通过测试基于奇数分数的候选选择规则，评估候选匹配的改进。最后，对具有监督学习的GeoAware-SC进行了额外测试。

Result: 研究发现，DINOv2在部分位置一致时的准确率从86.66%下降到冲突时的19.63%，而MARCO则从95.27%下降到86.16%。在固定输入几何的条件下，MARCO的奇数准确率在原始网格中为34.12%，经过特征学习上采样和下采样后恢复到了88.84%。基于奇数分数规则选择候选的方法在大部分情况下比原始匹配策略表现更好，特别是在处理部分位置冲突时。

Conclusion: 虽然在部分位置冲突时基于密集描述符的匹配算法表现较差，但通过实验设计可以部分克服这一问题。这表明未来的研究可以在特征处理和候选选择方面挖掘更多潜力，以提高在复杂场景下匹配的准确性。

Abstract: Overall part-discrimination accuracy can conceal failures when image position favors the wrong bilateral part. We examine whether dense descriptors distinguish the correct part under this conflict and whether their discrimination scores help select matching coordinates. We evaluate position agreement and conflict within the same target images and annotated part pairs, using a bounding-box-side score to define the conditions. We compare the original descriptors with their even and odd components under horizontal reflection, examine feature upsampling with the backbone and adapters fixed, and test candidate-selection rules based on odd scores. On SPair-71k, odd discrimination accuracy falls from 86.66% under agreement to 19.63% under conflict for frozen DINOv2, compared with 95.27% to 86.16% for MARCO. With MARCO's input geometry fixed, odd accuracy in box-side conflict was 34.12% on the native grid and 88.84% after learned upsampling and pooling back to that grid. Odd-score rules improve on the original hard-peak matchers overall for DINOv2 and DINOv3 and on conflict queries for MARCO. With MARCO's soft decoder fixed, reranking the cosine peaks changes mean per-pair PCK by +0.47 pp. For supervised GeoAware-SC, using the odd score to select the localization window center yields only 15 net additional correct matches, with 330 corrections and 315 introduced errors. Evaluating discrimination under position conflict reveals failures hidden by aggregate accuracy. Reporting corrections and introduced errors separately also shows whether a discriminative score produces useful matching decisions.

</details>


### [153] [HOG-WAM: Improving Visual Robustness through Future Gradient Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34811)
*Boxuan Dong,Yupeng Qian,Ying Wu,Heshuai Li,Junhao Tan,Zhenxiong Tan,Weijia Wu,Weihao Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HOG-WAM 通过在训练过程中引入未来 Histograms of Oriented Gradients (HOG) 的辅助模块，增强了视觉鲁棒性，实现了在多种场景下的成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型在预测未来观测与动作方面表现出色，但在视觉损坏的影响下，可能会导致不良的策略。因此，研究团队引入了 HOG-WAM，该方法增强了视觉鲁棒性，以确保在噪声条件下模型依然能够产生有效的动作。

Method: HOG-WAM 方法集成了一种未来 HOG 差分变换（DiT）辅助模块，该模块通过不对称注意力机制读取 RGB 特征，形成用于控制的视觉表示。该模块仅用于训练，在推理过程中被移除。这种架构不仅保持了 FastWAM 的 RGB 到动作的结构，还能从演示图像中提取更多的结构监督。

Result: HOG-WAM 在四个控制观察损坏的测试中获得了最高的成功率。与 FastWAM 相比，混合噪声的成功率从 78.86% 提高到 82.86%。此外，在 LIBERO Plus 上的整体成功率为 74.49% 提高到 75.26%，特别是在传感器噪声下，成功从 74.14% 提高到 78.89%。平均而言，在四个 Franka 任务中，成功从干净观测下的 40% 提高到 59%，在噪声观测下从 17% 提高到 24%。

Conclusion: HOG-WAM 在改进视觉鲁棒性方面取得了积极成果，特别是在物理任务中具有更大绝对增益，高于仿真实验。研究还表明，结合 RGB 观测与辅助 HOG 预测的效果优于完全用 HOG 替换 RGB 现象。

Abstract: World-action models jointly predict future observations and actions, yet visual corruption can disrupt the resulting policies. We introduce HOG-WAM, which adds future Histograms of Oriented Gradients (HOG) as structural supervision derived directly from demonstration images. An auxiliary HOG diffusion transformer (DiT) module reads RGB features through asymmetric attention, allowing its objective to shape the visual representation used for control. The module is used only during training and removed at inference, preserving FastWAM's RGB-to-action architecture. We evaluate four controlled observation corruptions through a visual-corruption extension of RoboTwin, termed RoboTwin-Plus; HOG-WAM achieves the highest success among the compared policies under all four. Mixed-noise success increases from 78.86% to 82.86% relative to FastWAM, with comparable performance on clean scenes. On LIBERO Plus, overall success increases from 74.49% to 75.26%, with the largest gain under sensor noise, from 74.14% to 78.89%. Across four Franka tasks, average success increases from 40% to 59% under clean observations and from 17% to 24% under noisy observations. A representation ablation favors combining RGB observations with auxiliary HOG prediction over replacing RGB with HOG. These findings support future-gradient prediction as complementary supervision for visually robust control, with larger absolute gains on physical tasks than in simulation. Code is available at https://github.com/iPPLab/HOG-WAM.

</details>


### [154] [What Makes a Gavagai? Learning Visual Concepts from One Positive and Few Negatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34105)
*Katsuya Ogata,Kazuya Nishimura,Yuta Nakashima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过Gavagai基准测试，探讨视觉-语言模型如何利用正负样本识别新颖概念，揭示了模型在有限证据下识别属性和推理方面的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人类如何仅通过少量观察即能识别新概念，以及视觉和推理在这一过程中的作用，尤其是在面对不确定性和竞争假设时。

Method: 建立Gavagai基准，包含合成图像和真实新物种图像，混合使用正负样本，评估模型的性能。

Result: 部分模型即便正确给出属性也难以准确识别概念，而使用模型预测属性则会进一步加大差距，表明无论是属性识别还是从少量证据中进行推理，模型都存在困难。

Conclusion: 揭示了视觉-语言模型识别复杂概念时的关键挑战，并强调了进一步改进的方向。

Abstract: Humans can recognize novel concepts from only a few observations by identifying defining visual attributes and eliminating alternative interpretations. However, a single observation is consistent with multiple possible concepts, and accurate visual perception alone is insufficient to determine the intended interpretation. Negative evidence can reduce this ambiguity by eliminating competing hypotheses. Inspired by Quine's gavagai problem, we introduce the Gavagai Benchmark to evaluate whether vision-language models can identify the attributes defining a novel concept from positive and negative examples. The benchmark consists of synthetic images depicting novel combinations of visual attributes and real images of newly described species. Using these datasets, we examine how models use negative examples to identify novel concepts and analyze the respective roles of visual perception and reasoning. Several models struggle even when correct attributes are provided, while others show large gaps when ground-truth attributes are replaced with model predictions. These diagnostic comparisons highlight challenges in both attribute perception and inference from limited evidence.

</details>


### [155] [Solve Vision with Code: Coding Agents Are Stronger Visual Reasoners Than Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34813)
*Dezhi Luo,Fengyuan Yu,Haotian Zhang,Zehong Zhao,Ran Ji,Siyuan An,Huayi Qin,Qingying Gao,Zhongang Cai,Lei Yang,Ziqi Huang,Ruisi Wang,Maijunxian Wang,Hokin Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究对比了视频生成模型和编程生成的视觉推理，发现对于从初始帧和提示推断答案的任务，程序合成在当前更为可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自然语言提示和初始帧下评估视频生成模型与编程生成模型的视觉推理能力，认为传统上视觉推理在视频中直观展现优于语言或代码，但未对比符号推理系统的可靠性。

Method: 设计VBVR-Pro-Bench基准测试，包括来自五个认知分类的500个实例，通过相同的输入条件，让两种类型的模型——视频模型和编码代理进行评估。

Result: 编码代理在闭合模型和开放模型中分别以0.923、0.894和0.893的得分显著高于视频模型；在开放模型中，仅一个模型GLM-5得分高于视频模型，总体表明程序合成是更可靠的视觉推理方法。

Conclusion: 研究表明，程序合成对于从初始帧和提示生成答案的任务，是目前比视频生成更为可靠的视觉推理手段，并进一步区分了表征型程序和命题型程序，以揭示视觉推理的本质。

Abstract: Video generation models are increasingly evaluated as visual reasoners: given a first frame and a natural-language prompt, can the model produce the video in which the specified task is carried out? The paradigm is considered advantageous because the reasoning is native to the visual medium, unfolding directly in the frames rather than through language or code. Whether this native route is in fact the more reliable path to visual reasoning has not been tested against a symbolic alternative under the same conditions. We therefore pose the same tasks, with identical inputs, output specification and evaluator, to both media: rather than denoising pixels, a coding agent writes a program that renders the answer video, which an unmodified rule-based evaluator scores. On VBVR-Pro-Bench (Xu et al., 2026), a 500-instance suite spanning five cognitive categories with symbolic ground truth, the agent receives the same first frame and prompt as the video model, with no access to ground truth, metadata or generation models, and one attempt per instance. Three closed-model agents (Codex with gpt-6-astra, Claude Code with Fable 5.1 and Gemini CLI with Gemini 3.1 Pro) score 0.923, 0.894 and 0.893, each more than 0.22 above the strongest video model. Among 23 runnable open-weight models, the best, GLM-5, reaches 0.572, above all video models except the RL-trained one. Together, we show that for tasks whose solutions can be computed from a first frame and a prompt, program synthesis is currently a substantially more reliable route to visual reasoning than video generation. We further distinguish depictive programs, which navigate the image to recover a transferable scheme, from propositional programs, which operate on hard-coded values, revealing how visual reasoning unfolds in agent-written programs and what it implies for its nature of reasoning in general.

</details>


### [156] [Detectable by Design: Learning a Continuous 3D Saliency Field from 2D Detector Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34108)
*Han Sun,Jun Li,Christy Jie Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DetField 提出了一种基于图像检测能力的 3D 关键点兴趣图，优于现有的 3D 关键点检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 3D 关键点检测方法倾向于从语义、可重复性、重建或描述符匹配能力的角度定义显著性，但忽略了这些点在图像中实际被检测到的可能性，因此选择的大多数点在实际图像中并未被检测到。DetField 定义了 3D 显著性为图像可检测性，即在可见视图中 2D 兴趣点检测器在表面点处的响应。

Method: DetField 是一个连续的 3D 空间中的特征图，它接受点云作为输入，并预测任意位置的检测能力。通过将 2D 检测器在图像上的响应反投影到点云上来进行监督，并通过解码器传递多尺度曲率不变量，跳过编码器的体素池化。

Result: 在隔离了 3D 点选择的定位基准测试中，DetField 在对象和真实的室内场景上优于 Harris-3D 和五个已学习的 3D 关键点方法。在未见过的房间中，它比 Harris-3D 提高了 7.4 个定位成功率。对于物体，它还在未使用的 2D 检测器下领先。在稀疏地图上，它在对象和真实场景中都保持了对 Harris-3D 的领先。

Conclusion: DetField 提出的 3D 显著性定义和方法为 3D 场景中的相机定位提供了新的视角，通过改善点的选择提高了定位的准确性。

Abstract: The 3D points kept in a map decide whether a camera can be localized against it, and this choice matters more as the map becomes sparser. Existing 3D keypoint detectors usually define saliency by semantics, repeatability, reconstruction or descriptor matchability. None of these definitions asks whether a point will actually be detected in an image, and as a result most of the points they select are not detected again in images.
  In this paper, we define 3D saliency as image detectability: the response of a 2D interest-point detector at a surface point, aggregated over all views in which the point is visible. We propose DetField, a continuous field over 3D space that takes a point cloud as input and predicts detectability at any location around it. The supervision is the response of a 2D detector, back-projected from images onto the point cloud. Multi-scale curvature invariants are passed to the decoder at every query point, bypassing the voxel pooling of the encoder.
  In a localization benchmark that isolates the choice of 3D points, DetField outperforms Harris-3D and five learned 3D keypoint methods on objects and on real indoor scenes. On unseen rooms, it improves localization success by 7.4 points over Harris-3D, the strongest baseline. On objects, it also leads under a 2D detector not used in training. On sparse maps, it stays ahead of Harris-3D on both objects and real scenes.

</details>


### [157] [A Simple Yet Effective Agent for Open-World Aerial Object Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34115)
*Jitai Hao,Liangxing Yang,Zhenyang Li,Fan Liu,Qiang Huang,Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种不需要特定任务训练且使用固定视觉语言模型的简易无人机搜索代理SimpleUAV，在UAV-ON和AerialDojo-200K基准上分别实现了41.61%和11.98%的成功率，显著优于先前的最先进方法。进一步转移到Aerial VLN任务后性能提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在评估在无需特定任务训练及使用现成视觉语言模型的前提下，简易无人机搜索代理的性能上限，探索其在实际场景中的潜力。

Method: SimpleUAV 运用了当前视角的目标评估与历史条件下的行动选择分离的机制，结合即时候选证据和轻量级搜索上下文。它使用局部几何学将视觉选择转化为有界直线运动，基于深度检查中断堵塞路径，并使用度量执行反馈指导后续决策，形成了一个闭环以保持搜索而不依赖持久的空间地图或特定任务训练。

Result: 在UAV-ON基准上，SimpleUAV实现了41.61%的成功率，而先前最强方法仅达到23.80%。在AerialDojo-200K基准上，SimpleUAV实现了11.98%的成功率，而最强方法仅为3.20%。通过最少基准特定的适应，SimpleUAV在TravelUAV上的全成功率至少提高了19.66%。

Conclusion: 此研究确立了评估额外架构复杂性对于提升无人机搜索性能的重要性的基准，同时也展示了在无需特定任务训练及使用现成视觉语言模型条件下，简易无人机搜索代理的潜在表现。

Abstract: Open-world aerial object search methods often rely on persistent spatial maps, learned world models, or task-specific policy training. To examine how far a minimal, training-free design can go, we introduce SimpleUAV, an agent built around a frozen, off-the-shelf vision-language model (VLM). SimpleUAV separates current-view target assessment from history-conditioned action selection, combining fresh candidate evidence with lightweight search context. Local geometry converts visual selections into bounded straight-line movements, depth-based checks halt blocked segments, and measured execution feedback guides subsequent decisions. This closed loop sustains search without persistent spatial maps or task-specific training. SimpleUAV achieves 41.61% Success Rate (SR) on UAV-ON and 11.98% on AerialDojo-200K, compared with 23.80% and 3.20% for the strongest prior baselines, respectively. With limited benchmark-specific adaptation, the same core agent transfers to aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) on TravelUAV, improving Full SR by at least 19.66% across all three splits under L1 assistance. These results establish a strong baseline for evaluating when additional architectural complexity benefits aerial search.

</details>


### [158] [Probing Visual Idiosyncrasies in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34843)
*Yongqian Peng,Hongjie Li,Yichao Zhou,Chi Zhang,Yixin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究发现，尽管T2I模型生成的图像视觉质量高，但对于新型的ResNet-50模型而言，这些图像仍然可以被正确识别来源，准确性达到100%，而人类参与者只能达到62.61%，暗示存在不易察觉的特征差异，这些特征可以通过低级纹理统计、物体布局、深度、反射率及图像描述中的特定偏好体现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨T2I模型生成的高质量图像是否仍保持模型的独特特征，以及这些特征如何影响模型间的具体评估。

Method: 通过对比四个顶尖的开源T2I模型生成的图像，使用ResNet-50和人类参与者进行识别实验。

Result: ResNet-50能够100%准确地区分四个T2I模型生成的图像，而人类识别率仅为62.61%。初步分析显示，这些特征源自纹理统计、物体布局、深度和反射率，以及图像描述中的特定喜好。

Conclusion: 研究指出，T2I模型的输出图像具有显著的视觉差异，即使在生成高质量图像后这些差异仍会被捕捉到，这表明在评估T2I模型时需要考虑这种差异性。

Abstract: Text-to-Image (T2I) models turn short text descriptions into images, and the leading models now produce images of comparable visual quality. Whether their outputs still carry model-specific characteristics matters for attribution, for model comparison beyond aggregate quality, and for evaluations that assume anonymity. We ask whether images from frontier T2I models can still be classified by source, and which visual properties make this possible. Here we show that a standard ResNet-50 identifies the source of an image among HiDream-O1-Dev, FLUX.2-dev, HunyuanImage-3.0, and Qwen-Image-2512, the four highest-ranked open-source models on the Text-to-Image Arena, with 100.00% accuracy on held-out prompts. Human participants reach only 62.61% on the pairwise version of this task, so the distinguishing cues are not visually obvious. They are also not tied to a particular classifier and survive severe image corruptions and out-of-distribution prompts. We trace them to visual idiosyncrasies at three levels. Low-level texture statistics alone let a lightweight multi-layer perceptron match ResNet-50. Object layout, depth, and albedo estimated from the images each carry a source signal. Image captions reveal model-specific preferences in style and background that the prompts never specify. A one-sentence hint about these cues raises human accuracy by 9.03 percentage points. These idiosyncrasies make source classification a tool for measuring similarity between models and between real and generated images, and they caution against assuming anonymity in human preference evaluation of T2I models.

</details>


### [159] [BOP-Refer: Benchmark for Monocular 3D and 2D Object Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34862)
*Tomas Hodan,Vineet Bhat,Martin Sundermeyer,Jonathan Tremblay,Yash Patel,Médéric Fourmy,Stephen Tyree,Junwen Huang,Varun Burde,Martin Cífka,Eric Brachmann,Vincent Lepetit,Stan Birchfield,Jiri Matas,Carsten Rother*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BOP-Refer 是首个用于日常及工业对象的单目 3D 和 2D 描述目标基准，基于 9 个不同数据集。使用 VLM 生成和人工验证的参考表达式，得出了准确的目标 3D 和 2D 边缘框。评估 17 种顶点 VLM 模型，最佳模型 Gemini 4 Carbon 在 2D 方面表现良好，但在 3D 方面较差。主要错误来源是目标的深度误差，可能需要可靠的物体尺寸先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏一个综合评估单目 3D 和 2D 目标检测能力的标准，BOP-Refer 的引入填补了这一空白，提供了更为全面的评估平台。

Method: 基于多个 BOP 数据集，通过 VLM 生成和人工验证的描述表达式与 3D 模型和 6DoF 姿势注解相结合，生成准确的目标边界框。

Result: 17 种 VLM 模型中，Gemini 4 Carbon 是最好的，但在 2D 检测中得分 72.6% AP_IoU2D，在 3D 检测中仅得 12.4% AP_IoU3D。

Conclusion: 虽然 Gemini 4 Carbon 在 2D 检测中表现良好，但在 3D 检测中的深度误差表明，需要更好的尺度估计，特别是需要可靠的物体尺寸先验。

Abstract: We introduce BOP-Refer, the first benchmark for monocular 3D and 2D grounding of everyday and industrial objects sourced from 9 diverse BOP datasets. We pair images from these datasets with VLM-generated and human-verified referring expressions, and derive accurate ground-truth 3D and 2D bounding boxes of the referred objects from their 3D models and 6DoF pose annotations. Our systematic evaluation of 17 frontier VLMs across the major proprietary and open-weight families (Gemini, GPT, Claude, Qwen, Gemma) shows that the best model, Gemini 4 Carbon, reaches 72.6% AP_IoU2D in 2D but only 12.4% AP_IoU3D in 3D, despite the more lenient IoU thresholds in 3D. The predicted 3D boxes are usually centered on the target objects in the image but often off in depth by more than the object's size, suggesting that metric scale estimation, which needs reliable object size priors, is currently by far the main source of error.

</details>


### [160] [A Calibration-Referenced Protocol for Measuring Decision Stability under Reference Bank Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34134)
*Yiwen Chen,Shengming Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个四步协议，比较了在匹配部署预算下不同压缩方法的效果，建立了一个基于200次重新校准的参考分布，评估了与两种校准基准的分歧，并衡量了与全库阈值的距离。结果表明，在VisA和MVTec AD数据集上，预算匹配下的产品量化(PQ)在10%预算下对WideResNet-50的最佳决策分歧表现优于其他方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了优化工业图像检测中参考银行的存储和决策准确性，作者提出了一种方法来评估在不同预算限制下的压缩方法效果。

Method: 该协议包括四步：1. 比较压缩方法在匹配的部署规模下，2. 从200次重新校准中建立参考分布，3. 与两种校准基准进行比较以评估分歧，4. 计量与全库阈值的距离。

Result: 在VisA和MVTec AD数据集上，预算匹配的产品量化(PQ)在10%预算下对WideResNet-50的表现最好，与其他方法相比决策分歧最小。Int8标量量化在其他四个预算下给出了较低的分歧。

Conclusion: 论文研究表明，在预算限制条件下，产品量化(PQ)是最佳的压缩方法，特别是在ResNet-18和参考库清洁的情况下。

Abstract: Reference banks used for industrial image inspection can be compressed to fit a deployment budget, but the resulting anomaly decisions depend on both the compressed scores and the operating threshold. We present a four-step protocol that compares compression methods at matched deployment sizes, establishes a reference distribution from 200 recalibrations, evaluates disagreement against two calibration benchmarks, and measures changes by distance from the full-bank threshold. With WideResNet-50, budget-matched product quantization (PQ) at the 10% budget has the lowest decision disagreement among five matched methods, including int8 scalar quantization, on VisA and MVTec AD at both operating levels. Int8 scalar quantization gives lower disagreement than PQ at the other four budgets. On MVTec AD at the lower operating level, PQ meets the calibration median in 34/39 cells and the upper quantile in 39/39 cells. Category-equally weighted image-level AUROC loss at this budget ranges from 0.0003 to 0.0037 across the evaluated configurations. On VisA at the 1% budget and a 1% operating level, disagreement reaches 28.12% (95% CI 23.25--33.58%) among near-threshold anomalies and 1.30% (0.79--2.14%) across the normal holdout. The 10% budget-matched PQ setting retains the lowest disagreement among four compared methods with ResNet-18, with clean reference banks, and when thresholds are estimated from normal-directory probes. The protocol connects deployment size to a calibration-referenced decision measure and identifies the evaluation populations most informative for inspection.

</details>


### [161] [Enhancing Maritime Domain Awareness using Latent Space Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34899)
*Rikard Vinge,Miguel A. Belenguer-Plomer,Isabelle Wittmann,Octavian Dumitru,Michele Lazzarini,Sergio Albani,Conrad M Albrecht*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究利用地球观测嵌入技术直接处理Sentinel-1压缩潜空间表示来增强海上态势感知，通过线性和非线性探针检测船舶，分类船舶AIS类型代码，并发布了一个新的海洋遥感数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究针对操作EO处理中日益严重的数据引力瓶颈，通过使用GeoFMs直接处理压缩的潜空间表示，旨在提升海上态势感知的质量。

Method: 研究利用线性和非线性探针对压缩的SAR影像潜空间表示进行探针，使用这些探针来检测、定位和描述船舶，并构建了新的数据集以支持实验。

Result: 研究实现了船舶检测的ROC-AUC为0.95，长于100米的船舶定位误差中位数约为300米，船舶AIS类型代码分类准确率为62%（主要类别），但与船舶长度高度相关。

Conclusion: 研究展示了GeoFMs在实际应用场景中进行精细粒度的海上态势感知的潜力和局限性，并引入了可用于与NeuCo-Bench神经压缩基准框架兼容的新数据集。

Abstract: This work presents a novel application of Earth Observation (EO) embeddings for Maritime Domain Awareness (MDA) from the perspective of the geospatial foundation model TerraMind. By operating directly on compressed latent-space representations rather than on the original Sentinel-1 imagery, this approach directly addresses the data gravity bottleneck that increasingly constrains operational EO processing, in line with the objectives of the Embed2Scale project. In particular, linear and non-linear probing of compressed embeddings, latent space representations of synthetic aperture radar (SAR) imagery, is used to detect, localise, and characterise vessels from Sentinel-1 GRD images. Vessel detection achieves a ROC-AUC of 0.95, while vessel localisation has a median error of around 300 m for vessels longer than 100 m, and vessel AIS type-code classification achieves 62% accuracy for the majority classes. The latter, however, is highly correlated with vessel length. These results reveal both the potential and current limitations of Geospatial Foundation Models (GeoFMs) when applied to fine-grained MDA understanding in real-world scenarios.
  Additionally, a new maritime remote sensing dataset is released, pairing about 2,000 Sentinel-1 images with temporally aligned AIS signals from commercial vessels. Through manual inspection, each candidate sample was verified for bounding-box quality and for consistency between the AIS-reported vessel length and the observed vessel extent, cross-checking vessel identity and location against publicly available vessel records and, where available, photographs. The dataset was made ready for use with the NeuCo-Bench neural compression benchmark framework, publicly available on HuggingFace through https://huggingface.co/datasets/embed2scale/S1-single-ship-downstream .

</details>


### [162] [An Image Detectability Field as a Viewpoint Utility for 2D-3D Localization in Sparse Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34139)
*Han Sun,Jun Li,Christy Jie Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图像可检测性域的方法来选择视点，以提高3D-2D匹配的准确性。在合成数据集和真实室内场景中，该方法在稀疏地标下表现出色，而在稠密地标下表现与Harris-3D相仿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在2D-3D定位中，相机姿态估计依赖于图像与3D地图的匹配。当前研究旨在考察是否可以在地图的几何结构基础上预测图像的探测可能性，从而优化视点的选择。

Method: 该方法通过对3D点附近局部几何结构进行分析，预测其投影在2D图像上被探测到的可能性，形成一个图像可检测性域。该域在检测点选择视点时无需进行细调，直接用于指导基于SuperPoint和EPnP-RANSAC的局部化过程。

Result: 该方法在合成ShapeNet对象和稀疏点地图中展示了显著的优势，相较于简单的地标计数提高了约2到3个定位成功率点。然而，在真实室内场景和稠密地标中，该方法的表现不如Harris-3D，并提出了添加了域的局部地标选择策略以改进表现。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，图像可检测性域可以作为视点选择的有用工具，特别是在标记稀疏的情况下，但对于稠密地标则需要进一步的优化。

Abstract: In 2D-3D localization, a camera estimates its pose by matching an image to a 3D map. A camera held in a hand or mounted on a robot can often choose its viewpoint before it takes the image. In this work, we ask whether an image detectability field is a useful viewpoint utility when the planner reads only the geometry of the map. The field can be queried at any 3D point near a surface. From the local geometry around the point, it predicts how likely a 2D image detector is to fire at the projection of the point. We use the trained field as it is, without fine-tuning it for view selection. A fixed SuperPoint and EPnP-RANSAC localizer then estimates the pose from the chosen view. We answer in two settings, each with a test registered before the runs. On synthetic ShapeNet objects with sparse point maps, summing the field over the visible landmarks beats counting them by +2.0 points of localization success rate (object-clustered 95% interval [+0.7, +3.2]). It also beats summing Harris-3D ranks by +2.2 [+0.8, +3.5]. On real indoor scenes from SceneFun3D, our adaptation of the learned view scorer LWL falls below counting on the test rooms. We therefore add the field to a learned per-landmark planner. On the test rooms with sparse maps whose landmarks the field also selects, the planner with the field beats the same planner without it by +1.8 points. It beats the planner with Harris-3D or curvature in its place by +1.0 and +0.8 points. All three room-clustered intervals lie above zero. In both settings, the field helps in sparse maps and is on par with Harris-3D in dense maps.

</details>


### [163] [Benefit of Structured Representations: When Equivariance Helps in Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34140)
*Florian A. Hölzl,Daniel Rueckert,Georgios Kaissis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 文章提出了一种基于四分之一转角旋转组（C4）的旋转不变卷积网络ConvNeXt-C4，在减少参数和计算量的同时，相比基础模型在所有任务上表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流的视觉编码器很少将不变性应用于大部分几何对称性，本文通过以四分之一转角旋转组（C4）为代表的研究，探讨几何先验对视觉编码器的作用。

Method: 研究了四分之一转角旋转组（C4），构建了基于该旋转组的旋转不变卷积神经网络ConvNeXt-C4，并通过从零开始训练的实验展示了其优势。

Result: ConvNeXt-C4在所有任务上表现优于基础模型ConvNeXt，同时参数量减少6倍，计算量减少1.6倍。其性能优势随着训练数据减少呈对数增长，预训练对两版本模型均有相似的性能提升。

Conclusion: 结果表明旋转不变性具有样本效率优势，保留的方向信息可以显著提高性能，文章鼓励在图像分类之外的应用进行进一步探索。

Abstract: Equivariant neural networks promise improved representation learning through structural guarantees. However, mainstream vision encoders rarely incorporate equivariance for most geometric symmetries. Images are a particularly interesting modality with many such symmetries and extensive datasets from which to learn this structure. In this work, we investigate when vision encoders benefit from geometric inductive biases. We study the discrete rotation group of quarter-turns (C4) which acts losslessly without interpolation or boundary artifacts. Combined with image classification as a controlled invariant objective, this allows for a principled empirical investigation into the role of structural guarantees. We introduce ConvNeXt-C4 as a rotation equivariant ConvNeXt with dimension-equivalent yet orientation-structured representations. Training from scratch, our experiments show, that the C4 variant outperforms its base model across all tasks while operating with 6x fewer parameters and 1.6x fewer FLOPs. Its performance advantage increases log-linearly with decreasing training set size, with pre-training providing a similar improvement across both model variants. Analyzing the classifier head reveals that orientation information from the representations' structure is actively used, improving performance by 4.90% across all datasets. Our results highlight the sample-efficiency benefits of equivariance and the value of retained orientation information, motivating further investigation beyond image classification. Code is available at https://github.com/hlzl/equivariant-convnext.

</details>


### [164] [Spe3DUp: Faithful Trajectory Distillation for Fast Image-to-3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34141)
*Liangchao Xu,Jiaye Fu,Youtian Lin,Feihu Zhang,Yao Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Spe3DUp通过引入Fine-to-Coarse框架（基于Faithful Trajectory Distillation，FTD）实现快速且准确的3D生成，提升效率同时保持几何细节和输入对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速3D生成过程，同时保持高几何质量和输入参考物的对应关系，适用于游戏资产重建和产品数字化等应用。

Method: Spe3DUp采用了细到粗的方法，首先通过 Faithful Trajectory Distillation (FTD) 监督生成过程，然后分别应用于粗态几何生成和几何细化，同时提高了几何解码和并行重网格化效率。

Result: 实验结果表明，Spe3DUp在保持教师模型的几何质量和输入对应关系的情况下，分别加快了粗态和细化生成阶段10.0倍和19.9倍，并且整个流程端到端加速了7.7倍。

Conclusion: Spe3DUp通过优化生成过程中的监督和加速技术，提高了3D生成的效率和准确性，为3D模型在各领域的应用提供了潜在的优势。

Abstract: Recent 3D generative models can produce high-quality meshes with complex structures and fine geometric details, with broad applications in games, film, and digital design. However, high-quality generation typically relies on long multi-step sampling processes that repeatedly evaluate large networks, leading to long inference times. At the same time, reference-driven tasks such as game-asset reconstruction and product digitization demand close correspondence to the input image: generated geometry must faithfully reconstruct the specific reference object. Acceleration must therefore reduce sampling computation while preserving the model's ability to reconstruct that object. To enable fast and faithful 3D generation, we introduce Spe3DUp, a coarse-to-fine framework based on Faithful Trajectory Distillation (FTD). FTD directly supervises few-step students with the teacher's input-conditioned denoising trajectories to preserve its generation behavior. We apply FTD separately to coarse geometry generation and geometric refinement, compressing both sampling processes while maintaining correspondence to the reference input. We further accelerate the complete mesh-generation pipeline with efficient geometry decoding and parallel remeshing. Experiments show that Spe3DUp maintains geometric quality and input correspondence close to those of the teacher, accelerates the coarse and refinement DiTs by 10.0x and 19.9x, respectively, and achieves a 7.7x end-to-end speedup over the original teacher pipeline.

</details>


### [165] [Non-Linear Motion from Two Frames: Cubic Hermite Trajectories for Video Frame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34143)
*Md Rifat Ur Rahman,Md Abtahi Majeed Chowdhury,Md Sakib Hossain Shovon,Jaesik Choi,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HermiteVFI 提出了一种新的双帧视频帧插值方法，通过两个端点导数作为中间路径的控制点，利用三次Hermite曲线生成流，相较于传统方法能更好地捕捉复杂非均匀运动，提高插值质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了克服传统双帧视频帧插值方法中采用恒定速度直线路径的不足，尤其是面对复杂非均匀运动时的表现不佳。

Method: HermiteVFI 方法采用了一种新的基于三次Hermite曲线的技术来生成插值中的运动流。通过测量的两个端点位移固定两个路径的位置，而两个从帧对中学习到的端点导数控制中间路径。该方法结构上保留了测量到的端点位移，并允许在任意查询时间点进行预测。

Result: HermiteVFI 方法在 X4K1000FPS、SNU-FILM 和 Vimeo90K-Septuplet 数据集上，特别是在复杂非均匀运动和高插值比的情况下，表现出了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: HermiteVFI 对运动轨迹系数的预测进行了参数化，而后续的逆转、精化和合成过程仍然依赖于查询。该研究促进了对复杂运动序列的更好理解和建模。

Abstract: Two-frame video frame interpolation commonly resamples endpoint optical flow by the query time, which selects a constant-velocity straight-line path even though the same endpoint displacement is compatible with many intermediate trajectories. We present HermiteVFI, which replaces independent query-time motion prediction with one explicit continuous trajectory per pixel. The measured endpoint displacement fixes the two trajectory positions, while two latent endpoint derivatives learned from the frame pair control the interior path. A cubic Hermite curve is the minimum-degree polynomial that can independently satisfy these two position and two derivative boundary quantities. Its residual form contains linear motion exactly, structurally preserves the measured displacement endpoints for any network output, and has coefficients that are predicted once per frame pair and evaluated in closed form at arbitrary t. We further show that one interior query time gives a rank-deficient constraint on the two free trajectory coefficients, whereas any two distinct interior times are full rank at the representation level, motivating multi-time supervision. Appearance-derived directional cues help infer the latent derivatives that endpoint displacement alone cannot identify. On X4K1000FPS, SNU-FILM, and Vimeo90K-Septuplet, the largest gains occur for difficult non-uniform motion and high interpolation ratios. The amortization applies to trajectory-coefficient prediction; downstream reversal, refinement, and synthesis remain query dependent.

</details>


### [166] [HSTTA: Hierarchical Selective Test-Time Adaptation for Label-Free Cross-Subject EEG-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34153)
*Hongzhou Chen,Yuhao Wei,Lianghua He,Yihang Liu,Zengqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HSTTA 提出了一种新的框架，通过建立图像嵌入的层级结构和选择性预测来适应新主题的 EEG 到图像检索，有效提高了预测准确性和模型更新的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的 Test-time Adaptation 方法难以为每个查询提供准确的伪标签，导致新主题的性能下降。

Method: HSTTA 利用图像嵌入构建层级结构，进行选择性预测，并根据预测置信度为每个 EEG 查询分配合适的监督粒度，同时引入了用于校准层级预测全局置信阈值的 CSCC，以及用于减少错误强化的 RHB。

Result: HSTTA 在两个公开基准上取得了优异的性能，在 THINGS-EEG2 中的 Top-1/Top-5 准确率分别提高了 17.9(13.4) 和 8.9(3.7) 个百分点，且在适应过程中提供更准确的伪标签，支持更可靠的模型更新。

Conclusion: HSTTA 及其关键组件有效地解决了 EEG 到图像检索中新主题适应的挑战，提供了更准确的预测和更可靠的模型训练。

Abstract: Cross-subject EEG-to-image retrieval suffers from substantial performance degradation on new subjects due to subject shift. Test-time adaptation (TTA) can mitigate this shift by adapting pretrained models with unlabeled target-subject EEG. However, most existing TTA methods struggle to match supervision granularity to each query's prediction confidence, leaving many queries without reliable pseudo-labels. To address this, we propose a novel framework named Hierarchical Selective Test-Time Adaptation (HSTTA). HSTTA builds a hierarchy over gallery image embeddings, uses hierarchical selective prediction to assign each EEG query a supervision granularity matching its current prediction confidence, and constructs adaptation objectives accordingly. We further propose Cross-Session Consistency Calibration (CSCC), an unsupervised method that calibrates the global confidence threshold to target a predefined accuracy level for hierarchical predictions. Moreover, we introduce Reciprocal Hierarchical Backoff (RHB), which moves queries lacking reverse-matching support to coarser nodes to mitigate error reinforcement. Experiments on two public benchmarks and ablation studies validate the effectiveness of HSTTA and its key components. On THINGS-EEG2, HSTTA outperforms the strongest EEG-specific TTA baseline by 17.9 (13.4) and 8.9 (3.7) percentage points in Top-1/Top-5 accuracy, respectively, under cosine (CSLS) ranking. Further analyses show that HSTTA yields more accurate pseudo-labels during adaptation, supporting more reliable model updates.

</details>


### [167] [OceanFix: Reference-Guided Diffusion for Sparse-View Underwater 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34155)
*Gen Li,Maoxin Chen,Yao Xiao,Xinyang Li,Yu Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OceanFix 提出了一种参考导向的稀-view 海下三维重建框架，结合了扩散基础视角修复与高斯场景细化，通过迭代渲染、修复和细化，改善了稀疏视角下的重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对海下重建过程中因稀疏观测、光线衰减和回散带来的问题，提出了一种新的方法来提升稀疏视角下海下3D重建的质量。

Method: 该方法分为两个阶段训练。第一阶段学习海下外观和介质特征，第二阶段通过相机姿态和参考深度对齐参考特征，并使用相对深度监督进行结构修复。此外，还提出了一种介质感知蒸馏方法，用于将修复后的视图转移至3D场景中。

Result: 通过多次渲染、修复和蒸馏，OceanFix 在稀疏视角下的海下3D重建中改善了渲染质量，减少了可见伪影并恢复了更精细的场景细节。

Conclusion: OeanFix 方法在多个海下数据集上验证了其有效性，证明了其在稀疏视角下海下3D重建中的优势。

Abstract: Recent advances in Gaussian splatting have enabled efficient underwater reconstruction, but sparse observations, light attenuation, and backscatter still leave scene regions underconstrained and cause novel-view artifacts. We present OceanFix, a reference-guided framework for sparse-view underwater 3D reconstruction that combines diffusion-based view repair with Gaussian scene refinement. Our repair model follows a two-stage training strategy that separates underwater appearance adaptation from geometric refinement. The first stage learns underwater appearance from reference observations and estimated medium features. The second uses camera poses and reference depth to align reference features, with relative-depth supervision guiding structural repair without requiring target-view depth at inference. We further propose medium-aware distillation that transfers repaired views into the 3D scene according to geometric repair demand and channel-specific optical observability. Through iterative rendering, repair, and distillation, OceanFix refines underconstrained regions while retaining supervision from the observed views. Experiments on underwater datasets demonstrate that OceanFix improves rendering quality in sparse-view 3D reconstruction, reducing visible artifacts and recovering finer scene details.

</details>


### [168] [Modality-Shift Contrastive Decoding for Temporally Grounded Audio-Visual Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34162)
*Minyeong Jeong,Yeonju Kim,Yong Man Ro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了AV-LLMs对音频-视频同步任务的处理能力，并提出了一种称为Modality-Shift Contrastive Decoding (MSCD)的新方法来改善音频-视频时间对齐，从而提高了基准测试中的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AV-LLMs在处理音频-视频同步任务时对时间对齐的敏感性有限。为此，研究者们开发了MSCD方法以提高模型对音频-视频时间对齐的敏感度。

Method: 研究者通过将音频进行时间上的扰动，使得查询的声音与不同的视觉事件重叠，来测试模型的音频-视频时间对齐能力。同时，他们提出了MSCD方法，通过对比在保持和破坏时间对齐的情况下生成的logits，使模型更加倾向于支持音频-视频对齐的预测。

Result: 在两个AV-LLMs上进行了广泛的评估，结果显示MSCD方法显著提高了基于时间对齐的音频-视频理解的准确性。特别是，与其他基线模型相比，MSCD方法在Daily-Omni基准上的准确率分别提高了2.2和2.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究结论表明，强调时间对齐可以显著提高更广泛的音频-视频理解效果，证明了MSCD方法的有效性。

Abstract: Audio-visual large language models (AV-LLMs) are designed to represent the temporal structure of audio and video, yet it remains unclear how effectively they use this temporal information to relate sounds to visual events. To examine this, we probe an AV-LLM on an audio-visual synchronization task by shifting the audio so that the queried sound overlaps a different visual event. We observe that the model still favors the originally aligned event over the newly aligned one, indicating limited sensitivity to changes in audio-visual temporal alignment. To address this, we propose Modality-Shift Contrastive Decoding (MSCD), a training-free decoding method that emphasizes audio-visual temporal alignment in model predictions. MSCD contrasts logits under preserved and disrupted temporal alignment to favor predictions supported by aligned audio and video. We disrupt this alignment by temporally shifting either the audio or the video, while keeping the other modality fixed. Extensive evaluations with two AV-LLMs across three benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of MSCD for temporally grounded audio-visual understanding. In particular, MSCD improves accuracy over the base models on Daily-Omni by 2.2 and 2.8 percentage points for OmniVinci and video-SALMONN 2+, respectively, suggesting that emphasizing temporal alignment can also benefit broader audio-visual understanding.

</details>


### [169] [Admoab-DETR: Resolution-Tiered Fusion for Infrared-Visible Object Detection with Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34163)
*Wenchao Li,Haoran Jia,Shuyuan Wen,Keyan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Admoab-DETR引入了Resolution-Tiered Fusion和Mode-Matched Feature Distillation机制，实现单一模型支持红外、可见光和配对输入，且在无推理开销的情况下，参数量减少18.1%，配对延迟减少39.6%，并在缺少模态和配对基准上达到最佳的准确率-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的配对输入目标检测器依赖于两个感知通道，但在实际应用中，传感器可能会发生故障导致某个感知通道缺失，这对基于配对输入的目标检测器造成不便。Admoab-DETR通过引入Resolution-Tiered Fusion和Mode-Matched Feature Distillation机制，解决了这一问题，使其能够在单通道和配对输入方面均具有更高的性能。

Method: Admoab-DETR结合了Resolution-Tiered Fusion (RTF)和Mode-Matched Feature Distillation (MMFD)机制。RTF通过空间令牌密度和特征粒度来分配融合能力，优化了高分辨率尺度下的轻量化局部融合，并在粗粒度的语义尺度上利用通道压缩的可变形Admoab颈部捕捉跨模态对应。MMFD则通过同步全融合的教师模型，对多尺度编码器特征进行了对齐，防止了跨模态的虚幻感知并减少了参数。

Result: 实验表明，Admoab-DETR在缺少模态和配对基准上达到了最佳的准确率-效率权衡。具体而言，参数量减少了18.1%，配对延迟减少39.6%，而没有任何推理开销。

Conclusion: Admoab-DETR通过创新的融合策略和特征对齐技术，提供了一种实时的目标检测方法，该方法能够在单通道和配对输入的场景中都展现出优越的性能，为全天候条件下的目标检测提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Infrared and visible images offer complementary cues for all-weather object detection, yet real-world sensor dropouts can leave one stream unavailable and degrade paired-input detectors. Meanwhile, existing modality-adaptive methods apply heavy cross-modal attention uniformly across scales, incurring prohibitive token overhead on high-resolution maps. We propose Admoab-DETR, a unified real-time detector supporting infrared-only, visible-only, and paired inputs with a single set of weights. Specifically, \emph{Resolution-Tiered Fusion} (RTF) allocates fusion capacity by spatial token density and feature granularity: a lightweight Spatial Modality Gate governs fine local fusion at the highest-resolution scale with zero-cost single-view pass-through, while channel-compressed deformable Admoab necks capture cross-modal correspondence at coarser semantic scales. Furthermore, \emph{Mode-Matched Feature Distillation} (MMFD) aligns multi-scale encoder features with an availability-synchronized full-fusion teacher, avoiding ill-posed cross-modal hallucination while cutting parameters by 18.1\% and paired latency by 39.6\% at zero inference overhead. We also construct Admoab, a strictly registered 20,000-pair infrared--visible benchmark across diverse scenes. Experiments show Admoab-DETR achieves state-of-the-art accuracy--efficiency trade-offs across missing-modality and paired benchmarks. Our code will be available at https://github.com/liwenchao0615/Admoab-DETR.

</details>


### [170] [Loop4D: Embodied World Modeling with 4D VAEs and Looped Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34199)
*Gen Li,Fan Luo,Mengjun Lin,Shu Han,Bohan Li,Hao Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Loop4D 通过引入运动保留的 4D 表征和循环生成精炼，解决了机器人操控中视觉外观和几何形状预测的挑战，实现了高质量的 4D 生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测机器人操作的成果需要同时考虑视觉外观和动态变化的三维几何，但视频压缩会模糊细节，而同时预测 RGB 和几何则需要足够的生成能力，这是当前研究中的挑战。

Method: Loop4D 采用 4D VAE 对 RGB 和点图序列进行编码，保留了运动细节和几何结构。RGB VAE 结合几何指导监督和预测性重建以减少压缩引起的运动伪影。几何 VAE 使用轻量级适配器来适应预训练的 RGB 表征，同时保留其先验知识。基于这些表征，Dual-Stream Looped Diffusion Transformer 维持了分开的外观和几何流，使用共享的 Transformer 权重和跨模态连接。通过在循环间重用 Transformer 块，无需复制骨干参数即可增加有效深度。

Result: Loop4D 在合成机器人操作任务中展示了高质量的 4D 重建，并验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: Loop4D 通过创新的 4D 表征和循环生成方式解决了机器人操控中视觉和几何预测的挑战，显示出在高效模型设计中的潜力。

Abstract: Predicting the outcomes of robotic manipulation requires modeling both visual appearance and evolving 3D geometry. This poses two challenges: video compression can obscure fine-grained robot motion, while jointly predicting RGB and geometry demands sufficient generative capacity. We introduce Loop4D, a framework that addresses these challenges through motion-preserving 4D representations and recurrent generative refinement. Our 4D VAE encodes RGB and pointmap sequences into compact spatiotemporal latents while preserving motion details and geometric structure. Our RGB VAE combines geometry-guided supervision with predictive reconstruction to reduce compression-induced motion artifacts. Our geometry VAE uses lightweight adapters to adapt pretrained RGB representations while preserving their priors. Building on these representations, our Dual-Stream Looped Diffusion Transformer maintains separate appearance and geometry streams with shared Transformer weights and cross-modal connections. By reusing Transformer blocks across loops, we increase effective depth without replicating backbone parameters, enabling models with fewer parameters to improve joint RGB-geometry prediction through additional computation. Experiments demonstrate high-quality 4D generation and validate Loop4D in simulated robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [171] [Variation-aware Temporal Correspondence for Self-Supervised Video Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34220)
*Yang Liu,Qianqian Xu,Peisong Wen,Siran Dai,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VT-CoRe框架通过提出变分意识的时间对应和时间变异建模策略，解决了视频表示学习中的挑战，提升无监督视频表示学习的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于视频的时空变化性和静态图像的不同，传统的方法难以捕捉到视频中的时序一致性与变化信息。VT-CoRe旨在提出一种有效的解决方案来应对这一挑战。

Method: VT-CoRe框架通过引入双面时间参考的三明治采样策略和拼贴注册模块来建立可靠的局部对应关系。同时，通过自蒸馏架构实现潜在语义对齐，并提出时间变异建模来约束随时间变化的表示轨迹。

Result: VT-CoRe在8个下游任务的不同基准测试中表现出色，特别是在像素级、帧级、视频级和基于视觉的机器人学习场景中。

Conclusion: VT-CoRe通过明确的框架设计提升了无监督视频表示方法的效果，并提供了更结构化的潜在表示空间，以支持变分意识的时间对应学习。

Abstract: Self-supervised video representation learning has achieved remarkable progress by leveraging large-scale unlabeled video data. Recent methods commonly extend Masked Image Modeling (MIM) to Masked Video Modeling (MVM), where video representations are learned by capturing semantic information from temporal contexts during masked content recovery. Unlike static images, videos exhibit inherent temporal variation, as visual objects continuously change their positions, appearances, and motion states across frames. Therefore, MVM needs to preserve semantic consistency while capturing temporal changes of corresponding regions, which raises two central challenges: 1) how to establish reliable temporal correspondence across frames, and 2) how to formulate an appropriate representation learning objective for modeling both semantic consistency and temporal variation. To address these challenges, we propose Variation-aware Temporal Correspondence for video Representation learning (VT-CoRe) framework. For challenge 1), VT-CoRe introduces a sandwich sampling strategy to construct two-sided temporal references and a Patch Registration Module (PRM) to establish reliable patch-level correspondence. For challenge 2), VT-CoRe imposes latent-space semantic alignment on registered representations through a self-distillation architecture and further proposes temporal variation modeling to constrain representation trajectories over time. Experiments on 8 downstream tasks across 16 benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and applicability of VT-CoRe across pixel-level, frame-level, video-level, and vision-based robotic learning scenarios. Complementary representation analysis shows that VT-CoRe learns a more structured latent space for variation-aware temporal correspondence learning.

</details>


### [172] [AnyCam4D: Ray-Based 4D Gaussian Rendering for Arbitrary Camera Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34223)
*Sascha Kirch,Rafael Pagés,Jan Ondřej,Clara Pérez-Molina,Sergio Martin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnyCam4D 使用原生 4D 高斯模型通过光线基于的光栅化方式训练，能够有效地处理各种镜头效果，同时保持视野和分辨率，并在合成和实际视频中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前大多数动态高斯散射假设相机为理想针孔模型，这在实际应用中导致画面失真、视野损失或细节不足，尤其是在使用宽角和鱼眼镜头时。此研究旨在克服这些问题。

Method: 通过光线基于的光栅化将原始视频转换为 4D 高斯分布，再利用这些分布来训练 AnyCam4D 模型。

Result: 在合成 170 度鱼眼镜头的场景中，训练原始图像比标准投影提高了 3.0-8.1 dB 的峰值信噪比（PSNR）。在实际视频中，AnyCam4D 的感知误差比适应各个镜头的标准投影低，并且在具有显著视角差异时，PSNR 增加了 6.2 dB。

Conclusion: AnyCam4D 能够在保持视野和分辨率的同时处理多种镜头效果，并且通过数值稳定的方法获得方向和大小，使得在各种实际场景中的应用潜力巨大。

Abstract: Real cameras are not pinholes, yet most dynamic Gaussian splatting assumes they are. Footage is therefore undistorted first, losing field of view or detail, especially with wide-angle and fisheye cameras. Static Gaussian splatting has largely solved this with ray-based rendering, which suits any camera. Native 4D Gaussians, which model motion in space-time, cannot use it directly: each Gaussian's orientation and size cannot be recovered reliably from its covariance. We present AnyCam4D, which trains native 4D Gaussians on original footage by ray-based rasterization, preserving field of view and resolution at about the per-step cost of standard projection. Our key contribution is a numerically stable way to obtain orientation and size: we condition a square root of the 4D covariance. Trained models render through other cameras, with rolling shutter, depth of field or chromatic aberration. With a synthetic 170-degree fisheye camera, training on original images improves peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) by 3.0-8.1 dB. On real footage, AnyCam4D has lower perceptual error than the standard projection adapted to each camera, at comparable PSNR and without camera-specific derivations. With the synthetic camera rotated 60-80 degrees away from the capture rig's view, it leads by 6.2 dB against ground truth.

</details>


### [173] [EgoMyo: Physical Hand State Estimation from Egocentric Video and Wrist Electromyography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34224)
*Guanglong Sun,Kanglei Zhou,Ziteng Wang,Nana Wang,Hengkai Tan,Xiaolong Liu,Hang Su,Yi Zhong,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究旨在通过结合视觉几何和神经肌电图(sEMG)信号，提高手部物理状态的估计精度，并提出了MyoBridge方法来优化视觉与sEMG信号的融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手部物理状态的估计对于理解手部与物体的交互至关重要，但相同外观的手部状态可能涉及不同的接触状态和力。因此，研究者通过EgoMyo数据库定义了一个大规模基准，以提高手部运动和接触力的估计精度。

Method: 研究者利用EgoMyo数据库中的视频帧和腕表面肌电图数据，提出了一种基于视觉特征和sEMG信号的手部物理状态估计方法，即MyoBridge。该方法通过让手部区域作为视觉与sEMG融合的单位，让每个区域为自己的sEMG信号分配权重，并只将区域力值分布到预测的接触顶点，实现多模态信息的处理。

Result: MyoBridge方法在保持点和力的估计方面优于最强基线，分别减少了39.8%和18.5%的误差，在未见会话和受试者的数据上达到了最小的保持点误差。

Conclusion: MyoBridge方法展示了通过结合视觉和sEMG信号有效地估计手部生物力学状态的潜力，为手部交互研究提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Physical hand interaction depends on configuration, contact, and force, yet nearly identical appearances can conceal different contact states and exerted forces. We therefore formulate physical hand state estimation from egocentric video and wrist surface electromyography (sEMG), combining visual geometry with neuromuscular evidence to jointly recover these coupled components. To support this task, we introduce EgoMyo, a benchmark spanning 5.75M video frames, 50 participants, 34 contact configurations, and repeated donnings, synchronizing these inputs with 3D hand pose and with contact and normal force registered onto the hand surface from bare-hand pressure. Controlled analysis reveals that vision resolves spatial structure while sEMG better estimates force magnitude, that sEMG's gains concentrate in regions where vision is least reliable, and that contact localization limits force accuracy. Guided by these findings, we propose MyoBridge, which makes the hand region the unit of vision-sEMG fusion, lets each region gate its own share of sEMG, and distributes regional force magnitudes only over predicted contact vertices at inference. On held-out trials, MyoBridge reduces pose and force error by 39.8% and 18.5% over the strongest baseline and achieves the lowest pose error on unseen sessions and subjects.

</details>


### [174] [LiveRelight: Sub-Second Global Illumination Editing for Gaussian Interiors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34226)
*Noah Rothenberger,Dor Verbin,Serge Belongie,Bernhard Kerbl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合光照调整方法，能够在不进行每次编辑优化的情况下，实现快速、高保真的室内直接光照和间接光照的实时更新，平均提高5dB PSNR，并将编辑延迟降低三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的光照调整技术由于视觉反馈慢或不准确，限制了编辑对结果的精细调整。因此需要一种能够快速实时调整光照且不牺牲质量的方法。

Method: 该方法通过预计算几何光照交换而非照明响应，保留了低成本的传输求解，能够适应发射以及表面反射率的变化。对于直接光照使用视见感知采样的单面发射体，通过稀疏辐射传递在三角形代理上传播漫反射间接反射。通过稀疏投影结合Gaussian alpha混合，将代理解决方案转移至重建。

Result: 在合成测试套件的八个光照调整条件下，该方法在亚秒内进行了编辑，平均提高了5dB PSNR，将编辑延迟降低了三个数量级。同时，实验证明了该方法能够实现逼真的交互式光照调整。

Conclusion: 该研究结合了预计算几何光照交换和实时反馈，显著提高了室内光照调整的速度和保真度，适用于现实世界和合成场景的光照调整工作。

Abstract: Relighting reconstructed 3D scenes remains challenging due to the complex nature of light transport: slow or approximate visual feedback restricts the editor's ability to fine-tune the results to satisfaction. We present a hybrid relighting approach that enables interactive, high-fidelity updates of direct and indirect indoor illumination without per-edit optimization. Our approach permits the editor to make a wide range of relighting edits at room-scale, with near-instant feedback. Our key insight is to precompute geometric light exchange rather than illumination responses, retaining an inexpensive transport solve that accommodates changes in emission and surface reflectance. We evaluate direct illumination on reconstructed 2D Gaussians using visibility-aware sampling of one-sided emitters, while propagating diffuse interreflection through sparse radiosity on a triangle proxy. A sparse projection fitted through Gaussian alpha compositing transfers the proxy solution to the reconstruction. Our formulation further supports fast fixture transformations and emitter insertion by recomputing light source injection. Across eight relighting conditions of our synthetic test suite, our sub-second edits improve relighting fidelity over prior path-traced editing by an average of 5\,dB PSNR, while reducing editing latency by up to three orders of magnitude. Qualitative results further demonstrate realistic, interactive relighting of real-world captures.

</details>


### [175] [OS-Omni: Benchmarking Generalist Computer-Using Agents on Long-Horizon Workflows Across Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34232)
*Hui Shen,Yunta Hsieh,Jianing Ma,Zhuoran Yin,Zhuhan Bao,Ziyuan Liu,Qi Han,Xiuqi Xu,Xianbang Wang,Wenhao Chai,Sihan Xu,Chenyang Zhao,Yanheng Shang,Dongfu Jiang,Rui Sun,Xingzhi Li,Chenqi Yin,Zesen Zhao,Boyuan Zheng,Jisen Li,Xiaoxia Wu,Ruoyan Zhang,Ruiyao Liu,Jing Xiong,He Xiao,Nan Zheng,Hangxiang Chen,Shengyang Tao,Deyang Luorong,Yifeng Liu,Jingxuan Zhang,Chaofan Tao,Yuren Wang,Jiaqi Mo,Rui Hu,Xing Wei,Xiaofeng Liu,Xin Xu,Han Wu,Mengyao Wen,Guobin Zhang,Shatong Zhu,Hei Ting Una Chan,Sicheng Chen,Kai Zhang,Mengkang Hu,Zhongwei Wan,Xin Wang,Chuanyang Zheng,Ziheng Zhang,Hengyuan Zhang,Zunhai Su,Kanzhi Cheng,Dehan Kong,Yian Wang,Hanrong Zhang,Jiawei Lu,Yifan Zhang,Junnan Li,Changyi Yang,Jintao Qu,Wenyao Gao,Jiaxuan Luo,Jiaxin Deng,Yuhao Chen,Xuesong Ye,Jingwen Gu,Yichi Zhang,Qun Lou,Shen Yan,Ben Athiwaratkun,Qiushi Sun,Mi Zhang,Ping Luo,Wenhu Chen,Ngai Wong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OS-Omni是一个跨平台基准和执行环境，评估计算机使用的代理在Windows、Ubuntu、macOS、iOS、Android和Web环境中的表现。首次发布包括48个任务，12个跨平台的工作流程，并且识别了当前代理在跨平台处理长期任务时的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要针对单一操作系统和环境，这使得评估通用代理在多操作系统和持久应用状态下的长期任务执行能力变得模糊。因此，提出了OS-Omni来提供一个全面的评估平台。

Method: OS-Omni通过定义版本化的任务包和包括桌面与移动设备的跨平台工作流程，提供了一个可重复初始化、平台适配器、轨迹日志记录和执行后的状态评估器，同时还包含控制的服务支撑应用。

Result: OS-Omni任务需要67.54次交互步骤，超出了OSWorld的参考步骤15.0。Claude Opus 5.5代理在OS-Omni首次发布中的成功率为68.5%，而人类参考率为77.3%，iOS和macOS上有超过25个百分点的差距。

Conclusion: 当前代理在跨平台处理长期任务时仍存在不足，特别是在持续状态跟踪和持久化最终状态改变方面。

Abstract: Computer-using agents are increasingly expected to complete realistic user goals rather than isolated GUI actions. However, existing benchmarks typically isolate web, mobile, or desktop environments, leaving it unclear whether a single generalist agent can execute long-horizon workflows that span multiple operating systems and persistent application states. We introduce OS-Omni, a live cross-platform benchmark and execution environment for evaluating computer-using agents across Windows, Ubuntu, macOS, iOS, Android, and Web environments. OS-Omni is released in versioned waves of 300 tasks each; this paper focuses on the first release, v1, which contains 48 tasks per platform and 12 cross-platform workflows that pair a desktop with an Android phone. OS-Omni provides reproducible initialization, platform-specific adapters, trajectory logging, and executable final-state evaluators, and includes controlled service-backed applications for workflows involving account, cart, budget, calendar, message, and other persistent state. Human studies show that OS-Omni tasks require 67.54 interaction steps, substantially exceeding OSWorld references of 15.0 steps. On v1, the strongest evaluated agent, Claude Opus 5.5, reaches 68.5% average success, trailing the 77.3% human reference by 8.8 points; the gap is uneven across platforms, with frontier models exceeding humans on Linux but trailing them by more than 25 points on iOS and macOS. These results indicate that current agents remain far from platform-robust, long-horizon computer use, especially when success requires sustained state tracking and durable final-state changes.

</details>


### [176] [VibeVideoAgent: Closing the Perceptual Loop for Code-Driven Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34237)
*Xuanyu Zheng,Luoyuan Zhang,Yancheng Long,Haoyang Xu,Muxi Diao,Yuzhe Chen,Kaiyu Jiang,Bin Wen,Tingting Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VibeVideoAgent 是一种无需培训的框架，让编码代理在生产过程中可以检查他们的草稿，通过多模态感知返回详实且相关的发现，并通过特定的原语在统一的多时钟表示下操作资产和草稿，提升了整体的音频视频质量和规范遵守度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的视频生成方法在音频同步、多视图呈现和交互性等问题上存在局限性，VibeVideoAgent 旨在通过让编码代理在生产过程中能够检查草稿，达到更高质量的视频生成。

Method: VibeVideoAgent 使用多模态感知在短生命周期中运行，并通过一个轻量级阶段协议让代理首先在极小的预览中测试其风险最大的决定。它包含七个原语，这些原语在统一多时钟表示的资产和草稿上操作，支持从粗到细的媒体探索，验证要求指标，进行版本间比较，并衡量流信号。

Result: 在239个VibeVideoBench任务中，VibeVideoAgent 在 Claude Opus 5.5（从 4.03 到 4.43）、GPT-6 Astra（从 4.03 到 4.27）和 Claude Sonnet 5.5（从 3.97 到 4.10）的总体音频视频质量和规范遵守度上均有所提升。特别是在音频视频同步上有了最大的改进（高达 +0.56）。

Conclusion: VibeVideoAgent 通过提供一个无需训练的框架，成功地在生产过程中检测和改进草稿的质量，显著提高了视频生成的精度和可重复性。

Abstract: Code-driven video generation asks a coding agent to write and run rendering programs. This gives exact control over typography, data visualizations, synchronized audio, and multi-shot scenes, and it leaves an editable project behind. A program that exits cleanly can still render overlapping labels, a camera aimed away from its subject, broken object interactions, or narration that drifts out of sync, and none of these raise a runtime error. Feeding rendered frames and audio back into the coding session bloats its long-horizon context, while critiquing only the finished full-resolution render turns early design mistakes into costly rewrites. We introduce VibeVideoAgent, a training-free framework that lets coding agents inspect their drafts throughout production. Multimodal perception runs in short-lived contexts kept apart from the coder and returns compact, grounded Findings. Seven primitives operate on a unified multi-clock representation of assets and drafts: they explore media from coarse to fine, verify quoted requirements with evidence from every required modality, compare a draft with a reference or an earlier revision on separate clocks, and measure stream signals locally. A lightweight staged protocol has the agent test its riskiest decision on a minimal preview first. On all 239 VibeVideoBench tasks, with matched agent runtimes and a fixed perception model, VibeVideoAgent improves holistic audiovisual quality (L3) for Claude Opus 5.5 (4.03 -> 4.43, while L2 rubric compliance rises from 98.87% to 99.35%), GPT-6 Astra (4.03 -> 4.27), and Claude Sonnet 5.5 (3.97 -> 4.10), with the largest gains in audio-visual synchronization (up to +0.56). Controlled ablations show that each component contributes, and trajectory audits show that the granularity of code edits governs the trade-off between rubric adherence and perceptual quality.

</details>


### [177] [Analemma $\infty$: A Temporal Benchmark for Geospatial Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34240)
*Alex López-Cifuentes,Laura Martínez-Ferrer,Juan Ignacio Bravo Pérez-Villar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Analemma 是一个跨六个数据集的时序基准，涵盖了 10 米和 20 米的 Sentinel-2 波段数据和生长季节的时间序列，填补了数据集中缺少的空间、日期或云掩码的信息。TESSERA 模型在所有模型中表现最佳，从零开始构建的月度合成模型在某些情况下优于基础模型。未提及的三种评估选择对 TESSERA v1 的性能影响显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究背景是为了弥补现有公共基准中缺乏时间维度或对其进行抽样的不足，因此我们需要创建一个覆盖六个数据集的基准，包含详细的时空信息，以进行更全面的时序分析。

Method: 该方法采用了冷冻编码协议来评估所有模型在不同标签比例和种子上的表现。基准中包括两个不进行预训练的基准模型和一个排名最高的TESSERA模型。

Result: TESSERA 模型在所有模型中排名第一，从零开始构建的模型在某些情况下（如使用月度合成数据）表现优于基础模型。

Conclusion: 研究结论是通过填补数据集中的缺失信息并引入冻结编码协议，基准测试揭示了时序特征对性能的影响。这将引导未来的研究重点。

Abstract: Satellite time series capture how land surfaces change over time. Pixel-wise temporal foundation models read a year of satellite observations per pixel, yet public benchmarks lack a temporal axis or subsample it to a few dates. We introduce Analemma, a temporal benchmark of six datasets that carry every 10 and 20 m Sentinel-2 band and a time series spanning a growing season, completed where their releases omit geometry, dates, or cloud masks. One frozen-encoder protocol evaluates all models across label ratios and three seeds, with two baselines without pretraining. The TESSERA model ranks first overall. A from-scratch model on monthly composites outranks every foundation model except OlmoEarth v1.3-Base, suggesting that temporal input explains most of the performance. Three evaluation choices left unstated by the source publications each shift TESSERA v1 by 7.6 to 18.6 mIoU, against top-two gaps of 0.6 to 2.8. Data, harness and leaderboard will be publicly released soon.

</details>


### [178] [Detector Operating Behavior Inference via Data-Space Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34252)
*Stefan Becker,Simon Weiss,Wolfgang Hübner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DOBI 提出了一种单图像条件下的检测器行为推断方法，并在 YOLOv10-Nano 人体检测器上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前检测器的召回率和误报率受输入分布影响，但没有一种方法可以从单一未标记图像内部证据中推断出这种依赖关系。DOBI 的关键贡献在于通过控制数据空间采样，从单一未标记图像中学习一个轻量级的逆映射。该方法在 YOLOv10-Nano 人体检测器上取得显著效果，降低了预测 PR 曲线的偏差，并在未见过的条件组合中显示了其特有的功能。

Method: DOBI 方法包括两个主要部分：一是使用控制数据空间采样从单一图像中探测检测器的行为状态；二是通过学习从单个图像到检测器行为状态的轻量级逆映射来推断检测器在不同分布上的表现。

Result: DOBI 在控制条件下的实验证明了其有效性，预测的 PR 曲线偏差从 0.128 降低到 0.054。此外，通过使用单一图像推断的检测器功能距离与实际情况高度一致（Spearman 相关系数 0.96）。该方法在未见过的条件组合上优于模板查找表，而对插值具有较高的准确性。

Conclusion: DOBI 显示了基于单图像条件推断检测器分布级行为的可行性，为未来的实际应用提供了新的可能性。
