Abstract: A detector scores candidate detections, but the recall and false alarms at a given confidence threshold depend on the input distribution. We introduce Detector Operating Behavior Inference (DOBI), a second-level inference task: estimating a fixed detector's threshold-dependent, distribution-level operating response from the internal evidence of a single unlabeled image. DOBI treats the detector's threshold response, i.e. recall, false positives, and precision across thresholds, as a latent behavioral state under the current data condition. Controlled data-space sampling probes this state space offline, and a lightweight monitor learns the inverse map from one image to this state, whose single functional output supports multiple operating policies. In a controlled proof of concept with a fixed YOLOv10-Nano person detector and COCO-derived degradations, predicted PR behavior reduces mean absolute deviation from 0.128 for an image-independent mean-behavior prior to 0.054, and predicted functional distances track empirical regime distances (Spearman 0.96). From one unlabeled image, the inferred behavior selects operating points as well as a non-deployable lookup that knows the true regime (macro-regime $F_1$ 0.6604 for both, versus 0.6407 under pristine calibration; oracle 0.6620). On this discrete benchmark, regime-supervised curve lookups are strong controls. Pre-specified continuous and compositional probing shows where functional inference matters: on unseen combinations, the functional decoder has a lower mean curve error than a template lookup, increasingly with distance from the training conditions, whereas the lookup is more accurate for interpolation. These results establish the feasibility of single-image inference of distribution-level detector behavior over the probed conditions. Source code is available at https://github.com/beckerio/dobi

</details>


### [179] [TORE: Token-Wise Taylor Order Selection via Decomposed Residual Error Estimation for Diffusion Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34261)
*Lei Tang,Baoquan Zhang,Yakun Zhang,Yunming Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TORE 提出了一种基于 Taylor 展开残差估计的方法，用于加速扩散模型，通过估计预测误差来选择每个 token 的泰勒级数项数，从而实现显著加速，同时保持高质量生成结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法使用浅层特征预测全模型残差，存在代理特征与目标残差的不匹配问题，为此建议采用 TORE 方法，直接针对目标残差进行泰勒级数项数选择。

Method: TORE 方法首先将预测误差分解为截断误差和有限差分导数误差，通过分析这些误差的来源，利用缓存的残差来预先选择每个 token 的泰勒级数项数，以此来进行残差估计和加速。

Result: 实验表明，TORE 在 FLUX.1-dev 和 Wan2.1 上分别实现了 4.31 倍和 4.18 倍的加速，且生成结果的质量和保真度保持在高水平。

Conclusion: TORE 提供了一种有效的策略来减少扩散模型的推理延迟，通过精确估计残差预测误差来快速选择泰勒级数项数，从而显著提高扩散模型的效率，而不会牺牲生成质量。

Abstract: Diffusion models excel at image and video generation, but iterative denoising incurs substantial inference latency. Recent token-adaptive acceleration methods select Taylor prediction orders for the full-model residual using shallow-layer proxy scores. However, candidate orders are evaluated on these proxy features but used to predict the full-model residual, creating a proxy--target mismatch: the proxy-preferred order may not minimize residual prediction error. To address this mismatch, we propose \textbf{TORE} (\textbf{T}aylor \textbf{O}rder selection via \textbf{R}esidual \textbf{E}rror estimation), which selects token-wise Taylor orders based on estimated prediction errors for the full-model residual. Yet direct error evaluation requires future ground-truth residuals, which are unavailable during order selection. TORE therefore estimates prediction error by analyzing how errors arise in Taylor extrapolation. Specifically, it decomposes prediction error into truncation error and finite-difference derivative error, then estimates both contributions from cached residuals to preselect token-wise orders for upcoming skip steps. Extensive experiments show that TORE achieves $4.31\times$ speedup on FLUX.1-dev and $4.18\times$ on Wan2.1 while maintaining high generation quality and fidelity.

</details>


### [180] [Semantic State Reading for Joint Image-Semantic Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34279)
*Guanxi Lai,Zixuan Li,Zhiwei Li,Qi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种称为 Semantic State Reading (SSR) 的方法，它联合去噪图像潜在变量、全局 CLS 贴片和紧凑的局部状态嵌入，并通过独立路径在每个变压器块中注入当前全局状态。该方法在 ImageNet 数据集上实现了最佳的 FID 分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉表示可以提高扩散-变换器训练的效果，但利用全局上下文和局部空间结构需要决定哪些语义状态要建模以及如何访问它们。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了 Semantic State Reading (SSR) 方法。

Method: SSR 方法联合去噪图像潜在变量、全局 CLS 贴片和紧凑的局部状态嵌入。局部状态嵌入在输入处与相应的图像标记融合，独立投影将当前全局状态注入每个变压器块。研究表明，局部状态建模和重复全局读取对模型具有互补的益处。

Result: 在 ImageNet 256x256 数据集上，使用 SSR 方法的 SiT-XL/2 达到了与 SiT、REPA 和 Representation Entanglement for Generation (REG) 相比的最佳 FID 分数，使用更少的训练迭代次数。在没有分类器指导的情况下，SSR 达到了 FID 1.78，在仅使用分类器自由引导的情况下达到了 FID 1.34。结合分类器自由引导和自动引导，FID 分数进一步降低到 1.31。

Conclusion: SSR 方法通过有效地建模和访问语义范围来改进扩散-变换器的训练，为生成任务提供了显著的改进。

Abstract: Pretrained visual representations improve diffusion-transformer training, yet exploiting both global context and local spatial structure requires deciding which semantic states to model and how to access them. We introduce Semantic State Reading (SSR), which jointly denoises image latents, a global CLS token encoding image-level context, and a compact patch state preserving spatial correspondence. SSR couples this global-local modeling with distinct reading paths: local states are fused with corresponding image tokens at the input, while independent projections inject the current global state into every transformer block. Both semantic scopes thus remain accessible throughout denoising. Ablations show complementary benefits from local-state modeling and repeated global reading, and semantic-trajectory interventions alter image layout under a fixed class label. On ImageNet $256 \times 256$, SiT-XL/2 with SSR reaches or surpasses the reference FIDs of SiT, REPA, and Representation Entanglement for Generation (REG) with $100\times$, $40\times$, and $2\times$ fewer training iterations, respectively. SSR achieves unguided FID 1.78 at 2M iterations and FID 1.34 with classifier-free guidance alone. Combining classifier-free guidance with autoguidance yields FID 1.31 at 1M iterations, achieving the best FID in our main comparison table.

</details>


### [181] [ReMeDi: Single-Image PBR Material Estimation via Reparameterization between Metal and Dielectric Hypotheses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34284)
*Fan Fei,Xin Huang,Yixin Zhu,Jiawei Lu,Xin Yang,Jinglin Liang,Ruining Tang,Yifei Feng,Boxin Shi,Chunchao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReMeDi 提出了一个新方法，通过重新参数化基础颜色并限制两个独立网络遵循单一假设，确保铝反射率图与金属假设一致，从而提高了材料贴图的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的金属-粗糙度流程虽让金属性变得明确，但未强制实际基础色匹配，尤其是对自动预测的算法。ReMeDi 研究通过该问题提出了新的解决思路。

Method: ReMeDi 通过重新参数化基础颜色成两变体的金属度加权混合，以及将每个单独网络限制于单一假设（全金属/全介电），确保反射率图与其假设一致，从而提高材料贴图的准确性。

Result: 实验表明，ReMeDi 在材质精度和复光保真度方面优于现有最先进的方法。

Conclusion: ReMeDi 是一种有效的方法，用于通过确保基础颜色与假设的一致性来提高材料贴图的准确性。

Abstract: Physically based rendering (PBR) material estimation facilitates relighting, material editing, and 3D content creation. As the reflectance of a surface heavily depends on whether it is a metal or a dielectric, producing any map that determines albedo involves a decision on metalness, which we call the "intended metalness" of the map. Rendering maps with mismatched intended metalness yields physically implausible or misinterpreted materials. The metallic--roughness workflow makes metalness explicit and thus encourages base color to match it, but does not enforce this match, especially for automated methods directly predicting each map. Therefore, we propose ReMeDi, which enforces the match through two components. First, we reparameterize base color as a metalness-weighted blend of its two variants, estimated under the all-metal and all-dielectric hypothesis, respectively. Second, as available supervision cannot teach a single network both hypotheses at every pixel, we restrict each of two separate networks to one hypothesis, so that each variant is ensured to follow its hypothesis. Besides enforcing the match, this also improves each estimated material map. Experiments show that ReMeDi outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both material accuracy and relighting fidelity.

</details>


### [182] [PostMesh: Self-Rewarded Preference Post-Training for Flow-Based Artist Mesh Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34319)
*Gihyun Kwon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PostMesh 是一种后训练方法，适用于基于扩散或流的艺术家网格生成器。它通过自我样本训练，结合特定奖励计算，以较短的时间获得了与缓慢自回归生成器相当或更好的艺术家网格质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前大多数网格生成器速度慢，难以达到艺术家级别的质量。PostMesh 旨在通过一种新的奖励机制和优化方法，在保持高效的同时提高生成的艺术家网格的质量。

Method: PostMesh 采用了一种多负流偏好优化来转移奖励给生成器，并通过计算密集型输入和输出之间的奖励来训练生成器。这种方法不需要昂贵的艺术品制作真值，并在较短的 LoRA 后训练中显著提高了生成的艺术家网格的质量。

Result: PostMesh 在大多数艺术家网格质量指标和盲测中达到了与缓慢自回归生成器相当或更好的效果，同时大大减少了所需时间。

Conclusion: PostMesh 是一种有效的后训练方法，通过改进现有的扩散或流模型，显著提高了生成艺术家网格的质量和生成效率。

Abstract: Artistic mesh generators turn a dense 3D object into an artist mesh: a compact, quad-dominant mesh that is easy to edit. Most of current models are auto-regressive methods which decode a mesh token by token, take tens to hundreds of seconds per mesh. Diffusion- and flow-based generators are much faster, but their meshes still fall short of artist quality. To our knowledge, PostMesh is the first post-training method for a diffusion- or flow-based artist mesh generator, and it needs no expensive artist-made ground truth: the generator is trained on its own samples, scored by a reward computed from the dense input and the output. We propose an artistic mesh reward that keeps the output consistent with the dense input while making the mesh structure into artistic. We also propose a multi-negative flow preference optimisation that transfers the reward to the generator. After a short LoRA post-training, PostMesh is comparable to or better than slow autoregressive generators on most artist-mesh quality metrics and in a blind VLM preference test, at a fraction of their time.

</details>


### [183] [SPARC: Semantic Calibration with Latent Multimodal Knowledge for Robust Image-Text Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34344)
*Zhongkuan Mao,Wenzhuo Zhao,Haidong Chen,Shangze Li,Yidong Wang,Jinhe Bi,Yunpu Ma,Junfeng Fang,Fei Shen,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于不完整查询的鲁棒图像-文本检索方法，即SPARC，该方法通过语义投影与关系约束提升候选物的区分性和排名稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图像-文本检索方法主要关注适应分布变化，但未充分解决不完整查询的候选选择问题和排名稳定性。

Method: 首先，通过产品球面空间中的语义校准模块优化不完整的视觉和文本特征；其次，引入语义引导模块，利用完整特征中的隐式多模态知识与跨模态语义锚点促进校准特征的语义对齐；最后，引入匹配关系约束模块，通过融合原始查询特征与校准特征并施加软排名约束，限制排名波动。

Result: 实验结果表明，SPARC在Flickr30K和COCO数据集下，在不同图像遮挡和单词删除设置中，均优于其他对比方法，并且在不同的预训练骨干网络下取得一致的增益。

Conclusion: SPARC 提供了一种有效的方法来处理不完整查询，通过多模态语义投影和约束增强图像-文本检索的鲁棒性和稳定性。

Abstract: Robust image-text retrieval aims to maintain accurate cross-modal matching when image or text queries are incomplete, supporting reliable information access in real-world applications. However, existing methods typically focus on adapting to distribution shifts, without adequately addressing candidate discrimination for incomplete queries and ranking instability. To this end, we propose semantic projection and alignment with relational constraints (SPARC), which elicits latent multimodal knowledge from frozen encoders to improve candidate discrimination and ranking stability. Specifically, we first devise a semantic calibration module on a product of spheres that refines incomplete visual and textual features through tangent-space updates, maintaining the unit-sphere structure used for cosine similarity. Second, we introduce a robust semantic guidance module that combines latent multimodal knowledge encoded in complete image-text features with cross-modal semantic anchors to promote semantic alignment of the calibrated features. Finally, we introduce a matching relation constraint module that fuses original query features with their calibrated counterparts and applies a soft ranking constraint to penalize reductions in initially positive matching margins, thereby limiting ranking perturbations. Extensive experiments on Flickr30K and COCO demonstrate that SPARC outperforms competing methods under various image occlusion and word deletion settings, while achieving consistent gains across different pretrained backbones.

</details>


### [184] [AIRIS: Benchmarking Social Embodied Intelligence in Egocentric Streaming Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34347)
*Yuchen Zhou,Hongsheng Wu,Jiayu Tang,Yunxuan Mao,Hanjing Ye,Shenghao Zhang,Jing Tang,Chun-Kai Fan,Yufei Huang,Yonggen Ling,He Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AIRIS 提供了一个因果流式基准，用于评估和诊断代理在实时社交决策中的能力，特别关注时间锚定的注意力、意图、反应和互动。该基准包括来自 357 个第一人称视角剪辑的 8000 个专家精选问题，评估模型在不同情境下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常评估社交认知能力在孤立环境或完成视频中的表现，这不充分。AIRIS 基准旨在填补这一空白，通过引入一个更加贴近实际社交互动的评估框架，以便更好地理解和改进实时社交智能。

Method: AIRIS 通过提出一个时间锚定的因果链框架（包括 Attention, Intention, Reaction, and Interaction），在 357 个第一人称视角剪辑中收集了 8000 个专家精选问题，涵盖 11 种情境类别和 36 个能力维度。每个问题都要求模型仅基于观察到的信息做出决策。

Result: 评估结果显示，通用视觉语言模型在被动线索定位和意图识别方面表现出色，而专项的实体模型在孤立反应子决策方面更为出色。但总体而言，这些模型经常在缺乏充分证据时预测意图，并且在干预时机、动作领域和具体执行上的联合对齐方面表现不佳。

Conclusion: AIRIS 为从被动的社会感知过渡到可靠的实体交互提供了诊断基础，有助于指导未来的模型改进和发展。

Abstract: Embodied agents operating around people must not only recognize social cues, but also decide when the available evidence is sufficient, whether to intervene, and how to respond without violating social norms. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate these abilities in isolation or with access to completed videos, obscuring where an online social decision fails. We introduce AIRIS, a causal streaming benchmark that formulates social embodied intelligence as a time-anchored chain of Attention, Intention, Reaction, and Interaction (A-I-R-I). AIRIS contains 8,000 expert-curated questions grounded in 357 egocentric clips across 11 context categories and 36 capability dimensions. At each query, models receive only the observation prefix available at that time; decision-aware metrics reward calibrated waiting and complete reactions while penalizing premature commitment. Evaluating 45 proprietary and open-source general-purpose, embodied, streaming-video, and omni-modal models reveals fragmented and temporally miscalibrated competence. General-purpose VLMs excel at passive cue grounding and intent recognition, whereas embodied specialists lead on isolated reaction sub-decisions; yet across all families, models frequently guess intents before sufficient evidence emerges and fail to jointly align intervention timing, action domain, and concrete execution within the same episode. AIRIS thus provides a diagnostic foundation for bridging the gap between passive social perception and reliable embodied interaction.

</details>


### [185] [One Patch, Many Policies: Object-Associated Physical Patch Attacks on Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34360)
*Yiqian Liu,Yuqi Lin,Hui Lu,Jiaming He,Qi Zhu,Deyi Ji,Bing Li,Kaizheng Wang,Zhiyuan Zha,Yi Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OAPA提出了一种物理贴图攻击框架，能够在不重新优化贴图的情况下，选择性地干扰目标对象的操控，展示了在不同任务集和策略上的强大迁徙攻击性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对VLA和WAM在视觉领域受到的对抗视觉干扰，研究者们提出了一种新的方法来应对这种威胁。

Method: OAPA框架结合了物体关联位置放置、可微物理合成和最终动作专家表示差异等技术，实现了对特定任务对象操控的干扰。

Result: 实验结果显示，OAPA方法在多个任务集和策略下均表现出强大的攻击迁移能力，并能够通过贴图位置选择性地干扰特定任务对象的操控。

Conclusion: OAPA是一种有效的物理贴图攻击框架，能够提高VLA和WAM模型的安全性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) and World Action Models (WAMs) are vulnerable to adversarial visual interference, motivating the study of universal and transferable physical patch attacks. We propose Object-Associated Patch Attack (OAPA), a universal and transferable physical patch framework that enables an attacker to select which task object's manipulation to disrupt through patch placement, without re-optimizing the patch. Our framework combines object-associated placement, differentiable physical compositing, and final action-expert representation divergence. Physical compositing accounts for perspective and occlusion across camera views, while representation divergence guides patch optimization using either relative RMS or cosine distance. Experimental results demonstrate strong attack transferability across task suites and policies, together with the ability to selectively disrupt manipulation of an attacker-chosen task object through patch placement.

</details>


### [186] [MoCA4D: Monocular 3D Bounding Box Lifting and Tracking with Camera and Object Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34370)
*Changwoo Jeon,Jongyoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoCA4D 提出了一种新的框架，通过结合观察分支和动态分支，在单目视频中实现 3D 边界框的提升和跟踪，显著改善了中心运动误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏同时考虑物体运动和摄像机视点变化的单目3D边界框跟踪方法。

Method: MoCA4D框架通过观察分支估计每帧几何结构，动态分支预测用于多个对象的相机变换和每个对象的单独刚性变换。引入了Star Optimization来通过这些变换传递的历史框精炼当前的深度和方向。

Result: 在Argoverse 2和ADT中的所有评估对象中，Star Optimization相对于Kalman滤波减少了38.1%和38.7%的中心运动误差。在MOVi-F上，动态分支在八过渡运动窗口中获得了最低的角和旋转误差，以及最高的IoU。

Conclusion: MoCA4D框架能够显著提高单目视频中的3D边界框跟踪精度，特别是在处理动态场景时。

Abstract: Monocular 3D bounding box tracking must account for both object motion and changes in camera viewpoint. Framewise box lifting with temporal filtering leaves these motion components implicit, while static-scene fusion assumes stationary objects. We introduce MoCA4D, a framework for 3D box lifting and tracking from monocular video, associated 2D box prompts, and camera intrinsics. An observation branch estimates per-frame geometry, while a dynamic branch predicts a camera transformation shared across objects and a separate rigid transformation for each object. We propose Star Optimization to refine current depth and orientation using the observation and historical boxes propagated through these transformations. The refined box then guides subsequent propagation. Across all evaluated objects in Argoverse 2 and ADT, Star Optimization reduces center motion error by 38.1% and 38.7%, respectively, relative to Kalman filtering of the same observations. On MOVi-F, the dynamic branch achieves the lowest corner and rotation errors and the highest IoU among the evaluated methods over eight-transition motion windows. Real-data camera-only controls further link predicted object motion to improved localization on dynamic-object tracks.

</details>


### [187] [Plausible Is Not Proven: Testing Failure Explanations in Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34393)
*Zeeshan Ali,Md Rayyan,Anshuman Chakravorty,Omer Faraz,Anshi Bajaj,Amit Saini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究推出了Vision Error Observatory（VEO），通过严格的测试评估单目深度模型中的故障标签，包括与注释、盲评者、靶向图像编辑和训练信号的对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于单目深度模型故障分析的研究缺乏系统的方法，许多标签未经验证，VEO旨在提供一种标准化的评估方法。

Method: VEO通过识别δ_1标准下的失败区域，为每个区域打上可能的故障标签，并通过四个测试评估这些标签：与未见过数据的注释的一致性、盲评者的共识、针对图像编辑的反应以及作为训练信号的价值。

Result: 研究发现，某些常见的标签（如遮挡边界、反射和无纹理）并不适用于所有模型或数据集，它们在不同数据集中的表现存在显著差异。遮挡边界的标签有一定的一致性，但在细粒度尺度下变化显著；反射和无纹理的标签则未能通过可靠性测试；VEO评估的标签与人类共识仅在随机水平上一致，且增强在这些诊断区域上的权重要求导致了目标像素错误的增加。

Conclusion: 该研究强调，在单目深度模型中提供的故障解释必须经过严格的测试和验证，否则不应被广泛信任；研究报告了测试结果、数据集、代码和其他资源，以便其他研究者进行进一步验证。

Abstract: Failure analyses of monocular depth models often name a cause for each error, such as an occluding boundary, a thin object or a reflective surface, but such labels are rarely tested. We present the Vision Error Observatory (VEO), an audit that finds regions failing the $δ_1$ criterion on sensor-measured pixels, labels each with a likely cause or abstains, and then subjects the labels to four tests: agreement with object annotations on data the label model never saw, agreement with blinded human raters, response to targeted image edits, and value as a training signal. We apply it to eight models on NYU Depth V2, DIODE and iBims-1, with labels also checked on Hypersim, and fix most decision rules before their results. The occluding-boundary label agrees with annotations (AUROC 0.85 on NYU, 0.81 on Hypersim, 0.69 on iBims-1), but a single depth-edge cue does as well, and most boundary failures of the strongest model vanish under a 2-pixel tolerance. The reflection label does not transfer to synthetic data, the textureless label is near chance against annotations, and only one of four edit tests, an exploratory one, supports its label. Evaluated against five blinded raters, VEO's labels matched the human consensus only at chance level (Cohen's $κ$ = 0.05). In addition, an increase in the weight on the diagnosed regions during fine-tuning led to an increase in the error on targeted pixels at every weight tested. The architecture-specific failure fingerprint claimed by an earlier version of this project also failed to replicate across the datasets. Plausible failure explanations, in short, need to be tested before they are trusted; we release the protocol, code, ratings and every result file.

</details>


### [188] [SPGSR: A Small Pixel-Space Generator for One-Step Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34418)
*Yun Yue,Luming Liang,Yiqi Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPGSR 提出了一种新的单步超分辨率框架，通过在低分辨率图像上添加学习得到的 RGB 修正，使用一个更小的参数量（仅 VOSR-0.5B 生成器的 6.5%）实现了与现有顶级方法 VOSR 相当甚至更好的图像质量，并且在 GPU 管道速度方面也具有显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鉴于低分辨率图像已经提供了大量结构信息，本文探讨了是否可以使用一个更小的生成器以与现有大型模型相当或更好的性能来实现图像的超分辨率重建。

Method: 文章提出了 SPGSR 模型，通过在低分辨率图像上添加一个由冻结的 DINOv2 指导的 RGB 修正，并使用一个轻量级的块进行局部修正的细化。生成器所使用的参数量仅为现有最大模型 VOSR-1.4B 的 0.60%。

Result: 实验结果显示，SPGSR 在三个基准测试上的 PSNR/SSIM 值均高于 VOSR，且在 LPIPS/DISTS 值上更低。此外，对于 512x512 的输出，SPGSR 较 VOSR-0.5B 和 VOSR-1.4B 分别实现了 2.80 倍和 3.35 倍的 GPU 管道速度优势。

Conclusion: SPGSR 成功地通过使用一个显著更小的生成器尺寸，实现了与现有最强单步超分辨率方法可比甚至更优的图像质量，证明了利用低分辨率图像的结构信息可以在保持模型尺寸小化的同时提高超分辨率重建的性能。

Abstract: In one-step super-resolution (SR), vision-only models extract semantic cues directly from the low-resolution (LR) image, keeping those cues spatially aligned with the structures they help restore. Recent models achieve high restoration quality with generators of up to 1.4 billion parameters. Given how much structure the LR image already provides, could a much smaller generator achieve comparable quality? We introduce SPGSR, a vision-only framework that adds a learned RGB correction to the LR image. A frozen DINOv2 guides prediction, and a lightweight block refines the correction locally. SPGSR achieves higher PSNR/SSIM and lower LPIPS/DISTS than VOSR, a leading vision-only SR method, across three benchmarks. Its $31.8\mathrm{M}$-parameter generator is just $6.5\%$ the size of VOSR-0.5B's. SPGSR also achieves competitive quality against T2I-based SR methods. SPGSR's generator uses only $0.60\%$ of the HR pixels used to train and distill VOSR's generator. For $512\times512$ outputs, SPGSR achieves $2.80\times$ and $3.35\times$ GPU-pipeline speedups over VOSR-0.5B and VOSR-1.4B, respectively.

</details>


### [189] [Can Visual World Models See Force?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34422)
*Felix Tristram,Benjamin D. Killeen,Nassir Navab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究评估了视觉基础模型在表示交互力方面的能力，发现大多数模型能够检测到接触力是否发生，但不能准确反映和跟踪交互过程中力的大小变化，强调了当前模型存在的关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于神经生理学的发现，该研究探讨了视觉基础模型在代表交互过程中涉及的力方面的潜在能力，希望通过改进机器人操作模型来更好地理解人类如何感知和处理这些看不见的力。

Method: 研究使用了一种轻量级的读取器，训练它识别同步视频和力测量数据中的接触力数据，对静态图像、视频、世界模型和策略模型的表示进行了测试。

Result: 研究结果显示，大多数模型能够检测到接触力是否发生，但不能准确地反映和跟踪交互过程中力的大小变化。值得注意的是，即使是经过训练的机器人也没有提高这一能力，突显了当前模型在这方面存在的关键差距。

Conclusion: 研究结论表明，现有的视觉基础模型在表示交互力方面存在严重不足，需要进一步研究以增强模型在处理和表示隐藏变量的能力，例如交互力。

Abstract: World models and vision-language-action policy models are increasingly proposed as foundations for robot manipulation. They predict how scenes evolve and which actions to take, yet successful manipulation depends not solely on following observable trajectories but also on the hidden variable of interaction force. Neurophysiology has shown that humans possess a visual prior for this hidden variable, motivating us to ask the question: Do the representations of visual foundation models also capture the forces involved in an interaction? We define force awareness as the ability of representations to (1) indicate when contact force is applied, (2) track how it changes during an interaction, and (3) reflect how large it is. We probe frozen image, video, world- and policy model representations with a lightweight readout trained on synchronized video and force measurements from two manipulation datasets. Our experiments show that most models capture when contact occurs but not how much force is being applied. Surprisingly, robot post-training does not improve this either, highlighting an important gap in current models capabilities.

</details>


### [190] [PreserveReward: Reliable Consistency Reward Modeling for Image Editing via Dual-Teacher On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34446)
*Xueheng Li,Yong Liu,Wen Xue,Shi-Yu Tian,Zhuofan Xia,Chengjun Xie,Yipeng Sun,Simiu Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PreserveReward的图像编辑一致性奖励模型，通过结合视觉On-Policy Self-Distillation来提高细粒度视觉感知和一致性评估的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在实现图像编辑时，可能会无意中改变应保持不变的内容，因此需要可靠的评估手段来保证内容的一致性。

Method: 该方法通过回归得分损失从参考保存分数中学习，并采用两种互补的老师视角进行自我蒸馏。局部老师利用匹配的剪辑来识别细微视觉变化，而联合老师结合局部细节与全局语境来判断这些变化是否符合编辑意图。此外，使用最小KL投影校准老师评分分布，并根据计分目标与梯度对齐适应性地选择教师过程监督，从而提高蒸馏监督的可靠性。

Result: 在多个基准上广泛实验表明，PreserveReward在非编辑内容保存方面与人类偏好有很强的契合，并且在各种编辑场景下都能很好地泛化。

Conclusion: 这项工作提供了一种新的图像编辑奖励模型PreserveReward，以提高细粒度视觉感知和一致性评估的准确性，并能够在多种编辑场景中可靠地执行。

Abstract: Instruction-guided image editing has a wide range of applications, yet existing models may inadvertently alter content that should remain unchanged. Reliable consistency evaluation is therefore essential for assessing content preservation and providing reward signals for Reinforcement Learning (RL). However, existing reward models may overlook subtle visual changes or fail to distinguish necessary modifications from unintended alterations, limiting the reliability of consistency assessment. To address this challenge, we propose PreserveReward, a consistency reward model for image editing that integrates visual On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) to enhance fine-grained visual perception and consistency assessment. PreserveReward learns from reference preservation scores through a regression-based scoring loss and employs two complementary teacher views for self-distillation. The local teacher uses matched crops to identify subtle visual changes, while the joint teacher combines local details with global context to determine whether these changes align with the editing intent. To improve the reliability of distillation supervision, we calibrate teacher score distributions via minimum-KL projection under reference-score error constraints and adaptively select teacher process supervision based on gradient alignment with the scoring objective, mitigating scoring bias and optimization conflicts. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that PreserveReward achieves strong alignment with human preference on non-edited content preservation and generalizes well across diverse editing scenarios.

</details>


### [191] [SceneDesigner++: A Unified 9-DoF Pose Control Framework for T2I and T2V Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34461)
*Xincheng Shuai,Zhenyuan Qin,Henghui Ding,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneDesigner++ 是一种统一框架，用于同时处理文本到图像 (T2I) 和文本到视频 (T2V) 生成中的 9 自由度 (DoF) 位姿控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的文本到图像或文本到视频生成方法在姿态控制和时间连贯性方面存在局限性。

Method: 框架包括绝对姿态编码和相对姿态编码策略，通过CNOCS图序列和VPRoPE机制实现跨帧动作建模，并通过可学习的正交变换保持输入特征的规范性。

Result: SceneDesigner++ 在姿态保真度和运动质量方面优于基于同一骨干的基线。

Conclusion: SceneDesigner++ 通过结合绝对和相对姿态编码策略，支持分别或联合控制物体和相机运动，为 T2I 和 T2V 任务提供了新的基准和评估方法。

Abstract: Precise 9-DoF pose control is essential for visual content generation, enabling users to specify object location, size, and orientation in 3D space. However, existing methods typically focus on either text-to-image (T2I) or text-to-video (T2V) generation, with limitations in pose controllability and temporal coherence. We present SceneDesigner++, a unified framework for 9-DoF pose control across both T2I and T2V generation. We construct ObjectPose9D++, a dataset containing high-quality images and videos with accurate pose annotations. Trained on ObjectPose9D++, SceneDesigner++ combines absolute and relative pose encoding strategies to decouple frame-wise spatial grounding from inter-frame motion modeling. Specifically, absolute pose encoding leverages CNOCS map sequences to provide explicit, frame-wise 9-DoF pose guidance. Meanwhile, our relative pose encoding, termed VPRoPE, incorporates relative pose information into rotary position embeddings to explicitly model inter-frame pose transitions, thereby promoting temporally coherent object motion. A learnable orthogonal transformation is further introduced for each attention head, allowing different heads to capture complementary geometric relationships while preserving input feature norms to promote training stability. We further adopt a two-stage training strategy to learn the absolute and relative pose encoding mechanisms separately. Benefiting from this lightweight and decoupled design, SceneDesigner++ also supports integration with an independently trained camera-control module, enabling separate or joint control over object and camera motion. Finally, we introduce T2IVPoseEval, a benchmark for evaluating diverse object poses and motion patterns in both T2I and T2V tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SceneDesigner++ consistently outperforms same-backbone baselines in pose fidelity and motion quality.

</details>


### [192] [Evidence-Aligned Benchmarking and Learning for Medical Video Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34465)
*Zhongpai Gao,Anwesa Choudhuri,Meng Zheng,Benjamin Planche,Terrence Chen,Ziyan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了MedEvidU方法，解决了医学视频语言模型在三个关键方面的表现不佳问题：忽略了视频证据的回答、帧间共享编码导致混淆以及模型的记忆效应。通过使用MedVidBench-v2基准测试集进行验证，以及提出Label-matched Question Resampling (LQR) 和 Query-slot 输入策略，显著提高了模型对视频片段的理解和响应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的医学视频语言模型常常无法有效利用视频中的视觉证据进行正确回答，尤其是在数据集标准上表现不佳，且存在模型对视频内容理解不够准确的问题。研究者旨在通过MedEvidU方法改进模型的性能。

Method: 研究者构建了一个新的基准测试集MedVidBench-v2，该集合包括34个任务，分别来自18个外科和临床视频数据集。为了解决多帧共享编码问题，他们提出了Query-slot 输入策略。为了解决模型的记忆效应问题，他们引入了Label-matched Question Resampling (LQR) 策略。

Result: 在MedVidBench-v2基准测试集上，通过采用MedEvidU方法，4B模型的Grounding IoU从30.4提升到44.4，甚至超过了27B的监督训练模型，而在未见过的新医院中的仪器定位准确率也从33.9提升到72.5。此外，在随机切割的窗口上，事件检测准确性和定位F1分数分别提高了5.5和8.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究证明了MedEvidU方法的有效性，能够显著提升医学视频语言模型对视频片段的地面真实度和定位能力，为实际应用中的问题解答提供了更为可靠的依据。

Abstract: A medical video language model should answer from the visual evidence its question asks about. We find three places where this breaks down and address each with an approach we call MedEvidU. First, benchmarks reward answers that ignore the video: on one dataset, the position of a clip within its recording predicts the surgical step in 37% of questions, and a constant answer beats every trained model on a standard safety-assessment metric. We build MedVidBench-v2, 34 tasks from 18 surgical and clinical video datasets, in which prompts and metrics are audited with answers given without video, and which tests unseen hospitals and windows cut blindly from whole recordings. Second, common video encoders merge consecutive frames into shared tokens, so a question about one frame is answered from a blend of two. Query-slot inputs give each queried frame its own tokens at almost no extra cost. Third, a fine-tuned model has memorized its training questions, which leaves little to learn from. Label-matched question resampling (LQR) adds unseen questions about the same recordings while keeping the answer distribution fixed, in a final supervised epoch and in reinforcement learning. Together, these raise the grounding IoU of a 4B model from 30.4 to 44.4, above a supervised 27B model, and instrument grounding at an unseen hospital from 33.9 to 72.5. On blindly cut windows, event-presence accuracy improves by 5.5 points and localization F1 by 8.1 points. Project page: https://gaozhongpai.github.io/MedEvidU/

</details>


### [193] [Attacking Hard-Label Large Vision-Language Models with Model-Sensitive Adversarial Patch Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34467)
*Nian Ai,Guangke Chen,Xiaowen Cai,Zhongliang Guo,Daizong Liu,Pan Zhou,Ognjen Arandjelović*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HardPatch的新颖方法，旨在仅通过查询模型生成对抗性视觉补丁，以评估LVLMs在局部视觉区域的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究显示LVLMs容易受到攻击，但攻击者依赖后向传播梯度进行优化，这在实际应用中是不可行的。本文旨在填补这一研究缺口，提出了一种在实际且具有挑战性的硬标签设置下评估LVLMs对抗性对抗性的方法。

Method: 该方法首先将图像均匀分割成小块，并对每个块进行遮掩以评估其对LVLM模型的敏感性。之后，按照敏感性得分的降序选择最脆弱的块进行噪声初始化和进一步的随机噪声优化，最终生成对抗性图像。

Result: 实验结果表明，HardPatch能够在各种LVLM模型和数据集上生成有效的对抗性图像，成功攻击硬标签的LVLMs。

Conclusion: HardPatch提供了一种在有限信息条件下评估LVLMs对抗性漏洞的有效方法，有助于更好地理解并提升模型的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in handling multi-modal downstream tasks, gaining increasing popularity. However, recent studies show that LVLMs are susceptible to both intentional and inadvertent attacks. Existing attackers ideally optimize adversarial perturbations with backpropagated gradients from LVLMs, thus limiting their scalability in practical scenarios as real-world LVLM applications will not provide any LVLM's gradient or details. Motivated by this research gap and counter-practical phenomenon, we propose a novel hard-label attack method for LVLMs, named HardPatch, to generate visual adversarial patches by solely querying the model. Our method provides deeper insights into how to investigate the vulnerability of LVLMs in local visual regions and generate corresponding adversarial substitution under the practical yet challenging hard-label setting. Specifically, we first split each image into uniform patches and mask each of them to individually assess their sensitivity to the LVLM model. Then, according to the descending order of sensitive scores, we iteratively select the most vulnerable patch to initialize noise and estimate gradients with further additive random noises for optimization. In this manner, multiple patches are perturbed until the altered image satisfies the adversarial condition. Extensive LVLM models and datasets are evaluated to demonstrate the adversarial nature of HardPatch. Our empirical observations suggest that with appropriate patch substitution and optimization, HardPatch can craft effective adversarial images to attack hard-label LVLMs.

</details>


### [194] [TACC: Target-Aware Carrier Compression for Dense-Context Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34476)
*Yiming Fan,Yijia Guo,Zewen Guo,Liwen Hu,Yuheng Yuan,Yang Li,Zhiwei Li,Xitong Ling,Caihong Yuan,Shengbo Chen,Tiejun Huang,Lei Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TACC（目标感知压缩模块）通过在一个目标感知压缩框架中组织源视图，实现有效的视图合成，显著提升了在密集输入场景下的有效帧率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖全局场景上下文，导致高内存和延迟开销，阻碍了对密集源视图上下文的处理。TACC通过目标感知压缩来解决此问题。

Method: TACC通过将每个源视图组织成逐步的载体层级结构，依据源-目标几何相关性选择性地检索令牌，动态分配计算配额，生成紧凑的目标条件表示。

Result: 在RE10K数据集上实验表明，TACC在大场景视图合成（LagerNVS）和轻量级场景模型（LVSM）中分别实现了比全上下文设置9.23倍和64倍更高的有效帧率。

Conclusion: TACC为前馈隐式新颖视图合成提供了一种有效的解决方案，显著提高了处理密集源视图上下文的能力。

Abstract: Recent feed-forward approaches mark a paradigm shift in novel view synthesis by eliminating the reliance on per-scene optimization, directly inferring 3D representations from input views. However, their reliance on global scene-token context incurs substantial memory and latency overhead, constraining their ability to handle increasingly dense source-view contexts. Compressing source representations offers an intuitive way to limit this token budget. Prior general-purpose compression methods are not tailored for novel view synthesis, as their fixed, target-agnostic nature fails to adapt evidence allocation to individual target views. To overcome this, we introduce TACC, a target-aware compression module tailored for feed-forward latent NVS. Specifically, TACC organizes each source view into a progressive carrier hierarchy. Driven by source-target geometric relevance, it adaptively retrieves tokens to dynamically allocate exact computational quotas. This selective retrieval directly yields a compact, target-conditioned representation. Extensive experiments on the RE10K demonstrate that under dense input settings, TACC achieves $9.23\times$ and $64\times$ higher Effective FPS than Full Context on LagerNVS and LVSM, respectively. Effective FPS excludes one-time source encoding and Carrier construction.

</details>


### [195] [Feed-Forward Adaptive Gaussian Splatting for Interactive Medical Volume Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34478)
*Zhongpai Gao,Benjamin Planche,Meng Zheng,Anwesa Choudhuri,Chaoyi Zhou,Terrence Chen,Ziyan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RenderFM-Next 是一种基于前馈的模型，解决了 RenderFM 的多种限制，能够处理 CT 和 MR 影像，具有更快的渲染速度和更精细的图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统路径追踪渲染方法在每次改变视角、裁剪平面或传输函数时都需要重新计算，限制了帧率。而 RenderFM 模型则可以快速预测高斯体，但由于其对 CT 和 MR 的处理能力限制和像素化问题，效果并不理想。因此，需要一种新的模型来解决这些问题。

Method: RenderFM-Next 是一种前馈模型，使用单个网络处理 CT 和 MR，预测粗体高斯并在需要时进行细化。该模型使用操作符精确裁剪高斯体，并在裁剪面附近始终进行细化。

Result: RenderFM-Next 在 CT 和 MR 数据集上比重新训练的 RenderFM 分别提高了 2.8 dB 和 2.4 dB，渲染 1600x1600 视图所需时间仅为 4-6 ms。细化技术进一步提升了渲染质量，而少量高斯体的精炼在性能上有显著提升。

Conclusion: RenderFM-Next 提供了一种高效的 CT 和 MR 图像渲染方案，结合了快速预测和精炼细化技术，达到了更高精度和更快的速度。

Abstract: Path-traced rendering shows CT and MR volumes with realistic lighting, but it restarts after every change of view, clipping plane or transfer function (TF), the map from voxel intensity to colour and opacity, which limits the frame rate. Feed-forward Gaussian splatting models such as RenderFM predict the Gaussians directly from the volume instead of fitting them to each scan, and are fast. RenderFM, however, handles only CT, runs its network again after every TF edit, has no clipping operator, and places its Gaussians on a uniform 3-mm grid that blurs organ boundaries and cut faces. We present RenderFM-Next, a feed-forward model that addresses these limits. One network handles both CT and MR. Its Gaussians are predicted once per volume: a new TF needs no pass of this network, and a clipping plane cuts them exactly, using operators from prior work. The model predicts coarse 3-mm Gaussians for the whole volume and refines them to 1.5 mm only where refinement improves the image. A small score head ranks the coarse Gaussians by how much refining them would reduce the rendering error; at render time the user chooses how many are refined, and those next to a clipping plane are always refined, which sharpens cut faces. We build a benchmark of about 2,000 CT and MR scans with path-traced references under clinical presets, TF edits and clipping. Our RenderFM-Next improves over RenderFM retrained on this benchmark by 2.8 dB on CT and 2.4 dB on MR and renders a 1600x1600 view in 4-6 ms. Refinement adds 0.30 / 0.69 dB over the same model trained without it (0.51 / 1.17 dB on cut faces), and refining a learned 10% of the coarse Gaussians matches a random 50% with 2.4x fewer Gaussians. Project page: https://gaozhongpai.github.io/renderfmnext/

</details>


### [196] [ProManO: Beyond Sim(3) for Metric Upgrade of Feed-Forward Geometry with Visual-Inertial Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34480)
*Lingxiang Hu,Zhenjun Zhao,Qi Shao,Naima Ait Oufroukh,Fabien Bonardi,Raymond Ghandour,Shenghai Yuan,Javier Civera,Baoru Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了ProManO框架，用于通过视觉惯性里程计进行密集预测与稀疏度量锚点的对齐，在不损害先验标定内参的情况下，通过包含三维自由度的十维流形来最小化深度相关的残差，实验在多个数据集上证明了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法使用相似变换或投影变换对准密集预测和稀疏度量锚点，但无法处理深度相关的失真或改变已标定的内参。该研究旨在设计一个框架，既能进行相似性对准，又能通过考虑三维空间中的深度不确定性来最小化深度相关的残差。

Method: 文章提出了ProManO框架，首先证明了兼容先验标定内参的变换形成了一个十维流形，其中包含可用于抵消深度相关残差的三个自由度。为了估计这些自由度，提出了一个基于概率流形优化的估计框架，其中每一对准时根据其沿视束的不确定性以及在单个期望最大化过程中的内点概率加权。

Result: 实验结果表明，ProManO在多个数据集上的平均F1值最高，并且在所有数据集上均表现优于仿射对准。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了ProManO在对准密集预测与稀疏度量锚点方面具有优越性，尤其在保持先验标定内参不变的情况下有效减小了深度相关的残差。

Abstract: Upgrading feed-forward geometry to metric scale with visual-inertial odometry (VIO) raises the question of which correction should align dense feed-forward prediction with sparse metric VIO anchors. Existing methods use either a similarity transformation, which cannot represent depth-dependent distortion, or a projective transformation, which alters pre-calibrated intrinsics. We prove that the transformations compatible with pre-calibrated intrinsics form a ten-dimensional manifold. Beyond Sim(3), this manifold contains three degrees of freedom (DoF) that locate the plane at infinity and leave residuals growing quadratically with depth range after any similarity alignment. These DoF are invisible in the image and can be resolved only by metric depth along viewing rays or by multi-view constraints. To estimate them, we propose ProManO, a probabilistic manifold optimization framework in which every anchor is weighted by its along-ray uncertainty and inlier probability within a single expectation-maximization (EM) procedure. ProManO certifies the similarity synchronization and refines the plane at infinity only where it is observable, which guarantees an objective value no worse than that of the optimal similarity alignment. Experiments on EuRoC, CID-SIMS, Oxford Spires, and KITTI-360 demonstrate that ProManO achieves the highest mean F1 and outperforms Sim(3) alignment on all four datasets. The project page is available at https://h0xg.github.io/ProManO/.

</details>


### [197] [Who Is Ego? Scaling Cross-View Camera Wearer Identification and Segmentation beyond Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34489)
*Yifan Jin,Zheng Zhang,Tianwen Qian,Dong Wang,Bo Zhao,Yanwei Fu,Yuqian Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了FindEgo-1M数据集和EgoRay模型，用于通过第三方视角图像识别并分割第一人称视角的穿戴者。EgoRay模型利用几何关系进行无监督训练，并在多个设置中达到了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展相机佩戴者识别任务至机器人、车辆、动物和无人机等，拓宽识别范围，并引入FindEgo-1M数据集，提供广泛的真实和模拟数据。

Method: 研究利用第三方视角图像中第一人称视角相机的投影（即epipole）来定位穿戴者的位置。EgoRay模型首先通过几何读取计算候选者口罩或分割提案相对于ego相机的角度和距离。然后通过姿态监督训练和无标签的测试时自适应，优化了模型。

Result: EgoRay-ZS在原标签上达到了78.0%的识别准确率，优于对标方法PEN的52.9%。在FindEgo-1M数据集上，EgoRay分别在已知和未知环境下实现了87.1和77.7的最优准确率，且在匹配查询中，与GPT-6 Astra相比，识别准确率提高了31.6%和21.3%，同时运行速度提升了18倍。

Conclusion: 这项研究通过引入FindEgo-1M数据集和改进的EgoRay-ZS模型，在相机佩戴者识别任务上取得了重要进展，扩展了应用场景和技术性能。

Abstract: A first-person (ego) image reveals what a camera sees, but not who or what carries it. Given a synchronized third-person (exo) image, camera wearer identification asks who is ego: which candidate carries the ego camera? We extend this task beyond humans to robots, vehicles, animals, and drones, and broaden its spatial scope (where is ego) from selecting a provided candidate mask to wearer segmentation with absence prediction and mutual camera localization across ego-exo views. We introduce FindEgo-1M, comprising 1.16M real and simulated ego-exo pairs across 12 wearer types, 17 domains, and 111 test settings. Our approach exploits a geometric relation: the ego camera's projection into the exo image, i.e., the epipole, lies on or near its wearer. We introduce EgoRay-ZS, the first method to turn a frozen feed-forward 3D reconstruction model, VGGT-$Ω$, into a training-free solution for wearer identification and segmentation. Its geometric readout ranks candidate masks or segmentation proposals by angular offset and distance to the ego camera, while the relative pose provides mutual camera directions. We then develop the full EgoRay model through pose-supervised training on FindEgo-1M and label-free test-time adaptation constrained by candidate masks. Without task-specific training, EgoRay-ZS achieves 78.0% identification accuracy on TF2023's original labels, versus 52.9% reported for the dataset-trained PEN. On FindEgo-1M, EgoRay achieves state-of-the-art chance-corrected accuracy of 87.1 and 77.7 on seen and unseen settings. On matched queries, EgoRay also outperforms GPT-6 Astra by 31.6 and 21.3 points on seen and unseen settings, respectively, while running $18\times$ faster. Data, code, and models will be released.

</details>


### [198] [Decoder-as-a-Probe: Verbalizing and Steering Visual Representations in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34515)
*Adhemar de Senneville,Xavier Bou,Jérémy Anger,Rafael Grompone,Gabriele Facciolo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DaP通过低压道作为探针的方法，使LVLM的感知更为可控，尤其是在数据稀疏的情况下。这种方法简洁高效，有助于LVLM的相关研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前方法如经典探针和方向盘在处理稀疏训练数据时效果不佳。因此，为了提高LVLM的可解释性和对特定属性的控制，提出了Decoder-as-a-Probe (DaP)，利用LVLM解码器的一阶泰勒展开作为分类器。

Method: DaP 方法利用低压道的第一阶泰勒展开作为分类器进行探针分析。这种方法在特定属性上具有高度可控性，能有效地调整LVLM的感知。

Result: 实验结果显示，DaP 在处理稀疏训练数据时显著优于传统方法。它可以清晰地描述LVLM对给定图像的感知，并能手术方式改变LVLM对特定属性的认识。

Conclusion: 鉴于其简单性和数据效率，我们相信 DaP 可以作为插件工具，用于LVLM研究中的可解释性、幻觉缓解或指令调优。

Abstract: It is commonly accepted that high-level concepts are represented linearly in the representation space of large neural networks. For Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs), finding those interpretable directions in visual representations is important for interpretability. To find them, the classical approach is to train a linear classifier on the representations, but it needs a large amount of labels and only recovers what can be read from the representations, not what the model actually uses. In this paper we start from the following insight: The LLM Decoder of an LVLM is also a classifier whose classes are the possible next tokens. So we use its first-order Taylor expansion as a linear classifier. Hence, we introduce Decoder-as-a-Probe (DaP), which significantly outperforms classical probing and steering baselines especially when training data is sparse. (1) It verbalizes what an LVLM perceives in a given image. (2) It can surgically change the perception of the LVLM about specific attributes (e.g. change only the color, the clothes worn or the action done). Due to its simplicity and data efficiency, we believe DaP can serve as a plug-and-play tool for LVLM research, such as interpretability, hallucination mitigation or instruction tuning.

</details>


### [199] [Rank Before You Encode: Reading a Few Percent of a Whole-Slide Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34523)
*Haoyu He,Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究引入了一种选择性推理方法，通过冻结全袋模型并仅处理精选的高倍率样本区域，显著降低了编码成本，同时保持了较高的分类准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高效方法通过选择较少的切片来减少推理成本，但通常需要训练新的模型。该研究探讨了在保持原有全袋模型不变的情况下仅处理精选样本区域的可行性。

Method: 该研究使用了一个轻量级的挑选器，该挑选器基于冻结模型的注意力机制，无需额外的标签。挑选器根据缩略图来评估切片区域，选择高倍率下编码的顶级区域，并按比例比例选择进行进一步处理。

Result: 在成本为(K,b)=(1000,10)时，编码成本约占全袋模型的6.6%，比全袋模型快10-14倍。在成本为(K,b)=(500,10)时，成本降至约4%，但推理速度加快13-22倍，不过在CAMELYON17任务中AUROC下降最多为0.035。

Conclusion: 随着挑选成本的降低，选择性的推理可以实现更高的效率，同时在大多数任务上保持与全袋模型相近的性能。该研究建议将现有模型应用在更少的样本上作为新高效模型的基准测试，以进行公平比较。

Abstract: Classifying a whole-slide image requires encoding thousands of high-resolution tiles with a pathology foundation model, which accounts for most of the inference cost. Existing efficient methods reduce this cost by selecting fewer tiles, but typically train a new model around their selection strategy. A largely unexplored alternative is to keep an existing full-bag model frozen and run it on selected tiles at the same encoder cost. We introduce this comparison, alongside random tile sampling at matched cost. To select tiles, we fit a lightweight selector with 3.3K parameters to the attention of a frozen model, without additional slide labels. The selector ranks regions from a slide thumbnail, encodes the top K regions at 5x magnification, and selects b for processing at 20x. We evaluate seven frozen MIL aggregators on six tasks and compare them with nine efficient methods trained on the same features and splits. At a budget of (K,b)=(1000,10), encoding costs about 6.6% of the full bag and is 10-14x faster, excluding tiling. The AUROC drop is no more than 0.005 in 33 of 36 aggregator-task pairs. At (500,10), the cost falls to about 4% and inference is 13-22x faster, but AUROC drops by up to 0.035 on CAMELYON17, where small metastases are easily missed. Selection improves over random sampling by about 0.10 AUROC on tasks with focal lesions, while random sampling performs well on diffuse tasks. In our comparisons, the frozen model at (500,10) is ahead of nine efficient methods in 55 of 60 paired comparisons. Giving an existing WSI model fewer tiles is thus a baseline that a new efficient model should be compared with at matched encoder cost.

</details>


### [200] [CADENCE: Sensor-GroundedWorld-Consistent Gaussian SLAM for Collaborative Mobile Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34549)
*Reshad Ul Karim,Abrar Samin,Md Rashad Al Hasan Rony*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CADENCE通过将匹配寻找与地图更新决策分离，利用传感器导向的区域记忆和多种特征进行匹配，并通过确定的几何验证和可选的物理检查接受匹配，同时使用软置信加权减轻图像退化的影响，改善了协作式室内地图构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作式重建室内地图可以更快更完整，但不同设备产生的观测值质量不一且跨设备匹配可能出错，从而影响地图质量。

Method: CADENCE系统通过区域记忆模块总结已有的观测值，结合相对姿态、重力、气压高度和观测质量等特征进行匹配，通过几何验证和物理校验决策匹配接受情况，同时使用软置信加权处理观测值更新。

Result: CADENCE在八个未经训练的记忆的Matterport3D房屋上进行了评估，区域记忆的AUROC值范围为0.95至0.97，几何验证将假链接率保持在0.5%以下，软置信加权减少了平均PSNR损失，使用惯性加平台运动跟踪所有iPhone扫描，具有6.98cm的平均绝对轨迹误差，且生成的细化地图在渲染质量上优于基线。

Conclusion: CADENCE提高了协作式同时定位与地图构建的鲁棒性和准确性，为改善多用户合作创建高质量室内地图提供了新的方法。

Abstract: Collaborative reconstruction with consumer phones promises faster and more complete indoor maps. However, heterogeneous devices introduce degraded observations and unreliable cross-device associations, so a single poor contributor or incorrect match can corrupt the shared Gaussian map. To address this, we propose \textbf{CADENCE}, a collaborative Gaussian simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) system that separates finding cross-device matches from deciding whether and how peer observations update the map. To find matches, a sensor-grounded regional memory summarizes committed observations and ranks candidate peer regions using appearance, relative pose, gravity, barometric altitude, and observation quality. Deterministic geometric verification and optional physical checks then decide which of the proposed matches are accepted. Once a match is accepted, separate cue-based confidences control how its observations contribute to pose constraints, geometry, appearance, and memory. As a result, useful depth can still contribute even when color is unreliable. Throughout this process, local tracking remains isolated from mapping, verification, and networking. We evaluate CADENCE on eight Matterport3D houses that were not used to train the memory. On these houses, regional memory achieves an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of $0.95$ to $0.97$ and thus outperforms appearance retrieval, while verification keeps false-link rates below $0.5\,\%$. Furthermore, soft confidence weighting reduces the mean receiver peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) loss from $2.66$ to $1.05$\,dB across eight degradations while retaining useful peer depth. Finally, using inertial and platform motion, CADENCE tracks all twelve held-out iPhone scans with a mean absolute trajectory error of $6.98$\,cm, and its refined maps achieve better average rendering quality than the baselines.

</details>


### [201] [Score-based Black-box Attack via Adversarial Condition Guided Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34564)
*Yuhang Chen,Chih-Hong Cheng,Kurt Debattista,Siddartha Khastgir,Xingyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 文章提出了一种新的对抗攻击方法，通过使用逆向扩散过程并利用目标模型的输出概率来指导潜在更新，而无需访问其梯度。此外，还提出了一种目标搜索引导策略，以优化原始条件嵌入的微小扰动。该方法在ImageNet兼容数据集上优于基于传输的方法，在攻击效果和图像质量上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散过程的黑盒攻击方法依赖于代理模型与目标模型之间的转移特性，受两者差异影响较大，且在提升转移性的同时可能会降低图像质量和扭曲语义内容。为此，文章旨在改进这些局限性，提出了一种新的攻击策略。

Method: 首先，文章采用分数为基础的黑盒攻击方法适应逆向扩散过程，利用目标模型的输出概率指导潜在更新；其次，引入了优化目标搜索引导策略，这种方法通过分数基础攻击搜索原始条件嵌入的小幅度扰动。

Result: 实验结果表明，与基于传输的方法相比，在攻击效果和图像质量方面，该框架具有更好的表现。

Conclusion: 提出的逆向扩散方法可以更有效地进行黑盒对抗攻击，同时还能保证较高的图像质量。

Abstract: Generative models enable unrestricted adversarial attacks that aim to fool classifiers while preserving the natural appearance and semantics of the original image. However, existing diffusion-based black-box attacks largely rely on transferability from surrogates, making their effectiveness sensitive to differences between surrogate and victim. Improving transferability can also distort semantic content and degrade image quality. Moreover, retaining the original prompt to preserve fidelity leaves the diffusion model's conditional prior underused for adversarial guidance. We address these limitations by adapting score-based black-box attacks to reverse diffusion, using the victim's output probabilities to guide latent updates without accessing its gradients. We further introduce adaptive-target guidance, which uses the victim's current runner-up class as an adversarial condition to steer denoising towards the decision boundary. Because such guidance can introduce unwanted semantic changes, we also propose target-search guidance, which searches for a small perturbation of the original condition embedding using a score-based attack. This achieves comparable attack effectiveness with better image quality than runner-up guidance. Experiments on the ImageNet-Compatible dataset show that our framework outperforms transfer-based baselines in both attack effectiveness and image quality.

</details>


### [202] [Physics-Guided Generative Modelling of Oxy-Combustion Flame Images: From Conditional LSGANs to Denoising Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34568)
*Imane Er-rabhi,Gorkem Oztarlik,Jean Caudal,Renaud Gaudron*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文开发了两种生成对抗网络类型和物理引导的去噪扩散概率模型，用于通过直接映射15维连续操作参数到512x64热释放火焰图像来加速工业氧燃烧器设计的验证过程。两种模型分别通过单次生成和迭代采样的方式，提升了设计探索速度，同时保持了图像的真实性和物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业氧燃烧器设计的传统验证方法成本高昂且迭代设计探索速度较慢，本文旨在开发生成模型作为更快速有效的实验替代方法。

Method: 本文主要采用条件最小子平方生成对抗网络(cLSGAN-L1-CBN)和物理引导的去噪扩散概率模型(DDPM)作为生成模型。cLSGAN-L1-CBN通过单次生成来实现即时推断；而DDPM则通过迭代采样有助于热释放梯度的平滑和连续性。

Result: cLSGAN在验证集上实现了RMSE 0.022和FID 20.68的性能，可在移动工作站GPU上以约20毫秒的速度生成图像，而DDPM经过长时间训练后，达到了RMSE 0.019和FID 20.29的性能，但每生成一张图像需约30秒。

Conclusion: 这两种生成模型都证明了它们可以作为可靠的燃烧实验视觉替代方案，尤其在cLSGAN能够以极快的速度提供接近实时的推断结果，且在物理上保持一致性。

Abstract: Validating industrial oxy-combustion burner designs traditionally requires costly experimental campaigns in which each operating condition is physically reproduced on an instrumented rig and imaged via OH* chemiluminescence, rendering iterative design exploration prohibitively slow. This work develops deep generative surrogate models that map a 15-dimensional vector of continuous operating parameters (thermal power, swirl numbers, momentum ratios, mixture fractions, etc.) directly to a $512\times64$ heat-release flame image, trained on 1,609 experimental samples from the OXYTEC dual-swirl non-premixed oxy-combustion database. Two complementary generative paradigms are investigated, as they offer opposite strengths for surrogate modelling: a custom spatially-anchored conditional least-squares GAN (cLSGAN-L1-CBN), whose single-pass generation enables near-instantaneous inference, and a custom physics-guided denoising diffusion probabilistic model (DDPM), whose iterative sampling favours the smooth, continuous heat-release gradients of fluid flows. Both are validated through ablation on a stratified held-out test set. The optimized cLSGAN reaches RMSE 0.022 and FID 20.68 and generates an image in about 20 ms on a mobile workstation GPU, though extended training leaves its texture realism unchanged; the DDPM improves steadily over long training horizons, reaching RMSE 0.019 and FID 20.29 at 40,000 epochs, at the cost of roughly thirty seconds per image on the same hardware --- three orders of magnitude slower than the adversarial surrogate. A nearest-neighbour analysis traces the persistent worst-case errors to sparsely sampled regions of the parameter space, and a parameter-sensitivity study confirms smooth, physically consistent responses to perturbed inputs. These results demonstrate that carefully guided generative models can act as reliable visual surrogates for combustion experiments.

</details>


### [203] [ADClaw: A Programmable Visual Agent for Industrial Anomaly Detection and Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34591)
*Xinyi Wu,Rongxin Gao,YuHui Lin,Yonglin Hong,Chu Li,Wei Li,Dingzhao Li,Shijia Zhao,Chaopeng Li,Haixin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ADClaw 是一种无需针对特定任务进行微调的检测代理，使用可执行代码测试异常假设。它通过结构化的正常性先验，结合先前步骤的掩码、异常图和测量值，进行检测试验，并通过证据评估指导下一步操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业异常检测方法依赖于大型领域特定数据集，且在检查过程中无法重新审视模糊区域。预定义的工具界面限制了代理商结合中间结果的能力。

Method: ADClaw 算法首先利用结构化的正常性先验识别适用规则并检索正常视觉引用，然后在持续的检工作台中编写并执行代码以结合早期步骤的结果，进行针对怀疑缺陷的新测试。之后，通过证据评估进行指导。

Result: ADClaw 在多个 2D 和 3D 工业异常检测基准测试和 MMAD 异常理解中均达到竞争力结果。

Conclusion: ADClaw 为工业异常检测提供了一种创新的方法，无需特定任务微调，并且能更准确地识别和定位潜在的微妙缺陷和错误类型。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly detection (IAD), the task of identifying and localizing deviations from normal product conditions, is essential to automated quality inspection in manufacturing. Many vision-language models for IAD rely on large domain-specific datasets for fine-tuning and make predictions in a single pass, leaving no opportunity to re-examine ambiguous regions. Although tool-using agents can revisit these regions, predefined tool interfaces limit how they can combine intermediate results in a follow-up check. When such checks are insufficient, models may miss subtle defects or misidentify the defect type. To address this limitation, we introduce ADClaw, an inspection agent that uses executable code to test anomaly hypotheses without task-specific fine-tuning. For each hypothesis, a Structured Normality Prior identifies the applicable rules and retrieves normal visual references for the inspection target. Guided by this prior, the agent writes and executes code in a persistent Inspection Workbench to combine masks, anomaly maps, and measurements from earlier steps into a new test of the suspected defect. After each check, Hypothesis-Guided Evidence Reasoning assesses whether the evidence supports the suspected defect and its location, using gaps or inconsistencies in that evidence to guide the next code action. ADClaw achieves competitive results in detection and localization across 2D and 3D industrial anomaly detection benchmarks and in anomaly understanding on MMAD.

</details>


### [204] [SEAM: A Self-Evolving and Adaptive Probing Framework for Automatic MLLM Hallucination Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34598)
*Shiyu Zhang,Zengyi Wo,Yuming Liao,Ran He,Caifeng Shan,Yanbo Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SEAM是一个自我进化的自动多模态大语言模型幻觉评估框架，通过内在和外在循环机制，利用技能引导的视觉分析、问题组成和层级质量验证来精细测试项目，揭示模型特有的幻觉，并通过历史探针轨迹提炼出局部可重用的规则更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的评估管道依赖于预定义的测试项目，这在构建成本、快速过时以及难以检测模型特有弱点等方面存在局限性。为此，提出SEAM框架，以解决这些问题，提高评估的大规模适应性和灵活性。

Method: SEAM框架包含一个可组合的幻觉探针构建库，该库包括十种幻觉探针技能、三种引出包装器和五种问题格式。在内在循环中，SEAM协调技术员和目标响应反馈，以优化测试项目并发现模型特定的幻觉。在外在循环中，技能更新器将历史探针路径提炼为可重用的规则更新，并将其冻结以测试。

Result: SEAM在四种目标大语言模型和十种幻觉类型上进行了实验，结果显示与代表性静态基线相比，SEAM在平均有效幻觉发现率的相对改进率为89.2%。

Conclusion: SEAM框架建立了一个自适应和自我进化的评估模式，该模式能够更有效地发现和识别大语言模型中的幻觉问题。

Abstract: The severe susceptibility of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to hallucinations poses a critical threat to their reliability, making a rigorous evaluation framework indispensable. However, existing evaluation pipelines largely rely on predefined test items, suffering from high construction costs, rapid obsolescence as MLLMs evolve, and ineffectiveness in probing model-specific weaknesses. To address these limitations, we propose SEAM, a self-evolving and adaptive probing framework for automatic MLLM hallucination evaluation. Specifically, SEAM introduces a composable Hallucination Probe Construction Library comprising ten hallucination-probing skills, three elicitation wrappers, and five question formats. Building on this library, SEAM establishes a dual-loop framework. In the inner loop (adaptive probing), skill-guided visual analysis, question composition, and hierarchical quality verification are coordinated with verifier and target-response feedback to refine test items and uncover model-specific hallucinations. In the outer loop (skill evolution), a skill updater distills historical probing trajectories into localized, reusable rule updates, which undergo held-out validation and are frozen before testing. Driven by these innovative structural designs, SEAM establishes an automated and self-evolving evaluation paradigm. Experiments across four target MLLMs and ten hallucination types demonstrate that SEAM substantially outperforms representative static benchmarks, achieving an 89.2\% relative improvement in mean valid hallucination discovery rate across 12 matched comparisons.

</details>


### [205] [You Only Look Multiple Times: YoloMT, a Real-Time Streaming Model on a T4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34601)
*Nessim Ben Abbes,Duc Han Le,Sabri Mtibaa,Van-Tam Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: YoloMT 是一个基于单 GPU 的实时流式字幕生成模型，参数量为 449.8M，它在同类生成视觉模型中提供了帕累托最优性能。该模型通过蒸馏一个 270 亿参数的教师模型生成，并在多个基准测试中超越了其他参数量小于 1B 的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决实时流式视频字幕生成的问题，特别是在资源受限的环境下，以提供高效且性能优秀的解决方案。

Method: YoloMT 通过从一个大的模型（270 亿参数）中蒸馏产生训练得到，利用软参数转移（SFT）和自适应重参数化策略（GRPO）来优化参数。在训练过程中，该模型对每个帧只进行一次编码，并将其编码为 16 个令牌，从而可以缓存并重复利用已编码的帧，提高生成效率。

Result: YoloMT 在多个基线测试中表现出色，特别是在参数量小于 1B 的模型中，它的性能超过了其他模型，尤其是在 OVO-Bench，MVBench，StreamingBench 和 Video-MME 基准测试中。

Conclusion: YoloMT 是一个高效的实时流式字幕生成模型，它通过合理的模型设计和技术手段，实现了高效率和高性能之间的良好平衡。

Abstract: We introduce a new model for real-time streaming captioning on a single GPU. YoloMT is a 449.8M-parameter streaming captioner that provides Pareto-frontier performance among generative vision models in its class. Distilled from a 27B teacher by SFT and GRPO, it leads every model under 1B parameters on OVO-Bench, MVBench, StreamingBench and Video-MME, answered from its captions, and beats LFM2.5-VL-1.6B and SmolVLM2-2.2B. YoloMT is designed to read more frames cheaply and make them informative, encoding each frame once and caching it as 16 tokens. This allows the model to incorporate and infer from past frames without additional vision passes, making it efficient and effective. We also provide a detailed recipe for training YoloMT, with its stages and data, as well as ablations that demonstrate the contribution of each component.

</details>


### [206] [DuCo: Exploiting Dual Cross-Layer Consistency for Sparse Geometric Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34608)
*Liangyu Xiang,Junyu Gao,Xiaofeng Wang,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DuCo 是一种无需训练的稀疏注意力框架，通过利用历史注意力信息和值的冗余性，实现对长图像序列的高效几何推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于稠密全局注意力的计算复杂度，难以在长时间序列图像上进行高效推理。现有稀疏注意力方法不充分利用跨层信息共享和值冗余。因此需要一个方法来解决这一问题。

Method: DuCo 方法利用了跨层注意力分布一致性及值冗余结构的局部变化，通过历史注意力信息估计当前层注意力概率，并通过对层间局部更新的聚类来利用冗余性，最终通过概率加权的价值失真优化稀疏掩码构建。

Result: 实验结果显示，DuCo 框架在三个视觉几何基础模型和五个基准数据集上获得了良好的准确率-效率权衡。

Conclusion: DuCo 通过跨层一致性分析和值冗余结构聚类，实现无需训练的高速高效稀疏注意力机制在视觉几何任务中的应用。

Abstract: Dense global attention is crucial for capturing cross-view geometry in visual geometry foundation models, yet its quadratic complexity limits efficient inference on long image sequences. Existing sparse attention methods primarily select entries of the global attention matrix based on attention probabilities and construct masks independently across layers, overlooking value redundancy and cross-layer information reuse. By analyzing attention distributions and clustering value representations across layers, we reveal two forms of \emph{cross-layer consistency}, characterized by highly similar attention probability distributions and localized changes in value redundancy structures. Building on these observations, we propose \textbf{DuCo}, a training-free sparse attention framework that exploits historical attention information and value redundancy for efficient geometric inference. Specifically, DuCo leverages historical attention information to estimate current-layer dense attention probabilities and introduces value clustering to exploit redundancy, with clusters efficiently refined through local updates across layers. Both factors are integrated through probability-weighted value distortion to jointly optimize four cluster representation states under an attention budget for sparse mask construction. Experiments across three visual geometry foundation models and five benchmarks demonstrate favorable accuracy--efficiency trade-offs.

</details>


### [207] [Commit2Drive: Perception-Grounded Language Decisions as Commitments for Trajectory Planning in Vision-Language-Action Driving Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34614)
*Zikai Zhang,Hubert P. Shum,Toby Breckon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Commit2Drive 提出了一种新的框架，通过将语言决策作为轨迹规划的约束，确保决策和轨迹的一致性。它使用一个轻量级的语言模型，并在单个消费级图形卡上实现了在线闭环推断，提高了驾驶分数和成功率，并解决了语言动作不一致的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法中存在的语言决策与轨迹不一致的问题，在闭环驾驶中是一个主要挑战。为了解决这个问题，提出Commit2Drive框架。

Method: 该方法使用语言决策作为轨迹规划的约束，实现决策和轨迹的一致性。具体做法是通过一个Maneuver Bank将决策映射到一个带有动态含义的代码，并通过一致性损失训练规划器执行场景中的每一个可行决策，使轨迹跟随决策。此外，自动数据管道提供了监督，使用专家未来运动进行决策标注，通过策略性执行和基于规则的函数进行语言注释的生成、审核和改进。

Result: Commit2Drive在Bench2Drive上的驾驶得分为78.50，成功率55.91%，使用了轻量级的0.5B语言模型，在单个消费级图形卡上实现了在线闭环推理，与当前最先进的技术相当，并显著减少了语言-动作的不一致。

Conclusion: Commit2Drive证明了将语言决策作为规划约束的可行性，并为智能驾驶系统提供了新的研究方向，尽管其灵活性和准确性需要进一步提升。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models introduce language reasoning into end-to-end autonomous driving. They output a language decision together with a trajectory, which makes the planner more interpretable. However, in existing methods the language decision is either an independent output or an index that selects among similar candidate trajectories, and its annotations are not verified against the trajectory. As a result, the language decision and the trajectory are often inconsistent in closed-loop driving. To address this, we propose Commit2Drive, a framework that imposes the language decision as a consistency constraint on trajectory planning. The decision is a commitment, a maneuver class with a continuous parameter that one deterministic rule reads from any trajectory, and it is grounded in facts reported by the perception of the model itself. A Maneuver Bank maps the commitment to a code with a kinematic meaning, and consistency losses train the planner to execute every admissible decision of a scene, so that the trajectory follows the decision. An automatic data pipeline supplies the supervision. Decisions are labeled from the future motion of the expert, counterfactual executions train the decision and the trajectory together, and rule-based functions with a Vision-Language Model (VLM) generate, review and refine the language annotations. The code is a lookup that involves no hidden state of the language model, so Commit2Drive is extensible to rule-based decisions, which the same planner executes without retraining. Commit2Drive uses a 0.5B language model and runs closed-loop inference online on a single consumer graphics card. On Bench2Drive, Commit2Drive reaches a Driving Score (DS) of 78.50 and a Success Rate (SR) of 55.91% with a lightweight model, which is competitive with the state of the art, and greatly reduces the language-action inconsistency. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [208] [Beyond Pose Control: Anatomically Consistent Ultrasound Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34622)
*Yue Zhou,Fangxu Han,Tony Danjun Wang,Nassir Navab,Yuan Bi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合解剖先验知识和3D潜在记忆的超声成像生成方法，以解决探头姿态控制下的生成器在未扫描区域生成不合理的解剖结构和重复访问时解剖结构不一致的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于姿态控制的超声成像生成方法在未扫描区域生成不合理的解剖结构和重复访问时解剖结构不一致的问题。

Method: 该方法结合了解剖先验知识，用于指导生成未扫描区域的解剖结构，并通过在线优化过程进一步细化。此外，还提出了一个针对超声成像的3D潜在记忆，该记忆将生成的断层图像复合成患者的体内的体积，并在新的姿态下进行重新切片，以保持重复访问的解剖结构一致性。细粒度的潜在记忆输入生成器，而粗粒度的解剖先验知识指导跨变压器块的布局。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在甲状腺、肾脏和腰椎上的视觉保真度、姿态跟踪和解剖一致性方面优于姿态条件和内存增强的基准方法。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的方法能够显著改进基于姿态的超声成像生成的视觉质量、姿态跟踪和解剖一致性。

Abstract: Camera-controlled video generation anticipates how a scene appears from new viewpoints by relying on a prior learned implicitly from large-scale data. Ultrasound poses the same problem, with the probe pose as the viewpoint, but under opposite conditions: anatomy offers an explicit prior that every sonographer relies on, whereas tracked scans are too scarce to learn it implicitly. As a result, pose control alone is not enough, and pose-conditioned ultrasound generators produce implausible anatomy in unscanned regions and inconsistent anatomy on revisits. We go beyond pose control and give the generator the two kinds of knowledge that make up a sonographer's mental model. An anatomical prior, built from a population of subjects, guides generation where nothing has been scanned, and is refined online by every generated chunk towards the scanned subject. A 3D latent memory, designed around the cross-sectional physics of ultrasound, compounds the generated slices into a volume of the patient and reslices it at new poses, keeping revisited anatomy consistent. The fine-grained memory enters the generator at the input, while the coarse anatomy prior guides the layout across transformer blocks. Experiments on thyroid, kidney, and lumbar spine show improved visual fidelity, pose following, and anatomical consistency over pose-conditioned and memory-augmented baselines.

</details>


### [209] [PRAM: Activating Latent Multimodal Knowledge through Partwise Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34640)
*Haidong Chen,Ying Guo,Shangze Li,Zhongkuan Mao,Linrui Xu,Canran Xiao,Fei Shen,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨模态分类和检索方法，称为部分推理跨模态（PRAM），通过共享的中间模态融合部分统计关系，提高了局部可区分信息和部分统计数据间的关联性。PRAM在10个数据集上的实验结果表明，其在16个分类设置和19个检索设置中达到了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的跨模态方法往往关注样本级对齐而忽视了局部区分信息和部分统计数据的关系，本文通过引入部分推理跨模态（PRAM）来解决该问题。

Method: PRAM方法包括多粒度协方差正则化模块、基于轴的光谱关系推理模块和多粒度评分融合模块。多粒度协方差正则化模块从模态-轴对中提取全局和局部向量的协方差，并通过自适应混合稳定局部估计。基于轴的光谱关系推理模块通过正则化协方差组成推断目标模态之间的全局和部分统计关系，并通过光谱分解提取投影方向和相关性。多粒度评分融合模块通过标准化和融合来自全局和局部关系的匹配分数，增强了任务特定的信息以及类别语义。

Result: 在10个数据集上的广泛实验表明，PRAM在16个分类设置和19个检索设置中达到了最先进的性能，同时在三个传感场景中的表现也非常竞争力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的PRAM方法在多种跨模态分类和检索任务中展现了优越的性能，为进一步研究提供了新的思路和方法。

Abstract: Emergent cross-modal classification and retrieval enable transfer between modalities without direct paired supervision. However, existing methods often prioritize sample-level alignment, overlooking local discriminative information and partwise statistical relations. To this end, we propose partwise reasoning across modalities (PRAM), which activates latent multimodal knowledge in frozen encoders by composing partwise statistical relations through a shared pivot modality. Specifically, we first present a multi-granularity covariance regularization module that estimates covariances from global and local vectors in two collections of modality-pivot pairs, stabilizing local estimates through adaptive blending with their cross-part means. Second, we devise a pivot-based spectral relational reasoning module that infers global and partwise statistical relations between target modalities through regularized covariance composition and extracts paired projection directions and their correlation strengths via whitening and spectral decomposition. Finally, we introduce a multi-granularity score fusion module that standardizes and fuses matching scores from global and local relations, incorporating task-specific information on pivot associations and class semantics for class prediction and candidate ranking. Extensive experiments across ten datasets demonstrate that PRAM achieves state-of-the-art performance in 16 classification settings and 19 retrieval settings, while exhibiting highly competitive performance in three sensing scenarios.

</details>


### [210] [A Hard Background Pairing Transfers across Classifiers and Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34645)
*Karl Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究将ImageNet上的40种图像分类器分为两组，一组选择了自身的最差配对，另一组则使用随机配对进行比较。结果显示，多数分类器在新背景图像的综合测试中的准确率下降了6.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了验证图像复合基准（composite benchmarks）作为严格测试的可靠性，研究者对比了按最难配对划分的图像分类器与其他随机配对的性能。

Method: 研究人员将40种基于ImageNet的图像分类器分为两组，一组选择了自己的最差配对，另一组通过其他随机配对进行比较。

Result: 对于不受到自身最难配对影响的一组分类器，其在新背景图像的综合测试中准确率对比随机配对下降了6.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 使用这些复合基准，可以比较这个池的分类器在固定和随机配对上的表现。

Abstract: An image classifier names the object in an image, and its accuracy is the share of images named correctly. A classifier that names a car on a road may miss the same car pasted onto clouds. Composite benchmarks measure such failures by pasting objects cut from photographs onto backgrounds from other photographs. A pairing puts every object label on a different background label, such as truck on sea. The pairing on which some classifiers do worst, their hardest pairing, makes a strict test, but may be hard only for them or only on the photographs used to choose it. We show that the hardest pairing of some classifiers stays hard for others and on new photographs. We split 40 ImageNet classifiers into halves balanced by reported ImageNet accuracy. One half picks its hardest pairing of 8 object and 8 background labels from Common Objects in Context (COCO). The other half, held out of the choice, compares that pairing with random pairings of the same objects and backgrounds. For the 14 held-out classifiers with little net accuracy change after pasting objects back onto their own backgrounds, mean accuracy on composites from new photographs falls from 50.3% over random pairings to 43.7% on the hardest pairing, 6.6 percentage points lower. A benchmark using these composites can compare this pool's held-out classifiers on the fixed and random pairings.

</details>


### [211] [Atunda: Building an Afrogenic Dance Movement Dataset for AI-Driven Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34649)
*Diego Carrascosa,Tristan Pank,Qudus Onikeku,Mas'ud Onikeku,Jeremy A Frusco,Osubi Craig,Angelos Barmpoutis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Atunda，这是首个系统收集的阿非罗基因舞蹈动作视频数据集，用于训练和评估运动分类的深度学习模型。使用该数据集训练和评估不同配置的时间卷积神经网络，分类准确率超过95%，构建了该领域的一项基准，并为未来研究奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于非洲和非洲裔美洲舞蹈文化的代表性不足，该研究旨在通过创建首个AI准备的阿非罗基因舞蹈动作数据集，填补这一领域的空白，为相关研究提供基础。

Method: 研究使用时间卷积神经网络来训练和评估不同配置，评估结果表明分类准确率超过95%，提供了运动分类的基准。

Result: 研究中使用的数据集涵盖了400多个视频和13种独特的舞蹈动作，实现了95%以上的分类准确率，这一结果为未来研究提供了一个基准。

Conclusion: 该研究不仅展示了阿非罗基因舞蹈动作数据集的实用性，还强调了自动化技术在保护和促进该文化形式的重要性。

Abstract: This paper introduces Atunda, the first systematically collected video dataset of Afrogenic dance moves, designed for training and evaluating deep learning models for movement classification. The first phase of the dataset includes nearly 400 videos covering 13 distinct dance moves from African and African Diaspora performative culture, making it the first AI-ready resource of its kind. Using this dataset, we train and evaluate various configurations of temporal convolutional neural networks, achieving a classification accuracy exceeding 95%. The baseline results provide a benchmark for future studies in movement classification, especially in the understudied area of Afrogenic performative culture and tradition. This work lays the foundation for developing in the future digital metrics of similarity, automated attribution systems, and tools that preserve and promote the cultural heritage of Afrogenic dances.

</details>


### [212] [Counting Beyond Frames: Codec Motion Tells Frozen Diffusion VLMs How Many](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34657)
*Zhuoqin Li,Jiayu Ding,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为CoDiCount的无需训练的统一框架，该框架利用视频中的运动来估计对象的重复次数，特别是在冻结的扩散型视觉语言模型（dVLM）去噪过程中实现。实验结果表明，这种方法在多种任务和模型中表现出了广泛的一致性改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于视频中的重复次数问题同时具有简单性与复杂性，现有的视频语言模型难以准确解决这一问题，尤其是在帧之间的运动分析方面。研究提出直接从视频帧的运动编码中推断重复次数，利用这种运动信息作为模型的输入，以提高计数准确性。

Method: CoDiCount框架首先将候选计数转换为可测试的运动假设，通过逐步优化模型的概率分布来衡量支持这些假设的程度。该方法在固定的扩散型视觉语言模型上实现，通过结构化的计数信息去噪来重新加权模型的内部概率。

Result: CoDiCount在MotionBench、TOMATO和SVCBench上取得了良好的性能，表现出了一致的提升。尤其是7B参数的dVLM在MotionBench上超越了GPT-5.1和Gemini-2.5-Pro，在TOMATO上超越了GPT-4o和更大型的开放模型，同时在SVCBench上与GPT-5.4相媲美。

Conclusion: CoDiCount方法利用了视频中的运动信息来解决计数问题，展示了在不同模型和数据集上的广泛适用性和有效性，特别是在冻结的扩散型视觉语言模型中实现了显著的性能改进。

Abstract: Counting how many times something happens is among the simplest questions about a video and among the hardest for video-language models, autoregressive and diffusion alike. The difficulty lies between the frames: a model samples only a few of them, while each repetition unfolds as movement in between. Video codecs already record this movement densely as block displacements, and codec-based video models use it to shape their visual input. We are the first to show that this motion provides direct evidence for the count: a candidate count is a testable motion hypothesis, and unwinding the cycles it implies measures how strongly the observed directions support it. We present CoDiCount, a unified training-free framework that turns this test into a count potential and applies it while a frozen diffusion vision-language model (dVLM) denoises its answer. At every denoising step, structured count messages map each option letter, or each grammar-valid edit of an open integer, to the count it expresses and reweight the model's own probabilities within that set. Consistent gains over the official decoders on MotionBench, TOMATO, and SVCBench demonstrate the broad generalization of CoDiCount across diffusion models, datasets, and diverse counting tasks. With CoDiCount, a 7B dVLM outperforms GPT-5.1 and Gemini-2.5-Pro on MotionBench, surpasses GPT-4o and much larger open models on TOMATO, and matches GPT-5.4 on SVCBench.

</details>


### [213] [ShowNow: Fine-Grained Speculative Streaming for Responsive World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34693)
*Yiren Song,Guian Fang,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了ShowNow框架，通过在播放期间利用空闲计算资源提前准备用户操作的前提结果，实现细粒度承诺与预先准备的结合，从而降低输入到显示的延迟，提高响应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界模型视频生成技术在实时吞吐量方面存在缺陷，无法及时响应用户的输入，ShowNow框架旨在解决这一问题，通过在播放时预处理未来操作的结果来减少输入延迟。

Method: ShowNow框架采用了细粒度承诺策略和可配置的预处理机制。通过构建多未来数据集训练模型，并使用共享历史树执行与融合承诺提高预处理效率。

Result: 在单个H100 GPU上，ShowNow模型可以以每帧16fps的速度生成480p的视频，并准备8个未来的操作。在ViZDoom环境中，完整部署的ShowNow系统将输入到显示的延迟从620ms减少到208ms，同时在使用匹配策略的实验中，减少了空闲时间并提高了动作变化响应的及时性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，细粒度承诺和预准备在视觉保真度和长视距一致性之间存在权衡，ShowNow框架利用播放时的计算资源实现了对用户输入的快速响应，提升视听交互体验。

Abstract: Interactive world models must generate video continuously while responding promptly to user actions. Real-time throughput alone leaves input latency unresolved: coarse generation blocks delay action updates, and generation after input adds work to the response path. We introduce ShowNow, a fine-grained speculative streaming framework that uses computation available during playback to prepare action-conditioned futures before input arrives. Single-latent commitment enables frequent decisions, while configurable preparation covers one to eight actions. We construct a matched multi-future dataset to train alternative continuations from a common state; shared-history tree execution and fused commitment make their preparation efficient. On a single H100, our 5B model sustains 480p generation at 16 fps with eight prepared futures. In ViZDoom, the complete ShowNow deployment reduces median input-to-display latency from 620 to 208 ms relative to the native-block SolarWM-FT deployment. Matched-policy experiments reduce restart empty ticks from 1.56% to 0.09% (94%) and improve on-time responses to action changes. These results establish complementary roles for fine-grained commitment and advance preparation, with measured trade-offs in visual fidelity and long-horizon consistency. ShowNow turns playback-time computation into responsive visual interaction. Project: https://github.com/showlab/ShowNow.

</details>


### [214] [Seeing the Right Context: Grounded On-Policy Distillation for Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34700)
*Yichang Luo,Hourun Zhu,Shixiong Liu,Kan Wei,Shi Liang,Yidan Zhang,Lei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV
