Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了基于大规模语言模型构建知识库的问题，并提出了一种名为Knowledge-Base Gradient Descent的方法来构建可编辑的知识结构，通过随机化编辑候选集，评估编辑效果并采用改进的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于知识库的结构决定了应用如何检索和连接所需信息以回答问题，因此需要研究如何基于大规模语言模型有效地构建知识库。

Method: 文章提出了一种名为Knowledge-Base Gradient Descent的方法。该方法通过比较预期答案和当前知识库回答之间的差异，诊断知识库中的不一致性，并在每次迭代中提出结构编辑。编辑被接受的标准是当学习损失降低时。模型会根据随机化编辑候选集来调整编辑操作的概率，这种方法也可以被视为一种候选联合编辑，因为它们的变化相互作用。

Result: 在大型文档（每份内容是一组研究论文）上的实验表明，使用这种方法更新的知识卡片和图模式在抽样生成的问题上获得了更高的Answer F1得分，且每个文档都有结构改进。对于人类提出的问题，图模式的大规模F1得分也有所提高，而卡片的大规模F1得分则有所下降。这意味着更新后的结构更适合它们所学的那种问题。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过结构化编辑过程，可以显著提高基于大规模语言模型的知识库的质量和适用性。

Abstract: We consider the problem of constructing knowledge bases for applications based on large language models, in which the structure of a knowledge base determines how an application can retrieve and connect the information needed to answer a question. We study Self-Structuring Learning, a setting in which questions and expected answers guide the construction of a persistent knowledge base whose editable structure can be a graph schema or the organization of linked knowledge documents. We propose Knowledge-Base Gradient Descent, which diagnoses discrepancies between expected answers and responses obtained through the current knowledge base. At each round, the method proposes a structural edit, rebuilds the knowledge base, and adopts the edit when the learning loss decreases. The retained structure is evaluated on other questions about the same sources. By randomizing over candidate edits, we express the loss change of an edit as a derivative with respect to the probability of selecting it, and we evaluate joint edits as single candidates because their changes interact. Schema Descent instantiates the method for graph schemas. In experiments on large documents, each a bundle of research papers, the updated knowledge cards and graph schemas obtain higher mean Answer F1 on held-out generated questions than their initial structures, with schema gains in every document. On human questions, mean F1 rises for schemas and falls for cards: the updated structures serve the kind of question they were learned from.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [395] [NEVA: Natural-Language Co-Evolution and Validation of Microarchitectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33346)
*Khoi Le,Sayanti Jana,Matthew DeLorenzo,Jeyavijayan Rajendran,Paul Gratz*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为NEVA的方法，通过提取、整合和适应现有研究中的架构概念，以自然语言形式生成新的微架构策略，优化单核和多核配置下的性能，并且通过与直接进化代码的对比实验显示了NEVA的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的微架构设计自动化方法多基于C++代码的直接进化，需要LLM推理通过具体实现细节而非直接作用于实际的架构机制、硬件状态和决策规则上。NEVA旨在解决这一问题。

Method: NEVA 方法从已发表的研究成果中提取核心架构概念，构建结构化的知识库，并以此作为指导来生成具有自然语言描述的联合架构规范。

Result: NEVA 在单核和多核配置下的SPEC CPU2017/2026、Google、GAP 和 XSBench 负载下展示了性能提升。在 Claude Opus 5.5 上，与 Pythia-Mockingjay 基线相比，NEVA 的单核性能改善了 4.8%。尽管 EvoX 设计在全面和受限内存带宽情况下分别获得了 3.4% 和 1.5% 的改进，但其多核性能与基线相当。EvoX 搜索评估了 40 个候选方案，耗时约2.2 天，使用了大约 $320 的LLM费用。

Conclusion: NEVA 通过引入自然语言表述的架构规范，有效提高了微架构设计的性能，展示了在特定配置和负载下与直接进化的 C++ 代码相比的优势。

Abstract: Recent large language model (LLM)-guided evolutionary approaches have demonstrated the potential to automate microarchitectural design exploration. However, existing approaches primarily evolve C++ simulator code rather than reasoning directly about the microarchitectural designs it represents. As C++ code embodies hardware designs with several layers of abstraction, evolving it directly forces LLMs to reason through implementation details rather than the actual architectural mechanisms, hardware state, and decision rules that define a microarchitectural design. To this end, we propose NEVA, an LLM-guided methodology that generates new microarchitectural policies by extracting, integrating and adapting architectural concepts from published research. NEVA extracts the underlying concepts from prior works into a structured knowledge library, which then guides the evolution of joint natural-language architectural specifications. This approach addresses three key challenges: architectural representation in natural language, validation of candidate designs under hardware constraints, and the coordination of microarchitecture components using diagnostic feedback through co-evolution. We evaluate NEVA across single-core and multicore configurations using SPEC CPU2017/2026, Google, GAP, and XSBench workloads, and compare specification-level evolution against code-level evolution across different search strategies. Under Claude Opus 5.5, NEVA discovers designs achieving up to 4.8% higher held-out single-core performance than the Pythia-Mockingjay baseline. A selected EvoX design achieves improvements of 3.4% and 1.5% under full and restricted memory bandwidth, respectively, although its multicore performance does not differ significantly from the baseline. The EvoX search evaluates 40 candidates over 2.2 days, with approximately $320 in LLM usage at API list prices.

</details>


### [396] [WADJET: Whole-Job Authorization and Dispatch for Job Execution Trust on FPGA System-on-Chip Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33406)
*Mohamed El-Hadedy*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: WADJET是一种在FPGA系统上实现的硬件机制，通过在FPGA逻辑中的单一逻辑门验证整个作业，以防止恶意或受损的软件篡改数据或重新提交作业。它利用Ascon-AEAD128进行加密和认证每个已批准的作业，具有比ShEF更低的查找表需求和增加的工作启动时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的保护措施可能不足以防止恶意或受损的软件篡改加速器寄存器或重新提交作业。WADJET旨在通过在FPGA逻辑中集成验证机制来确保作业的完整性和安全性。

Method: WADJET通过在FPGA中设计一个逻辑门，该门负责验证整个作业的完整性和安全性。具体的实现方法包括使用Ascon-AEAD128对作业进行加密和认证，并验证接收到的作业是否为新序列号。验证通过后，WADJET会直接将数据写入未修改的加速器寄存器。

Result: WADJET在Zynq-7020上验证了所有21,000个已批准的作业，但拒绝了14,700个修改或重复的作业。这一机制相比ShEF具有更低的查找表需求和少量的启动时间增加。

Conclusion: WADJET展示了在FPGA硬件层面上实现加密和验证功能的可行性，并展示了其在保护系统安全方面的有效性。

Abstract: On an FPGA system-on-chip, the software that submits work to an accelerator also decides what the accelerator does. It writes the registers one by one, and the hardware runs whatever it finds, so faulty or compromised software can change one address or resend a job. Existing protections decide who may reach an accelerator or keep its data safe. Deciding whether one job was approved and is still unused takes three things in one place: the only way to the accelerator, the whole job held back until checked, and a record of used approvals. WADJET puts the three in one gate in FPGA logic. A separate authority encrypts and authenticates each approved job, with a sequence number, using Ascon-AEAD128. The gate verifies the whole job before releasing any part, accepts only a newer sequence number, uses it up, and writes the registers of an unmodified accelerator itself. The gate replaces only the processor's direct line to the registers and works beside the existing protections. In a software model, a check on each write ran jobs mixed from two approved ones; the whole-job rule released none. Its release path is proven on the Verilog for all inputs. On a Zynq-7020 running a language model, it released all 21,000 approved jobs and none of 14,700 altered or repeated ones. It needs a third of the lookup tables of ShEF's register protection, which protects more, and adds about ten microseconds to starting a job. It assumes trusted control of its key, reset, and configuration.

</details>


### [397] [From Entropy Source to Stochastic Bit-Stream: Low-Cost Converters for True-Random Stochastic Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33686)
*Mehran Moghadam,Abu Masum,M. Hassan Najafi,Sercan Aygun,Di Wu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种将真随机数生成器（TRNG）的未偏置输出转换为具有任意目标概率的随机位流（SBS）的方法，这些方法与标准CMOS逻辑兼容，并提供了与其他随机基线相比具有优势的面积、能耗及面积延迟折衷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前常用的伪随机或准随机序列会使得系统可预测、周期性和流间相关，而本文提出的方法旨在利用真随机源生成器，克服这些局限，以提升安全性和准确性。

Method: 提出了三种将TRNG未偏置输出转换为SBS的方法，分别是块提取和比较电路、计数器逻辑电路和级联多数逻辑流水线。这些电路皆采用标准CMOS逻辑实现。

Result: 所有方法都达到了软件随机生成器的准确性，并支持标准的SC运算，误差随流长呈线性下降。硬件综合结果表明，这些转换器在面积、能耗和面积延迟折衷方面优于伪随机和准随机基准。注入软错误下，块提取方法表现优于最好的确定性基准。

Conclusion: 该研究为在标准CMOS上实现真正随机的SC提供了实际可行方案，有助于提升系统的安全性与准确性。

Abstract: Stochastic computing (SC) encodes values as the probability of ones in random bit-streams, which allows arithmetic circuits with very low area and power and a natural tolerance to faults. The accuracy and security of an SC system depend on the quality of those bit-streams. Conventional generators build them from pseudo-random or quasi-random sequences, which are predictable, periodic, and correlated across streams drawn from the same generator. This work proposes three methods for converting the unbiased outputs of a true random number generator (TRNG) into stochastic bit-streams (SBS) with arbitrary target probabilities: a block extraction and comparison circuit, a counter-logic circuit, and a cascaded majority-logic pipeline. All three accept bits from any true- random source and are built from standard CMOS logic. They reach the accuracy of a software random generator for both independent and correlated stream generation, and they support the common SC arithmetic operations with error that falls with stream length at the same rate. Hardware synthesis results show that the converters offer favorable area, energy, and area-delay trade-offs relative to pseudo- and quasi-random baselines. Under injected soft errors, block extraction degrades no faster than the best deterministic baseline. By bridging raw entropy sources and application-ready bit-streams, this work makes true-random SC practical on standard CMOS.

</details>


### [398] [L2C2: Enhancing Large-scale Chiplets for Agent Long-Context LLM Inference with Sparse and Distributed Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33691)
*Kangbo Bai,Zhonghua Mu,Hongze Zhou,Tianyu Jia*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为L2C2的系统优化方法，用于分布式稀疏注意力机制在长上下文推理中的应用。通过在部分预填充阶段提出一种名为Split-Comm Attention的新机制，并在解码阶段采用静态交错的token分配和动态敏捷缓存均衡，相比传统方法，L2C2能够在大规模chiplet上分别实现1.45$	imes$和1.53$	imes$的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型（LLMs）在代理应用中的需求增加，长上下文推理成为重要需求，为了降低计算量并提升硬件性能，稀疏注意力（SpAt）和chiplet技术被提出。然而，将SpAt部署到分布式chiplet中需要复杂的分布式注意机制。

Method: 提出L2C2系统优化方法。该方法在部分预填充阶段引入了一种名为Split-Comm Attention的机制，利用chiplet的高带宽互联以减少通信开销来降低内存访问。在解码阶段，通过静态交错的token分配和动态敏捷缓存均衡来解决内存访问不平衡问题。

Result: 通过L2C2优化，在大规模chiplet上的部分预填充和解码阶段分别实现了1.45$	imes$和1.53$	imes$的加速。

Conclusion: L2C2系统优化方法为分布式稀疏注意力机制在大规模chiplet上的应用提供了有效的解决方案，极大地提升了处理效率。

Abstract: Long-context inference is a rising demand for LLMs, especially for agent applications. Sparse attention (SpAt) and chiplet technology are promising solutions to reduce computations and scale hardware performance. However, deploying SpAt on distributed chiplets necessitates context parallelism for workload partitioning, introducing complex distributed attention. In this work, we present L2C2, a system optimization for distributed SpAt in long-context inference. During the partial-prefill stage, we propose a novel Split-Comm Attention mechanism that leverages the high-bandwidth interconnect of chiplets to trade communication overhead for reduced memory access. We combine it with conventional Ring Attention to form a Bi-Mode method that adapts to varying input sequence lengths. During the decoding stage, we analyze the cause of memory access imbalance and address it by static interleaved token assignment. Dynamic agile cache balancing is also proposed to adapt to SpAt skewness. Evaluations show our optimization can achieve 1.45$\times$ and 1.53$\times$ speedup in partial-prefill and decoding with SpAt on large-scale chiplets.

</details>


### [399] [GPS: Guiding Phase-Level Prefetcher Selection with Data-Driven Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33859)
*Buqing Xu,Mengming Li,Qijun Zhang,Ziyan Guo,Zhiyao Xie*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: GPS 是一种数据驱动的预测器选择框架，利用全面的调优和离线学习，通过软件生成复杂策略，避免在线探索，提高 IPC 存量并最小化额外硬件开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过单一特征和简单映射机制处理预测器选择，这可能会导致不准确的预测器策略，并且无法充分利用可用的硬件资源，导致性能低下。

Method: GPS 采用全面的特征集捕捉系统行为和硬件资源状态，在离线阶段使用机器学习模型学习特征与最佳 IPC 策略之间的映射，并在运行时通过预生成的策略提示进行轻量级执行。

Result: 与现有最佳方法相比，GPS 减少了 2.6% 的性能差距，SPEC CPU2006、SPEC CPU2017 和 GAP 基准测试集的几何均值 IPC 分别提高了 12.14%、8.34% 和 14.17%。GPS 只需要额外的 778 位存储来控制运行时策略。

Conclusion: GPS 框架通过改善预测器选择策略显著提高了性能，同时将存储开销保持在较低水平。

Abstract: Hardware prefetching is a widely used technique for hiding off-chip memory latency. Modern processors increasingly adopt composite prefetchers that combine multiple prefetching engines to handle the diverse memory access patterns found in real applications. However, coordinating multiple prefetchers requires a policy selection algorithm that maps observed system feedback to appropriate prefetching policies. Existing approaches suffer from two key limitations. First, relying on only a small set of features can lead to misleading prefetcher policies due to incomplete visibility into the system state. Second, limited on-chip hardware resources force these designs to use simple mapping mechanisms that cannot effectively navigate the large prefetching policy space.
  To address these challenges, we propose GPS, a data-driven framework for prefetcher selection that leverages comprehensive profiling and offline learning. GPS collects a rich set of features that capture both system behaviors and hardware resource states, and uses an offline machine learning model to learn the mapping between these features and the IPC-best policy. By offloading complex policy generation to software, GPS eliminates the need for costly online exploration and enables lightweight runtime execution through pre-generated policy hints. GPS reduces the performance gap to only 2.6%, compared to 13.6% and 14.2% in prior state-of-the-art methods. We evaluate GPS on SPEC CPU2006, SPEC CPU2017, and GAP benchmark suites. GPS outperforms the state-of-the-art prefetcher selection method I-POP by 12.14%, 8.34%, and 14.17% in geometric-mean IPC, respectively. GPS achieves these gains with only 778 bits of additional on-chip storage for runtime policy control.

</details>


### [400] [MixMoMo: Accelerating Extra--Large MoE Inference with Mixed--Precision GPU--NPU Execution on Mobile Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33981)
*Hailong Zou,Maoliang Li,Taohong Han,Haoze Chi,Zihao Zheng,Jiayu Chen,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: MixMoMo 提出了一种高效的混合精度推理运行时，用于在异构移动 SoC 上的超大型 Mixture-of-Experts 模型，通过结合离线量化影响特征和基于激活的运行时风险估计来优化专家的比特宽度分配，并采用精准结构化执行和 HVX 并行去量化来更好地利用资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着移动设备上的模型复杂度增加，如何有效压缩模型以降低存储和计算成本成为挑战。现有方法在专家量化精度分配及异构硬件资源利用方面存在局限性，MixMoMo 旨在优化这些问题。

Method: MixMoMo 结合了离线量化影响特征和基于激活的运行时风险估计，采用精准结构化执行和 HVX 并行去量化策略，以提高超大型 Mixture-of-Experts 模型在异构移动 SoC 上的推理速度。

Result: 在三项 Mixture-of-Experts 模型和五项基准测试中，MixMoMo 实现了平均 3.72 倍的速度提升，相比均匀的 Q4_0 量化提升了 3.72 倍，与最强的混合精度 Mixture-of-Experts 基线相比提升了 1.53 倍。

Conclusion: MixMoMo 通过精细化的量化精度管理及高效利用异构硬件资源，显著提升了超大型 Mixture-of-Experts 模型在移动设备上的推理性能。

Abstract: Mobile agents and on-device intelligence increasingly demand more capable models on smartphones. Through sparse expert activation, Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) scales model capacity, yet growing numbers of fine-grained experts complicate loading and computation. Model compression complements expert offloading by reducing storage and computation costs. Among compression techniques, mixed-precision quantization assigns lower expert bitwidths to balance model quality and inference efficiency. However, existing offline metrics and routing scores fail to reflect expert quantization sensitivity, undermining precision assignment. Diverse bitwidths also complicate balanced expert allocation across GPUs and NPUs with distinct execution characteristics, while tile-wise dequantization underutilizes NPU vector parallelism. We present MixMoMo, an efficient mixed-precision inference runtime for extra-large MoE models on heterogeneous mobile SoCs. MixMoMo combines offline quantization-impact profiles with activation-based runtime risk estimates for quality-constrained expert bitwidth assignment. It further adopts precision-structured execution for balanced GPU-NPU expert allocation and HVX-parallel dequantization for efficient NPU vector utilization. Across three MoE models and five benchmarks on two mobile platforms, MixMoMo achieves average decoding speedups of 3.72x over uniform Q4_0 and 1.53x over the strongest mixed-precision MoE baseline.

</details>


### [401] [A Differential Computing Accelerator for LLM Inference Leveraging Bit-Slice Transition Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34630)
*Yifan Wang,Zetao Guo,You Xiao,Wendi Sun,Song Chen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ISTS算法-硬件协同设计加速器通过利用比特切片向量（BSVs）在汉明空间中的聚集特性，减少计算量并实现平均3.09倍的速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有比特切片设计主要利用高阶切片的稀疏性，而低阶切片仍有大量未利用的重复使用机会。因此，需要一种新方法来充分利用这些低阶切片的密集性。

Method: ISTS算法通过Steiner吸收表构造（SATC）选择紧凑的Steiner点集合来吸收仅需少量双向位翻转的BSVs，并通过FlipPrune选择性地修剪翻转以进一步扩大可重复使用的BSV群体。硬件方面，设计了一个轻量级加速器来支持静态线性层权重和运行时生成的注意力K/V张量的Steiner重用。

Result: 实验结果表明，ISTS平均分别比ANT、BBS、LUT Tensor Core、Transitive Array和MCBP-BRCR快3.09倍、2.41倍、2.18倍、1.27倍和1.41倍。

Conclusion: 通过算法和硬件的协同设计，ISTS显著加速了大型语言模型中支配的矩阵乘法操作，并展示了在多个领先方案中的出色性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become foundational to modern artificial intelligence. Driven by scaling laws, their rapid growth poses increasing computational and memory challenges for deployment. To accelerate the general matrix multiplication (GEMM) operations that dominate LLM computation, bit-slice architectures decompose quantized operands along the bit dimension, exposing slice-level sparsity and redundancy. However, existing bit-slice designs primarily exploit sparsity in high-order slices, while dense low-order slices still offer substantial untapped reuse opportunities.
  We observe that bit-slice vectors (BSVs) are highly clustered in Hamming space, with many BSVs lying only a few bit flips away from a small set of representative patterns. Based on this observation, we propose ISTS, an algorithm-hardware co-designed accelerator that leverages representative BSV patterns as shared Steiner points to reuse partial sums across slices, substantially reducing computation. Specifically, the partial sum of each target BSV can be reconstructed from that of its assigned Steiner point using only a few additions or subtractions. On the algorithmic side, we propose Steiner Absorption Table Construction (SATC), which selects a compact set of Steiner points to absorb BSVs with only a few bidirectional bit flips, while FlipPrune selectively prunes flips to further expand the reusable BSV population. On the hardware side, we design a lightweight accelerator that supports Steiner reuse for static linear layer weights and runtime-generated attention K/V tensors. Comprehensive evaluations show that ISTS achieves average speedups of $3.09\times$, $2.41\times$, $2.18\times$, $1.27\times$, and $1.41\times$ over the state-of-the-art accelerators ANT, BBS, LUT Tensor Core, Transitive Array, and MCBP-BRCR, respectively.

</details>


### [402] [An Embedded Ergonomic Posture Monitoring and AI-Assisted Home Learning System on a Raspberry Pi 3B Edge Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34669)
*Truyen T. Le,Vinh Dang Ngoc,Thai Bao Huynh*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个基于树莓派3B、UVC摄像头和USB麦克风的隐私保护型嵌入式系统，用于家中学习空间。该系统利用MediaPipe Pose和OpenCV进行实时姿势监控，并通过定态有限自动机量化学习时间、执行健康休息和检测手机使用情况，同时集成多模态AI辅导代理，通过加密API接口解析语音和文本查询，生成概念提示，家长可通过轻量级的网页端获取加密的会话分析和排程设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自主学习过程中，学生常面临不良坐姿、长时间久坐以及注意力分散等问题。此外，缺乏实时教学支持也是痛点。因此，本文提出了一种隐私保护型嵌入式系统来解决这些问题，旨在提高学习效率和保障用眼健康。

Method: 系统基于树莓派3B、UVC摄像头和USB麦克风，利用MediaPipe Pose和OpenCV进行实时姿势监控；使用定态有限自动机确定学习时段、健康休息提醒并监测手机使用情况；集成多模态AI辅导代理，通过加密API接口解析语音和文本查询，提供概念性辅导。

Result: 原型机的端到端推理延迟为244-250毫秒，解析4.39度MAE的躯干角度误差，驻留集大小为320MB，峰值SoC温度为58.0度，并且脱机TTS反馈路径总延迟为1.58秒，展示了在资源受限边缘部署中的高实用性。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种创新的解决方案，通过实时监控与教育辅导相结合，旨在改善家庭学习体验，提高学习效率和保障健康。

Abstract: During independent home study sessions, students frequently sustain improper sitting postures, accumulate excessive sedentary hours, and experience productivity loss from off-task digital distractions. Furthermore, learners often lack real-time pedagogical scaffolding for demanding self-study curricula. To mitigate these ergonomic and instructional challenges, this paper presents an affordable, privacy-preserving cyber-physical embedded system for domestic study spaces. Engineered on a Raspberry Pi 3 Model B (1 GB RAM) paired with a UVC optical camera and a USB condenser microphone, the system executes on-device computer vision utilizing MediaPipe Pose and OpenCV to monitor upper-body alignment, sagittal head inclination, and coronal shoulder asymmetry in real time without persisting raw video streams. A deterministic finite state machine (FSM) quantifies active study durations, enforces health-compliant rest periods, and detects illicit smartphone usage. Simultaneously, an integrated multimodal AI tutoring agent parses voice and text queries via an encrypted API gateway, synthesizing Socratic conceptual hints rather than direct solutions. A lightweight parental web dashboard delivers encrypted session analytics and scheduling configurations. Empirical benchmarks on the physical prototype confirm an end-to-end inference latency of 244-250 ms at a 352x288 resolution (4.0-4.1 FPS), a trunk-angle estimation error of 4.39 degrees MAE over 5,760 synthetic ground-truth poses, a resident set size of 320 MB, a peak SoC temperature of 58.0 degrees Celsius under sustained load, and a 1.58 s total latency for the offline TTS feedback path, proving high practicality for resource-constrained edge deployment.

</details>
